# 2.11.4 Model

- [RT-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.15818): A field-defining vision-language-action model line that transfers web-scale vision-language knowledge to robotic control by representing robot actions as model outputs.
- [From Knowing to Doing: Learning Diverse Motor Skills through Instruction Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.09167): Trains motor-skill policies from language instructions so a robot can acquire diverse manipulation behaviors from instructional supervision.
- [Toward Generalizable Robotic Dual-Arm Flipping Manipulation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tie.2023.3288189): Studies dual-arm flipping policies that generalize bimanual manipulation across object states and coordinated arm motions.
- [Learning to Follow and Generate Instructions for Language-Capable Navigation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2023.3341828): Couples instruction following and instruction generation for navigation agents so language grounding and route description improve each other.
- [AttentionVote: A coarse-to-fine voting network of anchor-free 6D pose estimation on point cloud for robotic bin-picking application](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.rcim.2023.102671): Uses a coarse-to-fine point-cloud voting network to estimate 6D object poses for robotic bin picking without anchors.
- [A Depth Adaptive Feature Extraction and Dense Prediction Network for 6-D Pose Estimation in Robotic Grasping](https://doi.org/10.1109/tii.2023.3297653): Builds depth-adaptive feature extraction and dense prediction for 6D pose estimation in grasping scenes.
- [Robotic Grasp Detection Based on Category-Level Object Pose Estimation With Self-Supervised Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmech.2023.3287635): Uses self-supervised category-level pose estimation as the perception backbone for robotic grasp detection.
- [SEE-CSOM: Sharp-Edged and Efficient Continuous Semantic Occupancy Mapping for Mobile Robots](https://doi.org/10.1109/tie.2023.3262857): Maintains sharp-edged continuous semantic occupancy maps for mobile robots that need efficient navigation-ready scene models.
- [Multimodal Detection and Classification of Robot Manipulation Failures](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2023.3346270): Fuses multimodal robot signals to detect and classify manipulation failures during embodied execution.
- [Mobile ALOHA: Learning Bimanual Mobile Manipulation with Low-Cost Whole-Body Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.02117): Extends imitation learning to bimanual mobile manipulation by collecting low-cost whole-body teleoperation demonstrations.
- [Language-Conditioned Robotic Manipulation with Fast and Slow Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.04181): Splits language-conditioned manipulation into fast reactive control and slower deliberative reasoning for robot actions.
- [AffordanceLLM: Grounding Affordance from Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.06341): Grounds actionable object affordances from vision-language model predictions for robot manipulation.
- [ORGANA: A Robotic Assistant for Automated Chemistry Experimentation and Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.06949): Uses a robotic assistant to connect natural-language chemistry goals with automated experiment execution and characterization.
- [Robo-ABC: Affordance Generalization Beyond Categories via Semantic Correspondence for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.07487): Transfers manipulation affordances beyond object categories through semantic correspondence between seen and novel objects.
- [MultiPLY: A Multisensory Object-Centric Embodied Large Language Model in 3D World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.08577): Builds an object-centric embodied language model that combines multisensory 3D-world inputs for interaction reasoning.
- [Visual Robotic Manipulation with Depth-Aware Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.09038): Pretrains visual manipulation representations with depth-aware objectives before transferring them to robotic control.
- [TEXterity: Tactile Extrinsic deXterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.10230): Uses tactile extrinsic dexterity signals to improve contact-rich manipulation policies.
- [General Flow as Foundation Affordance for Scalable Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.11439): Treats general scene flow as a reusable affordance representation for scaling robot learning across manipulation tasks.
- [Multimodal Visual-Tactile Representation Learning through Self-Supervised Contrastive Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.12024): Learns MViTac with intra- and inter-modality contrastive losses over visual and tactile inputs, improving material classification and grasping-success prediction.
- [OK-Robot: What Really Matters in Integrating Open-Knowledge Models for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.12202): Analyzes how open vision-language and navigation models can be combined into a practical household robot manipulation stack.
- [Beyond the Contact: Discovering Comprehensive Affordance for 3D Objects from Pre-trained 2D Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.12978): Lifts affordance cues from pretrained 2D diffusion models to infer broader 3D object affordances beyond contact regions.
- [Adaptive Mobile Manipulation for Articulated Objects In the Open World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.14403): Adapts mobile-manipulation policies online for open-world articulated objects with changing part states.
- [LaMI: Large Language Models for Multi-Modal Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.15174): Combines large language models with multimodal human-robot interaction signals for robot intent understanding and response generation.
- [Reinforcement learning for versatile, dynamic, and robust bipedal locomotion control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.16889): Trains bipedal locomotion controllers with reinforcement learning for dynamic and robust whole-body movement.
- [M2CURL: Sample-Efficient Multimodal Reinforcement Learning via Self-Supervised Representation Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.17032): Improves sample efficiency in robot manipulation RL by pretraining multimodal representations with self-supervised objectives.
- [Semantic Belief Behavior Graph: Enabling Autonomous Robot Inspection in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.17191): Represents inspection tasks with semantic belief behavior graphs so robots can plan in unknown environments.
- [LeTO: Learning Constrained Visuomotor Policy With Differentiable Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.17500): Embeds differentiable trajectory optimization into visuomotor policy learning to satisfy constraints during manipulation.
- [Point Cloud Matters: Rethinking the Impact of Different Observation Spaces on Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.02500): Compares robot-learning observation spaces and shows how point-cloud inputs change policy generalization.
- [PoCo: Policy Composition from and for Heterogeneous Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.02511): Composes policies learned from heterogeneous robot data so skills can transfer across embodiments and tasks.
- [NavHint: Vision and Language Navigation Agent with a Hint Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.02559): Adds a hint generator to vision-language navigation so the policy can use intermediate language guidance during route following.
- [VLN-Video: Utilizing Driving Videos for Outdoor Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03561): Uses driving videos as scalable supervision for outdoor vision-and-language navigation policies.
- [CogCoM: A Visual Language Model with Chain-of-Manipulations Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.04236): Extends visual-language modeling with chain-of-manipulations reasoning for stepwise embodied action understanding.
- [The Essential Role of Causality in Foundation World Models for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.06665): Argues for causal structure as a core design principle in foundation world models for embodied agents.
- [Reasoning Grasping via Multimodal Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.06798): Uses a multimodal large language model to reason about grasp targets, affordances, and manipulation steps.
- [Grounding LLMs For Robot Task Planning Using Closed-loop State Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.08546): Grounds LLM task plans with closed-loop robot state feedback so plans can update during execution.
- [Investigating Premature Convergence in Co-optimization of Morphology and Control in Evolved Virtual Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.09231): Studies premature convergence when jointly evolving virtual soft-robot morphologies and controllers.
- [OpenFMNav: Towards Open-Set Zero-Shot Object Navigation via Vision-Language Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10670): Uses vision-language foundation models for open-set, zero-shot object navigation without fixed object categories.
- [3D Diffuser Actor: Policy Diffusion with 3D Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10885): Combines policy diffusion with 3D scene representations for embodied action.
- [Verifiably Following Complex Robot Instructions with Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.11498): Adds formal verification around foundation-model robot instruction following for complex multi-step commands.
- [DINOBot: Robot Manipulation via Retrieval and Alignment with Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.13181): Retrieves and aligns vision foundation model representations to drive robot manipulation policies.
- [Learning Highly Dynamic Behaviors for Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.13473): Learns quadruped control policies for highly dynamic behaviors beyond steady locomotion.
- [Enhancing Robotic Manipulation with AI Feedback from Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.14245): Uses feedback from multimodal large language models to improve robotic manipulation attempts.
- [CyberDemo: Augmenting Simulated Human Demonstration for Real-World Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.14795): Augments simulated human demonstrations to train real-world dexterous manipulation policies.
- [RoboEXP: Action-Conditioned Scene Graph via Interactive Exploration for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.15487): Builds action-conditioned scene graphs through interactive exploration to support manipulation planning.
- [NaVid: Video-based VLM Plans the Next Step for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.15852): Uses a video-based vision-language model to predict the next navigation step from embodied visual history.
- [RoboCodeX: Multimodal Code Generation for Robotic Behavior Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.16117): Generates robot behavior code from multimodal task context to synthesize executable manipulation routines.
- [Expressive Whole-Body Control for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.16796): Designs whole-body humanoid controllers that produce expressive coordinated motions.
- [PhyGrasp: Generalizing Robotic Grasping with Physics-informed Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.16836): Injects physics-informed constraints into multimodal models for more generalizable robotic grasping.
- [HOISDF: Constraining 3D Hand-Object Pose Estimation with Global Signed Distance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.17062): Uses global signed distance fields to constrain 3D hand-object pose estimation for manipulation.
- [ShapeLLM: Universal 3D Object Understanding for Embodied Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.17766): Trains a large language model interface for universal 3D object understanding in embodied interaction.
- [Diffusion Meets DAgger: Supercharging Eye-in-hand Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.17768): Combines diffusion policies with DAgger-style data aggregation for eye-in-hand imitation learning.
- [Pushing the Limits of Cross-Embodiment Learning for Manipulation and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.19432): Studies cross-embodiment transfer so manipulation and navigation policies can span different robot platforms.
- [Humanoid Locomotion as Next Token Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.19469): Frames humanoid locomotion as next-token prediction over action sequences.
- [Twisting Lids Off with Two Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.02338): Learns coordinated two-handed manipulation for twisting lids off objects.
- [MOKA: Open-World Robotic Manipulation through Mark-Based Visual Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03174): Uses mark-based visual prompting to guide foundation models for open-world robotic manipulation.
- [Behavior Generation with Latent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03181): Learns latent action representations for generating embodied behaviors.
- [Dexterous Legged Locomotion in Confined 3D Spaces with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03848): Trains reinforcement-learning controllers for dexterous legged locomotion in confined 3D spaces.
- [LeTac-MPC: Learning Model Predictive Control for Tactile-Reactive Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.04934): Combines learned model predictive control with tactile feedback for reactive grasping.
- [Efficient Data Collection for Robotic Manipulation via Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.05110): Reduces manipulation data collection by exploiting compositional generalization across objects and tasks.
- [Spatiotemporal Predictive Pre-training for Robotic Motor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.05304): Pretrains motor-control representations with spatiotemporal prediction before robotic policy learning.
- [NavCoT: Boosting LLM-Based Vision-and-Language Navigation via Learning Disentangled Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07376): Teaches LLM-based vision-language navigation to separate reasoning factors through disentangled chain-of-thought learning.
- [Learning Generalizable Feature Fields for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07563): Learns feature fields that transfer across mobile-manipulation scenes, supporting embodied perception and action in open environments.
- [CoPa: General Robotic Manipulation through Spatial Constraints of Parts with Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08248): Uses foundation models to infer part-level spatial constraints that guide general robotic manipulation.
- [ManiGaussian: Dynamic Gaussian Splatting for Multi-task Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08321): Uses dynamic Gaussian splatting for multi-task robotic manipulation.
- [DIFFTACTILE: A Physics-based Differentiable Tactile Simulator for Contact-rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08716): Provides a differentiable tactile simulator for training and analyzing contact-rich robotic manipulation.
- [3D-VLA: A 3D Vision-Language-Action Generative World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09631): Builds a generative 3D world model that connects vision, language, and action for embodied agents.
- [GaussianGrasper: 3D Language Gaussian Splatting for Open-Vocabulary Robotic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09637): Uses 3D language Gaussian splatting for open-vocabulary robotic grasping.
- [SculptDiff: Learning Robotic Clay Sculpting from Humans with Goal Conditioned Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10401): Trains a goal-conditioned diffusion policy from human demonstrations for robotic clay sculpting.
- [Can Large Language Models Solve Robot Routing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10795): Tests and adapts large language models for robot routing decisions under task and path constraints.
- [DarkGS: Learning Neural Illumination and 3D Gaussians Relighting for Robotic Exploration in the Dark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10814): Learns neural illumination and relightable 3D Gaussians to support robot exploration in dark environments.
- [GOMA: Proactive Embodied Cooperative Communication via Goal-Oriented Mental Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11075): Models goal-oriented mental alignment so embodied agents can communicate proactively during cooperation.
- [ManipVQA: Injecting Robotic Affordance and Physically Grounded Information into Multi-Modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11289): Injects robotic affordance and physically grounded information into multimodal large language models for manipulation reasoning.
- [Beyond Uncertainty: Risk-Aware Active View Acquisition for Safe Robot Navigation and 3D Scene Understanding with FisherRF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11396): Uses Fisher information over radiance-field representations to choose risk-aware active views for safe navigation and 3D understanding.
- [VIHE: Virtual In-Hand Eye Transformer for 3D Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11461): Introduces a virtual in-hand eye transformer for 3D robotic manipulation from wrist-centric observations.
- [LLM3: Large Language Model-based Task and Motion Planning with Motion Failure Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11552): Adds motion-failure reasoning to LLM-based task and motion planning for robots.
- [VITaL Pretraining: Visuo-Tactile Pretraining for Tactile and Non-Tactile Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11898): Pretrains visuo-tactile representations that transfer to both tactile and non-tactile manipulation policies.
- [EnvGen: Generating and Adapting Environments via LLMs for Training Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12014): Uses LLMs to generate and adapt training environments for embodied agents.
- [MineDreamer: Learning to Follow Instructions via Chain-of-Imagination for Simulated-World Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12037): Teaches simulated-world control agents to follow instructions through chain-of-imagination planning.
- [Sim2Real Manipulation on Unknown Objects with Tactile-based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12170): Uses tactile reinforcement learning to transfer manipulation policies from simulation to unknown real objects.
- [UniDexFPM: Universal Dexterous Functional Pre-grasp Manipulation Via Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12421): Applies diffusion policies to universal dexterous functional pre-grasp manipulation.
- [D-Cubed: Latent Diffusion Trajectory Optimisation for Dexterous Deformable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12861): Optimizes latent diffusion trajectories for dexterous manipulation of deformable objects.
- [Vid2Robot: End-to-end Video-conditioned Policy Learning with Cross-Attention Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12943): Learns robot policies end to end from video conditioning using cross-attention transformers.
- [Subgoal Diffuser: Coarse-to-fine Subgoal Generation to Guide Model Predictive Control for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13085): Generates reachability-conditioned subgoal sequences with a coarse-to-fine diffusion model to guide MPC through long-horizon articulated and deformable-object manipulation.
- [GeRM: A Generalist Robotic Model with Mixture-of-experts for Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13358): Uses a mixture-of-experts architecture as a generalist model for quadruped robot control.
- [Volumetric Environment Representation for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14158): Represents navigation scenes volumetrically so vision-language agents can reason over 3D environment structure.
- [Click to Grasp: Zero-Shot Precise Manipulation via Visual Diffusion Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14526): Uses visual diffusion descriptors to localize precise zero-shot grasp points from a user click.
- [CoNVOI: Context-aware Navigation using Vision Language Models in Outdoor and Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.15637): Applies vision-language models to context-aware navigation across outdoor and indoor environments.
- [RPMArt: Towards Robust Perception and Manipulation for Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.16023): Couples articulated-object perception with manipulation policies for robust interaction with movable parts.
- [A Robotic Skill Learning System Built Upon Diffusion Policies and Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.16730): Combines diffusion policies and foundation models into a robotic skill-learning system.
- [Leveraging Symmetry in RL-based Legged Locomotion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17320): Uses symmetry constraints to improve reinforcement-learning policies for legged locomotion.
- [RoboDuet: Learning a Cooperative Policy for Whole-Body Legged Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17367): Learns cooperative whole-body policies for legged robots that must locomote and manipulate together.
- [Hierarchical Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Graphs for Language-Grounded Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17846): Builds hierarchical open-vocabulary 3D scene graphs for language-grounded robot navigation.
- [Sailing Through Point Clouds: Safe Navigation Using Point Cloud Based Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.18206): Uses point-cloud control barrier functions to keep robot navigation safe around 3D obstacles.
- [RoboKeyGen: Robot Pose and Joint Angles Estimation via Diffusion-based 3D Keypoint Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.18259): Generates 3D robot keypoints with diffusion models to estimate poses and joint angles.
- [IVLMap: Instance-Aware Visual Language Grounding for Consumer Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19336): Grounds consumer-robot navigation commands in instance-aware visual-language maps.
- [RAIL: Robot Affordance Imagination with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19369): Uses large language models to imagine robot affordances for manipulation planning.
- [Keypoint Action Tokens Enable In-Context Imitation Learning in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19578): Represents demonstrations as keypoint action tokens to enable in-context imitation learning.
- [VLM-Social-Nav: Socially Aware Robot Navigation Through Scoring Using Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.00210): Scores candidate robot motions with vision-language models for socially aware navigation.
- [SUGAR : Pre-training 3D Visual Representations for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01491): Pretrains 3D visual representations tailored to downstream robotic perception and manipulation.
- [Lookahead Exploration with Neural Radiance Representation for Continuous Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01943): Uses neural radiance representations for lookahead exploration in continuous vision-language navigation.
- [APEX: Ambidextrous Dual-Arm Robotic Manipulation Using Collision-Free Generative Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.02284): Generates collision-free dual-arm grasp-and-place trajectories with a diffusion model for ambidextrous bimanual manipulation.
- [DELTA: Decomposed Efficient Long-Term Robot Task Planning Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03275): Decomposes long-horizon robot goals into reusable subtask plans so LLM planning can scale beyond single-step manipulation.
- [JUICER: Data-Efficient Imitation Learning for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03729): Improves assembly imitation learning by selecting informative demonstrations and reusing data more efficiently for contact-rich tasks.
- [Constrained 6-DoF Grasp Generation on Complex Shapes for Improved Dual-Arm Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.04643): Generates constrained 6-DoF grasps on complex object geometry so dual-arm policies receive feasible hand poses for coordinated manipulation.
- [Learning Prehensile Dexterity by Imitating and Emulating State-Only Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05582): Learns dexterous prehensile manipulation from state-only demonstrations by combining imitation with emulation losses.
- [Body Design and Gait Generation of Chair-Type Asymmetrical Tripedal Low-rigidity Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05932): Co-designs the body and gait generator for an asymmetrical low-rigidity tripedal robot that can move as chair-like furniture.
- [EVE: Enabling Anyone to Train Robots using Augmented Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06089): Uses augmented reality to let non-expert users demonstrate robot tasks and turn those interactions into training data.
- [Vision-Language Model-based Physical Reasoning for Robot Liquid Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06904): Adapts vision-language physical reasoning to infer liquid states and support robot perception for fluid-handling tasks.
- [One-Shot Transfer of Long-Horizon Extrinsic Manipulation Through Contact Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07468): Transfers a single long-horizon extrinsic-manipulation demonstration by retargeting contact sequences across objects and hands.
- [PhyScene: Physically Interactable 3D Scene Synthesis for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09465): Synthesizes 3D scenes with physical affordances and interaction constraints for training embodied agents rather than static scene viewers.
- [Closed-Loop Open-Vocabulary Mobile Manipulation with GPT-4V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.10220): Wraps GPT-4V in a closed-loop mobile-manipulation policy that replans from visual feedback for open-vocabulary household tasks.
- [FoundationGrasp: Generalizable Task-Oriented Grasping With Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.10399): Uses foundation-model task semantics to choose grasps that satisfy downstream object-use goals, not just stable pickup.
- [COMBO: Compositional World Models for Embodied Multi-Agent Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.10775): Introduces compositional world models for embodied multi-agent cooperation, using video dynamics to support interaction prediction.
- [OVAL-Prompt: Open-Vocabulary Affordance Localization for Robot Manipulation through LLM Affordance-Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.11000): Localizes open-vocabulary manipulation affordances by prompting LLMs to ground object parts and action-relevant regions.
- [RISE: 3D Perception Makes Real-World Robot Imitation Simple and Effective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12281): Uses 3D perception features to make real-world imitation policies more sample-efficient and less tied to camera viewpoints.
- [RoboDreamer: Learning Compositional World Models for Robot Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12377): Learns compositional world models for robot imagination.
- [Clio: Real-Time Task-Driven Open-Set 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.13696): Builds task-driven open-set 3D scene graphs in real time so robots keep only object relations relevant to the current instruction.
- [LLM-Personalize: Aligning LLM Planners with Human Preferences via Reinforced Self-Training for Housekeeping Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.14285): Aligns housekeeping robot planners to user preferences through reinforced self-training over LLM-generated plans.
- [Closed Loop Interactive Embodied Reasoning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15194): Couples interactive reasoning with manipulation feedback so the model can ask, observe, and update plans during robot execution.
- [ViViDex: Learning Vision-Based Dexterous Manipulation from Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15709): Learns dexterous manipulation policies from human videos by extracting vision-based hand-object interaction cues.
- [Leveraging Pretrained Latent Representations for Few-Shot Imitation Learning on a Dexterous Robotic Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.16483): Reuses pretrained latent representations to make few-shot imitation learning practical for dexterous robotic hands.
- [Learning Visuotactile Skills With Two Multifingered Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.16823): Trains two-hand multifinger manipulation skills from paired visual and tactile feedback for contact-rich dexterous tasks.
- [Ag2Manip: Learning Novel Manipulation Skills with Agent-Agnostic Visual and Action Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.17521): Uses agent-agnostic visual and action representations so novel manipulation skills can transfer across robot embodiments.
- [Quadruped robot traversing 3D complex environments with limited perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18225): Trains quadruped traversal policies for cluttered 3D terrain when sensing is partial or unreliable.
- [HELPER-X: A Unified Instructable Embodied Agent to Tackle Four Interactive Vision-Language Domains with Memory-Augmented Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19065): Unifies four interactive vision-language domains with an instructable agent that uses memory-augmented language models for task carryover.
- [DiffuseLoco: Real-Time Legged Locomotion Control with Diffusion from Offline Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19264): Trains offline diffusion policies for multi-skill legged locomotion with receding-horizon control and delayed inputs, enabling real-time quadruped deployment and skill transitions.
- [Learning Force Control for Legged Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.01402): Learns force-control policies that let legged robots coordinate locomotion with object interaction.
- [IntervenGen: Interventional Data Generation for Robust and Data-Efficient Robot Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.01472): Generates interventional robot demonstrations that improve imitation robustness under object and scene distribution shifts.
- [Track2Act: Predicting Point Tracks from Internet Videos enables Diverse Zero-shot Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.01527): Predicts point tracks from internet videos and converts them into zero-shot manipulation trajectories for diverse objects.
- [Plan-Seq-Learn: Language Model Guided RL for Solving Long Horizon Robotics Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.01534): Uses language-model plans to sequence reinforcement-learning subtasks for long-horizon robot problems.
- [CushSense: Soft, Stretchable, and Comfortable Tactile-Sensing Skin for Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.03155): Introduces a soft stretchable tactile skin that captures contact signals for safe physical human-robot interaction.
- [DexSkills: Skill Segmentation Using Haptic Data for Learning Autonomous Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.03476): Segments long-horizon manipulation into haptic skill units so autonomous policies can learn reusable contact phases.
- [Splat-MOVER: Multi-Stage, Open-Vocabulary Robotic Manipulation via Editable Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.04378): Uses editable Gaussian splatting as a scene representation for multi-stage open-vocabulary manipulation.
- [From LLMs to Actions: Latent Codes as Bridges in Hierarchical Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.04798): Maps LLM task descriptions into latent action codes that bridge high-level plans and low-level robot controllers.
- [Bi-VLA: Vision-Language-Action Model-Based System for Bimanual Robotic Dexterous Manipulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.06039): Builds a bimanual VLA system that grounds language and vision into coordinated dexterous actions for two robot arms.
- [Learning Reward for Robot Skills Using Large Language Models via Self-Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.07162): Derives robot-skill reward functions from LLM self-alignment rather than manually specified task rewards.
- [Consistency Policy: Accelerated Visuomotor Policies via Consistency Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.07503): Distills diffusion-style visuomotor policies into consistency policies for faster action generation during robot control.
- [SPIN: Simultaneous Perception, Interaction and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.07991): Integrates active perception, whole-body coordination, and mobile manipulation in a reactive policy instead of stitching separate navigation and arm skills.
- [Toward Automated Programming for Robotic Assembly Using ChatGPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.08216): Uses ChatGPT to decompose assembly instructions, generate robot control code, simulate execution, and debug syntax or collision errors.
- [TRANSIC: Sim-to-Real Policy Transfer by Learning from Online Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.10315): Learns residual policies from human online corrections so simulation-trained manipulation policies can close real-world gaps.
- [Octo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12213): An open-source generalist robot policy trained across robot embodiments and tasks, providing a pre-OpenVLA open-policy anchor.
- [One-Shot Imitation Learning with Invariance Matching for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13178): Learns invariant task regions and matches them between one unannotated demonstration and test scenes for one-shot manipulation.
- [Diffusion Actor-Critic with Entropy Regulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15177): Uses a diffusion model as the online RL actor and estimates policy entropy with Gaussian mixtures to balance exploration on MuJoCo and multimodal control tasks.
- [BWArea Model: Learning World Model, Inverse Dynamics, and Policy for Controllable Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17039): Decomposes controllable language generation into a language world model, inverse dynamics model, and cognitive policy inspired by Broca and Wernicke areas.
- [A Self-Correcting Vision-Language-Action Model for Fast and Slow System Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17418): Combines a fast VLA action predictor with a slow failure-reflection loop that seeks feedback and generates corrective manipulation actions.
- [Mani-GS: Gaussian Splatting Manipulation with Triangular Mesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17811): Binds 3D Gaussians to triangular meshes with shape-aware adaptation so large deformations, local edits, and soft-body simulations preserve high-fidelity rendering.
- [Safety Control of Service Robots with LLMs and Embodied Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17846): Combines embodied robotic control prompts with embodied knowledge graphs so LLM service-robot commands are checked against safety rules before execution.
- [Hierarchical World Models as Visual Whole-Body Humanoid Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18418): Trains a visual hierarchical world model in which a high-level agent commands a low-level humanoid controller, producing whole-body policies for eight simulated 56-DoF tasks.
- [Correctable Landmark Discovery via Large Models for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18721): Casts VLN as open-world sequential landmark discovery, using ChatGPT priors, CLIP landmark matching, and a learned cooccurrence scorer to improve unseen-scene navigation.
- [Tilde: Teleoperation for Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation Learning with a DeltaHand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18804): Couples the soft DeltaHand, a kinematic-twin TeleHand interface, and diffusion-policy imitation learning to collect real demonstrations for seven closed-loop dexterous tasks.
- [Dynamic Throwing with Robotic Material Handling Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19001): Trains reinforcement-learning controllers for underactuated material handlers, exploiting passive gripper joints to throw objects in simulation and on a 12-ton real platform.
- [Programmable Motion Generation for Open-Set Motion Control Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19283): Generates programmable motion for open-set motion-control tasks.
- [SAM-E: Leveraging Visual Foundation Model with Sequence Imitation for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19586): Fine-tunes SAM for embodied scene features and predicts multi-step action heatmaps in one pass for efficient long-horizon 3D manipulation.
- [Video-Language Critic: Transferable Reward Functions for Language-Conditioned Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19988): Trains a contrastive, temporally ranked video-language reward model on cross-embodiment data and uses it to score robot behavior traces for sample-efficient policy learning.
- [Vision-based Manipulation from Single Human Video with Open-World Object Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20321): Introduces ORION, which extracts object-centric manipulation plans from one RGB or RGB-D human video and conditions robot policies on those plans for novel objects and layouts.
- [NuRF: Nudging the Particle Filter in Radiance Fields for Robot Visual Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.00312): Uses radiance-field-generated novel views to nudge a particle filter, combining global place recognition with fine 6-DoF pose tracking for monocular robot localization.
- [Learning Manipulation by Predicting Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.00439): Pretrains manipulation features by predicting transition frames and interaction objects from keyframes plus language, teaching where and how objects will be manipulated.
- [PDP: Physics-Based Character Animation via Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.00960): Adapts diffusion-policy ideas to physics-based character animation, generating motions that interact with environments while satisfying simulation constraints.
- [Differentiable Task Graph Learning: Procedural Activity Representation and Online Mistake Detection from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01486): Learns procedural activity representations and online mistake detection from egocentric video.
- [ManiCM: Real-time 3D Diffusion Policy via Consistency Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01586): Distills point-cloud-conditioned diffusion policies into a consistency model that generates robot actions in one inference step for faster 3D manipulation.
- [RoboMamba: Efficient Vision-Language-Action Model for Robotic Reasoning and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04339): Integrates a vision encoder with Mamba and fine-tunes only a small policy head, giving VLA reasoning plus SE(3) pose prediction with lower inference cost.
- [InstructNav: Zero-shot System for Generic Instruction Navigation in Unexplored Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04882): Unifies zero-shot instruction navigation with Dynamic Chain-of-Navigation planning and multi-source value maps that convert language plans into robot trajectories.
- [CoBL-Diffusion: Diffusion-Based Conditional Robot Planning in Dynamic Environments Using Control Barrier and Lyapunov Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05309): Guides diffusion denoising with control barrier and Lyapunov functions so generated control sequences remain safe and stable around dynamic obstacles.
- [Learning Reward and Policy Jointly from Demonstration and Preference Improves Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06874): Jointly trains reward and policy from demonstrations and preferences in one alignment stage, with experiments on LLM alignment and MuJoCo robotic control.
- [BAKU: An Efficient Transformer for Multi-Task Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07539): Combines observation trunks, action chunking, multisensory inputs, and action heads in a transformer policy for efficient offline imitation across simulated and real robot tasks.
- [A3VLM: Actionable Articulation-Aware Vision Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07549): Learns robot-agnostic articulated-object structures and action affordances that can be translated into robot actions through simple primitives.
- [Scaling Manipulation Learning with Visual Kinematic Chain Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07837): Represents quasi-static robot actions as visual kinematic chains and trains a view-flexible transformer to forecast those structures across CALVIN, RLBench, Open-X, and real robots.
- [RVT-2: Learning Precise Manipulation from Few Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08545): Improves robotic-view-transformer architecture and system design for few-demo 3D manipulation, raising RLBench success and learning precise real-world plug insertion from ten demos.
- [LLM-Craft: Robotic Crafting of Elasto-Plastic Objects With Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08648): Uses an LLM to reason over clay-shape differences and iteratively generate deformation action sequences for robotic crafting of elasto-plastic objects.
- [LLM-Driven Robots Risk Enacting Discrimination, Violence, and Unlawful Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08824): Evaluates LLM-driven robot decisions across protected-identity and safety scenarios, showing discriminatory and unsafe action suggestions in HRI tasks.
- [OmniH2O: Universal and Dexterous Human-to-Humanoid Whole-Body Teleoperation and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08858): Uses kinematic pose as a universal interface for VR, verbal, and camera-based humanoid teleoperation, then trains sim-to-real whole-body policies from the OmniH2O-6 demonstrations.
- [OpenVLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09246): Trains a 7B open-source VLA on 970K real-world robot demonstrations and releases checkpoints, fine-tuning notebooks, and training code.
- [DISCO: Language-Guided Manipulation With Diffusion Policies and Constrained Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09767): Converts open-vocabulary instructions into VLM-generated 3D keyframes and uses constrained diffusion inpainting to guide manipulation while balancing learned motion priors.
- [Sim-to-Real Transfer via 3D Feature Fields for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09798): Transfers panoramic VLN policies to monocular robots by predicting semantic traversable maps and novel-view waypoint representations with 3D feature fields.
- [HumanPlus: Humanoid Shadowing and Imitation from Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10454): Builds a humanoid learning stack with low-level RL from human motion data and egocentric human-data imitation for autonomous whole-body skills.
- [Leveraging Locality to Boost Sample Efficiency in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10615): Introduces SGRv2, an imitation-learning policy that encodes action locality around the target object and nearby interactions to reduce robot demonstration requirements.
- [RoboPoint: A Vision-Language Model for Spatial Affordance Prediction for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10721): Instruction-tunes a VLM with synthetic robot-domain data so it predicts image keypoint affordances for language-specified manipulation without real demonstrations.
- [Physically Embodied Gaussian Splatting: A Realtime Correctable World Model for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10788): Builds a realtime correctable Gaussian-splatting world model for robotic interaction and physical-scene updates.
- [AIC MLLM: Autonomous Interactive Correction MLLM for Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11548): Uses prior low-level failure samples to let an MLLM correct contact poses during articulated-object manipulation rather than only revising high-level plans.
- [Imagination Policy: Using Generative Point Cloud Models for Learning Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11740): Generates desired point-cloud goal states and converts them into rigid robot actions, turning pick-and-place policy inference into local generative prediction.
- [Embodied Instruction Following in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11818): Couples exploration with instruction planning so an embodied agent discovers existing objects before generating feasible plans for unknown household environments.
- [Estimating map completeness in robot exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13482): Trains a convolutional network on partially explored grid maps with full-map-derived labels, then uses the predicted explored-area completeness as a stopping rule for mobile-robot exploration.
- [Transferable Tactile Transformers for Representation Learning Across Diverse Sensors and Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13640): Pretrains a shared tactile-transformer trunk with sensor-specific encoders and task-specific decoders over the aggregated FoTa tactile dataset.
- [AlanaVLM: A Multimodal Embodied AI Foundation Model for Egocentric Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13807): Trains a 7B VLM on egocentric video captioning and question answering data so embodied assistants can reason from first-person observations.
- [Enhancing the LLM-Based Robot Manipulation Through Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14097): Combines prompted GPT-4 motion decomposition with YOLO visual cues and human-robot collaboration to make language-driven manipulation plans executable.
- [IRASim: A Fine-Grained World Model for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14540): Trains a diffusion-transformer world model with frame-level action conditioning to generate robot-object interaction videos for policy evaluation and model-based planning.
- [Imperative Learning: A Self-supervised Neural-Symbolic Learning Framework for Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16087): Builds a self-supervised neuro-symbolic learning framework for robot autonomy with neural, reasoning, and memory modules.
- [Venturing into Uncharted Waters: The Navigation Compass from Transformer to Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16722): Surveys Mamba, structured state-space mechanisms, and Transformer-Mamba hybrids from architectural and kernel-function perspectives.
- [Dreamitate: Real-World Visuomotor Policy Learning via Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16862): Fine-tunes a video diffusion model on human task demonstrations and uses generated novel-scene executions as control signals for real robot manipulation.
- [DKPROMPT: Domain Knowledge Prompting Vision-Language Models for Open-World Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.17659): Automates VLM prompting with PDDL domain knowledge so robots can plan and execute natural-language tasks in open worlds.
- [GenRL: Multimodal-foundation world models for generalization in embodied agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18043): Learns multimodal foundation world models that connect language, vision, and environment dynamics for embodied generalization without per-task reward engineering.
- [EgoVideo: Exploring Egocentric Foundation Model and Downstream Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18070): Explores egocentric foundation models and downstream adaptation for first-person video understanding.
- [Open-vocabulary Mobile Manipulation in Unseen Dynamic Environments with 3D Semantic Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18115): Builds dense 3D semantic maps with pretrained VLM detection and grounded recognition, then uses them for open-vocabulary mobile manipulation in changing environments.
- [LLCoach: Generating Robot Soccer Plans using Multi-Role Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18285): Uses multi-role LLM prompting to turn RoboCup soccer context into executable plans over predefined robot behaviors in dynamic team play.
- [Lifelong Robot Library Learning: Bootstrapping Composable and Generalizable Skills for Embodied Control with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18746): Uses an LLM lifelong agent with soft memory, self-guided task exploration, and skill abstraction to grow a composable robot skill library without gradient retraining.
- [RoboUniView: Visual-Language Model with Unified View Representation for Robotic Manipulaiton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18977): Pretrains a unified multi-view representation decoupled from camera parameters, then learns manipulation actions from that representation across CALVIN and cross-camera settings.
- [ManiWAV: Learning Robot Manipulation from In-the-Wild Audio-Visual Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.19464): Collects synchronized ear-in-hand audio and visual demonstrations, then learns contact-rich manipulation policies that use sound to disambiguate object interactions.
- [Text2Robot: Evolutionary Robot Design from Text Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.19963): Converts text specifications into quadruped morphologies through text-to-3D initialization, geometric processing, and body-control co-optimization for manufacturable walking robots.
- [PoliFormer: Scaling On-Policy RL with Transformers Results in Masterful Navigators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.20083): Trains an RGB-only navigation policy with a vision-transformer encoder, causal transformer memory, and large-scale on-policy rollouts that transfer from simulation to robots.
- [OmniJARVIS: Unified Vision-Language-Action Tokenization Enables Open-World Instruction Following Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.00114): Uses unified vision-language-action tokenization and imitation decoding to train open-world Minecraft instruction-following agents.
- [SMPLOlympics: Sports Environments for Physically Simulated Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.00187): Releases sports-style humanoid simulation environments built around SMPL motion priors, giving physically simulated bodies diverse athletic tasks for control learning.
- [Language-Guided Object-Centric Diffusion Policy for Collision-Aware Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.00451): Conditions an object-centric diffusion policy on language and collision constraints so manipulation trajectories stay task-directed while avoiding scene contacts.
- [Sparse Diffusion Policy: A Sparse, Reusable, and Flexible Policy for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.01531): Makes diffusion policies sparse over observation and action structure, aiming for reusable robot policies that adapt across tasks without dense monolithic control.
- [Ego-Foresight: Agent Visuomotor Prediction as Regularization for RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.01570): Regularizes reinforcement learning with egocentric future visuomotor prediction, using anticipated agent-view dynamics as an auxiliary signal for embodied policies.
- [LDP: A Local Diffusion Planner for Efficient Robot Navigation and Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.01950): Plans navigation with a local diffusion model that generates collision-avoiding motion around nearby obstacles instead of relying only on global path search.
- [TrAME: Trajectory-Anchored Multi-View Editing for Text-Guided 3D Gaussian Splatting Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02034): Anchors text-guided Gaussian-splat editing to object trajectories, making 3D scene manipulation consistent across views and time.
- [DextrAH-G: Pixels-to-Action Dexterous Arm-Hand Grasping with Geometric Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02274): Maps pixels to dexterous arm-hand grasps using geometric fabrics, tying visual perception to coordinated hand and arm control.
- [VoxAct-B: Voxel-Based Acting and Stabilizing Policy for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.04152): Uses voxelized scene state to produce both bimanual actions and stabilizing behaviors, targeting two-arm manipulation where one hand must support the other.
- [Dadu-Corki: Algorithm-Architecture Co-Design for Embodied AI-powered Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.04292): Co-designs the manipulation algorithm with system architecture so embodied AI policies can run efficiently on robotic manipulation hardware.
- [RAM: Retrieval-Based Affordance Transfer for Generalizable Zero-Shot Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.04689): Retrieves related affordance examples and transfers them to novel objects, enabling zero-shot manipulation decisions without retraining on each object category.
- [BiRoDiff: Diffusion Policies for Bipedal Robot Locomotion on Unseen Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.05424): Trains diffusion policies for biped locomotion and evaluates generalization to terrain types not seen during policy learning.
- [RoboCAS: A Benchmark for Robotic Manipulation in Complex Object Arrangement Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.06951): Provides complex object-arrangement manipulation scenarios that stress policies on cluttered placement, rearrangement, and spatial-relation reasoning.
- [Mobility VLA: Multimodal Instruction Navigation with Long-Context VLMs and Topological Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.07775): Combines long-context vision-language modeling with topological graphs so a mobile agent can follow multimodal navigation instructions over extended routes.
- [Generative Image as Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.07875): Represents actions through generated images, turning visual prediction into an action model for embodied control.
- [Vegetable Peeling: A Case Study in Constrained Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.07884): Studies vegetable peeling as a constrained dexterous manipulation problem with tool contact, object compliance, and safety limits.
- [Learning In-Hand Translation Using Tactile Skin with Shear and Normal Force Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.07885): Learns in-hand object translation from tactile skin readings that capture both shear and normal forces during manipulation.
- [RoboMorph: Evolving Robot Morphology using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.08626): Uses LLM-guided evolutionary search to propose robot morphologies, connecting text descriptions with embodied design optimization.
- [DexGrasp-Diffusion: Diffusion-based Unified Functional Grasp Synthesis Pipeline for Multi-Dexterous Robotic Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.09899): Uses diffusion to synthesize functional grasps across multiple dexterous robotic hand designs rather than one fixed hand model.
- [Affordance-Guided Reinforcement Learning via Visual Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.10341): Injects visual affordance prompts into reinforcement learning so exploration is biased toward object regions likely to support successful manipulation.
- [UMI on Legs: Making Manipulation Policies Mobile with Manipulation-Centric Whole-body Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.10353): Adds manipulation-centric whole-body control to legged robots so policies trained for tabletop manipulation can move with the robot base.
- [GRUtopia: Dream General Robots in a City at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.10943): Builds a city-scale embodied simulation platform for general robots, supporting large navigation and interaction scenarios rather than isolated tabletop tasks.
- [ThinkGrasp: A Vision-Language System for Strategic Part Grasping in Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11298): Uses vision-language reasoning to choose strategic object parts for grasping in cluttered scenes, separating grasp target selection from low-level control.
- [RobotKeyframing: Learning Locomotion with High-Level Objectives via Mixture of Dense and Sparse Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11562): Learns locomotion by mixing dense rewards with sparse keyframe objectives, giving policies high-level motion targets without hand-designing every step.
- [Flow Matching Imitation Learning for Multi-Support Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.12381): Applies flow-matching imitation learning to multi-support manipulation, modeling contact-rich trajectory distributions where several supports constrain motion.
- [OVGNet: A Unified Visual-Linguistic Framework for Open-Vocabulary Robotic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.13175): Unifies visual and language features for open-vocabulary grasp detection, letting robots localize graspable targets described by free-form categories.
- [Simultaneous Localization and Affordance Prediction of Tasks from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.13856): Learns to localize task-relevant moments and predict affordances from egocentric video, turning human first-person demonstrations into embodied task cues.
- [Flow as the Cross-Domain Manipulation Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.15208): Treats flow fields as a shared interface for transferring manipulation behavior across domains and embodiments.
- [SARO: Space-Aware Robot System for Terrain Crossing via Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.16412): Uses a vision-language model to reason about space and terrain affordances so robots can choose feasible crossing actions.
- [From Imitation to Refinement - Residual RL for Precise Visual Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.16677): Starts from imitation policies and applies residual reinforcement learning to refine high-precision visual assembly actions.
- [Robust Point Cloud Registration in Robotic Inspection With Locally Consistent Gaussian Mixture Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.17183): Uses locally consistent Gaussian mixture modeling for robust point-cloud registration in robot inspection, supporting 3D localization under partial observations.
- [Lessons from Learning to Spin "Pens"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.18902): Analyzes policies for pen-spinning style dexterous manipulation, surfacing practical lessons about data, control, and evaluation for dynamic in-hand skills.
- [Theia: Distilling Diverse Vision Foundation Models for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.20179): Distills multiple vision foundation models into robot-learning representations, reducing dependence on a single visual backbone for manipulation policies.
- [ReplanVLM: Replanning Robotic Tasks With Visual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21762): Uses a visual-language model to detect task progress and replan robot actions when execution diverges from the original plan.
- [Astra: Efficient Transformer Architecture and Contrastive Dynamics Learning for Embodied Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.01147): Pairs an efficient transformer policy with contrastive dynamics learning for instruction-following agents in embodied environments.
- [Body of Her: A Preliminary Study on End-to-End Humanoid Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.02879): Builds an end-to-end multimodal network for speech and full-body humanoid behavior generation.
- [Task-oriented Sequential Grounding and Navigation in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.04034): Grounds task instructions as sequential 3D scene targets, coupling object grounding with navigation decisions in indoor environments.
- [FORGE: Force-Guided Exploration for Robust Contact-Rich Manipulation Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.04587): Uses force feedback to guide exploration in uncertain contact-rich manipulation, helping policies discover stable interaction strategies.
- [UniT: Data Efficient Tactile Representation With Generalization to Unseen Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.06481): Learns data-efficient tactile representations that generalize to unseen objects.
- [Learning Multi-Modal Whole-Body Control for Real-World Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.07295): Trains real humanoid whole-body control from multiple modalities, linking perception inputs with coordinated locomotion and manipulation actions.
- [General-Purpose Clothes Manipulation with Semantic Keypoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.08160): Represents garments with semantic keypoints so a robot can generalize clothes manipulation across categories and deformations.
- [Scaling Cross-Embodied Learning: One Policy for Manipulation, Navigation, Locomotion and Aviation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.11812): Scales a single policy across manipulation, navigation, locomotion, and aviation embodiments, testing whether shared action learning survives large embodiment shifts.
- [Multi-Finger Manipulation via Trajectory Optimization With Differentiable Rolling and Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13229): Combines trajectory optimization with differentiable rolling and geometric constraints for multi-finger object manipulation.
- [GR-MG: Leveraging Partially-Annotated Data via Multi-Modal Goal-Conditioned Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.14368): Trains a multimodal goal-conditioned policy from partially annotated data, reducing label requirements for robot manipulation goals.
- [Robo-GS: A Physics Consistent Spatial-Temporal Model for Robotic Arm with Hybrid Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.14873): Uses Gaussian-Mesh-Pixel binding to combine geometry, 3D Gaussians, and physics attributes for robotic-arm Real2Sim2Real control.
- [Depth Restoration of Hand-Held Transparent Objects for Human-to-Robot Handover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.14997): Restores depth for transparent hand-held objects, improving the 3D perception needed for safe human-to-robot handover.
- [Diffusion Policy Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.00588): Applies policy optimization to diffusion policies for embodied control.
- [Affordance-based Robot Manipulation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.01083): Uses flow matching to generate manipulation actions conditioned on affordance cues, tying policy generation to object interaction regions.
- [ReKep: Spatio-Temporal Reasoning of Relational Keypoint Constraints for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.01652): Represents manipulation goals as relational keypoint constraints over space and time, enabling robots to reason about object-part relations during execution.
- [Mamba as a Motion Encoder for Robotic Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02636): Uses Mamba sequence modeling as a motion encoder for imitation learning, targeting long action histories without transformer-style cost.
- [RoVi-Aug: Robot and Viewpoint Augmentation for Cross-Embodiment Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.03403): Augments robot embodiments and camera viewpoints to improve cross-embodiment policy transfer from shared demonstrations.
- [View-Invariant Policy Learning via Zero-Shot Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.03685): Uses zero-shot novel-view synthesis to train policies that remain stable when robot camera viewpoints change.
- [ActionFlow: Equivariant, Accurate, and Efficient Policies with Spatially Symmetric Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.04576): Builds equivariant flow-matching policies that exploit spatial symmetries for more accurate and efficient robot action generation.
- [High-Speed and Impact Resilient Teleoperation of Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.04639): Designs a high-speed humanoid teleoperation and learning setup that emphasizes impact resilience during real robot motion.
- [Forward KL Regularized Preference Optimization for Aligning Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.05622): Aligns offline diffusion policies to preference data via direct preference optimization with forward KL regularization to avoid out-of-distribution actions.
- [Robot Utility Models: General Policies for Zero-Shot Deployment in New Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.05865): Learns general robot utility policies intended for zero-shot deployment, emphasizing behavior selection in environments not seen during training.
- [InterACT: Inter-dependency Aware Action Chunking with Hierarchical Attention Transformers for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.07914): Models dependencies between two arms with hierarchical attention and action chunking, improving coordinated bimanual manipulation sequences.
- [Touch2Touch: Cross-Modal Tactile Generation for Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08269): Generates tactile signals across modalities for object manipulation, supporting policies that need touch information when direct tactile data is missing.
- [Hand-Object Interaction Pretraining from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08273): Pretrains visual representations from videos of hand-object interactions for embodied manipulation understanding.
- [AnySkin: Plug-and-Play Skin Sensing for Robotic Touch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08276): Provides plug-and-play skin sensing for robotic touch, expanding tactile inputs for embodied systems.
- [DexSim2Real$^{\mathbf{2}}$: Building Explicit World Model for Precise Articulated Object Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08750): Builds an explicit world model for articulated-object dexterous manipulation, supporting precise sim-to-real transfer of contact-rich skills.
- [Closed-Loop Visuomotor Control with Generative Expectation for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.09016): Uses generative expectations of future observations inside closed-loop visuomotor control, letting manipulation policies react to mismatched outcomes.
- [ManiDext: Hand-Object Manipulation Synthesis via Continuous Correspondence Embeddings and Residual-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.09300): Synthesizes hand-object manipulation with continuous correspondence embeddings and residual-guided diffusion for dexterous interaction trajectories.
- [A Control Barrier Function for Safe Navigation with Online Gaussian Splatting Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.09868): Builds control barrier functions over online Gaussian-splat maps so a robot can enforce safety constraints while navigating reconstructed 3D scenes.
- [Towards Physically Realizable Adversarial Attacks in Embodied Vision Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.10071): Studies physical adversarial attacks against embodied vision-navigation agents, focusing on perturbations that can be realized in the environment.
- [SplatSim: Zero-Shot Sim2Real Transfer of RGB Manipulation Policies Using Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.10161): Replaces mesh rendering with Gaussian splats in simulators to create photorealistic RGB training data for zero-shot sim-to-real manipulation policies.
- [BEINGS: Bayesian Embodied Image-Goal Navigation With Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.10216): Combines Bayesian image-goal navigation with Gaussian-splat scene representations, maintaining uncertainty while navigating toward visual goals.
- [HiFi-CS: Towards Open Vocabulary Visual Grounding For Robotic Grasping Using Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.10419): Uses vision-language models for open-vocabulary visual grounding, selecting grasp targets from language in cluttered robot scenes.
- [Bayesian Optimal Experimental Design for Robot Kinematic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.10802): Applies Bayesian experimental design to robot kinematic calibration, choosing measurements that reduce calibration uncertainty efficiently.
- [Pragmatic Embodied Spoken Instruction Following in Human-Robot Collaboration with Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.10849): Models pragmatic spoken instruction following with theory-of-mind signals so robots can infer collaborative intent beyond literal commands.
- [One Map to Find Them All: Real-time Open-Vocabulary Mapping for Zero-shot Multi-Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.11764): Builds a reusable open-vocabulary feature map with probabilistic semantic updates so a robot can reuse prior searches for real-time multi-object navigation.
- [AlignBot: Aligning VLM-Powered Customized Task Planning with User Reminders Through Fine-Tuning for Household Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.11905): Fine-tunes a LLaVA-7B adapter that converts personalized reminders into structured cues for GPT-4o household task planning, with retrieval of past successes.
- [DynaMo: In-Domain Dynamics Pretraining for Visuo-Motor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.12192): Pretrains visuomotor representations from robot demonstrations by jointly learning latent inverse and forward dynamics without internet or cross-embodiment data.
- [TinyVLA: Toward Fast, Data-Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.12514): Initializes compact VLA policies from fast multimodal backbones and adds a diffusion action decoder, targeting lower-latency manipulation without large robot pretraining.
- [Automatic Behavior Tree Expansion with LLMs for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.13356): Uses an LLM to expand and permanently update behavior-tree robot policies when planning or execution failures exceed the current tree's capability.
- [SoloParkour: Constrained Reinforcement Learning for Visual Locomotion from Privileged Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.13678): Trains depth-to-control quadruped parkour policies by warm-starting visual RL from a privileged-information policy under safety constraints.
- [Tactile Neural De-rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.13923): Reconstructs local 3D object structure from tactile signatures using a generative de-rendering model, supporting tactile pose estimation with uncertainty.
- [The Foundational Pose as a Selection Mechanism for the Design of Tool-Wielding Multi-Finger Robotic Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14158): Uses foundational tool-use poses as constraints in sampling-based multi-objective hand design, then validates the design criterion with simulated and prototype hands.
- [D3RoMa: Disparity Diffusion-based Depth Sensing for Material-Agnostic Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14365): Formulates stereo depth restoration as diffusion-based disparity prediction with left-right consistency guidance, improving manipulation around transparent and specular objects.
- [GraspMamba: A Mamba-based Language-driven Grasp Detection Framework with Hierarchical Feature Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14403): Fuses multi-scale Mamba vision features with language descriptions for faster grasp detection in cluttered scenes and real robot trials.
- [Scaling Diffusion Policy in Transformer to 1 Billion Parameters for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14411): Stabilizes large diffusion-transformer policies with factored observation embeddings and non-causal action attention, scaling visuomotor control to billion-parameter models.
- [Admittance Visuomotor Policy Learning for General-Purpose Contact-Rich Manipulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14440): Predicts action trajectories and desired contact forces from vision, proprioception, and force signals, then executes them through admittance control.
- [Updating Robot Safety Representations Online From Natural Language Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14580): Interprets language feedback and robot images with VLMs to update safety constraints online and warm-start Hamilton-Jacobi reachability controllers.
- [Observe Then Act: Asynchronous Active Vision-Action Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14891): Serially couples a next-best-view camera policy with a next-best-pose gripper policy for manipulation under occlusion and limited viewpoints.
- [ReVLA: Reverting Visual Domain Limitation of Robotic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.15250): Uses reversible visual adaptation to reduce domain limitations in robotic foundation models while preserving the original VLA policy interface.
- [In the Wild Ungraspable Object Picking with Bimanual Nonprehensile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.15465): Learns bimanual nonprehensile picking strategies for objects that cannot be grasped directly, using coordinated pushing and support actions.
- [SYNERGAI: Perception Alignment for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.15684): Aligns robot perception with human collaborators so shared task state and intent can be interpreted consistently during human-robot work.
- [Generative Factor Chaining: Coordinated Manipulation with Diffusion-based Factor Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16275): Represents coordinated manipulation as chained factors and uses diffusion over a factor graph to generate compatible multi-part actions.
- [Embedded IPC: Fast and Intersection-Free Simulation in Reduced Subspace for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16385): Accelerates intersection-free incremental-potential-contact simulation in a reduced subspace, giving robot manipulation models a faster differentiable physics backend.
- [Hierarchical Hybrid Learning for Long-Horizon Contact-Rich Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16451): Combines hierarchical task decomposition with hybrid learning to handle long-horizon assembly sequences that require precise contact-rich actions.
- [Behav: Behavioral Rule Guided Autonomy Using VLMs for Robot Navigation in Outdoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16484): Converts VLM-interpreted outdoor scene context into behavioral rules that guide robot navigation decisions beyond simple goal following.
- [FLaRe: Achieving Masterful and Adaptive Robot Policies with Large-Scale Reinforcement Learning Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16578): Fine-tunes robot policies with large-scale RL to adapt pretrained behaviors beyond demonstration cloning and improve downstream manipulation performance.
- [Blox-Net: Generative Design-for-Robot-Assembly Using VLM Supervision, Physics Simulation, and a Robot with Reset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17126): Generates assembly designs under VLM supervision, filters them with physics simulation, and uses robot reset interactions to support assembly learning.
- [Active Vision Might Be All You Need: Exploring Active Vision in Bimanual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17435): Studies bimanual manipulation policies that actively select views, showing how camera motion can substitute for fixed passive observations.
- [Canonical Representation and Force-Based Pretraining of 3D Tactile for Dexterous Visuo-Tactile Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17549): Pretrains 3D tactile representations with canonicalization and force signals before using them in dexterous visual-tactile policies.
- [Episodic Memory Verbalization Using Hierarchical Representations of Life-Long Robot Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17702): Converts long-term robot experience into hierarchical verbal episodic memories so later embodied tasks can retrieve and reason over past events.
- [Language-Embedded Gaussian Splats (LEGS): Incrementally Building Room-Scale Representations with a Mobile Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18108): Incrementally builds room-scale Gaussian splats with language embeddings from mobile-robot observations, enabling open-vocabulary spatial queries.
- [Robot See Robot Do: Imitating Articulated Object Manipulation with Monocular 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18121): Uses monocular 4D reconstruction of human demonstrations to retarget articulated-object manipulation policies to robot execution.
- [iWalker: Imperative Visual Planning for Walking Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18361): A model-side humanoid-control method using imperative-learning bilevel optimization for visual footstep planning and whole-body balancing; record for migration to Model.
- [Open-Nav: Exploring Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environment with Open-Source LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18794): Tests open-source LLMs as zero-shot VLN planners in continuous environments rather than relying on closed proprietary models.
- [A Parameter-Efficient Tuning Framework for Language-Guided Object Grounding and Robot Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.19457): A model-side grounding and grasping method using CLIP adapters and depth fusion for referring segmentation, grasp synthesis, and grasp affordance; record for migration to Model.
- [Grounding 3D Scene Affordance From Egocentric Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.19650): Learns 3D scene affordances from egocentric interaction traces, grounding where actions are possible in embodied spatial representations.
- [GravMAD: Grounded Spatial Value Maps Guided Action Diffusion for Generalized 3D Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.20154): Guides action diffusion with grounded spatial value maps so 3D manipulation trajectories align with task-relevant object regions.
- [RL-GSBridge: 3D Gaussian Splatting Based Real2Sim2Real Method for Robotic Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.20291): Uses 3D Gaussian splatting to build a real-to-sim-to-real bridge for training manipulation policies from reconstructed scenes.
- [Opt2Skill: Imitating Dynamically-Feasible Whole-Body Trajectories for Versatile Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.20514): Imitates optimized whole-body trajectories that satisfy dynamic feasibility, enabling humanoids to combine locomotion and manipulation skills.
- [UniAff: A Unified Representation of Affordances for Tool Usage and Articulation with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.20551): Learns a unified affordance representation for tool use and articulated-object interaction with vision-language models.
- [AHA: A Vision-Language-Model for Detecting and Reasoning Over Failures in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00371): Trains a VLM to detect manipulation failures and reason over their causes, supporting corrective robot execution.
- [CANVAS: Commonsense-Aware Navigation System for Intuitive Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01273): Injects commonsense reasoning into human-robot navigation so a robot can infer intuitive routes and interaction constraints from language.
- [Closed-Loop Long-Horizon Robotic Planning via Equilibrium Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01440): Models long-horizon robot plans as equilibrium action sequences, allowing closed-loop replanning as observations change.
- [Guiding Long-Horizon Task and Motion Planning with Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02193): Uses VLM-derived scene and task guidance to constrain long-horizon task and motion planning for robot manipulation.
- [Efficient Residual Learning with Mixture-of-Experts for Universal Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02475): Combines residual learning with mixture-of-experts routing to adapt dexterous grasping policies across objects and hand configurations.
- [Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Grasping with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02479): Uses reinforcement learning to transfer dexterous grasping behaviors across different robot hands and embodiments.
- [ReLIC: A Recipe for 64k Steps of In-Context Reinforcement Learning for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02751): Extends in-context reinforcement learning to 64k-step embodied episodes, testing long-context policy adaptation in interactive environments.
- [Task-agnostic Lifelong Robot Learning with Retrieval-based Weighted Local Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02995): Retrieves related prior experience and weights local adaptation so a robot can update policies across tasks without task-specific retraining.
- [Autoregressive Action Sequence Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03132): Treats manipulation control as autoregressive prediction over action sequences, modeling temporal dependencies directly during policy learning.
- [SELU: Self-Learning Embodied MLLMs in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03303): Lets embodied MLLMs self-improve in unknown environments by collecting interaction outcomes and updating their environment-grounded behavior.
- [Open-World Reinforcement Learning over Long Short-Term Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03618): Uses long and short horizon imagination rollouts to guide reinforcement learning in open-world embodied environments.
- [GenSim2: Scaling Robot Data Generation with Multi-modal and Reasoning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03645): Uses multimodal and reasoning LLMs to generate scalable robot training data and simulation scenarios.
- [Estimating Body and Hand Motion in an Ego-sensed World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03665): Estimates full-body and hand motion in an allocentric scene frame from egocentric sensing, using head-motion conditioning and kinematic constraints.
- [GR-2: A Generative Video-Language-Action Model with Web-Scale Knowledge for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.06158): Pretrains on 38 million internet videos, then fine-tunes for robot video generation and action prediction across more than 100 manipulation tasks.
- [Automated Creation of Digital Cousins for Robust Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07408): Automatically creates simulation scenes with similar geometric and semantic affordances to a real scene, improving zero-shot sim-to-real policy transfer.
