- [ForceMimic: Force-Centric Imitation Learning with Force-Motion Capture System for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07554): Collects force-motion demonstrations without a robot and trains hybrid force-position imitation policies for contact-rich tasks such as peeling.
- [RDT-1B: a Diffusion Foundation Model for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07864): Scales a diffusion transformer to 1.2B parameters with a unified physical action space for multi-robot bimanual manipulation.
- [Towards Synergistic, Generalized, and Efficient Dual-System for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08001): Pairs a VLA generalist with a small diffusion-transformer specialist, improving control frequency and data efficiency for manipulation.
- [SPA: 3D Spatial-Awareness Enables Effective Embodied Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08208): Uses differentiable neural rendering over multi-view images to give ViT representations 3D spatial awareness across embodied tasks and simulators.
- [Exploring Spatial Representation to Enhance LLM Reasoning in Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08500): Builds a semantic-topo-metric map prompt from projected landmark masks so an LLM can reason about aerial VLN actions.
- [VLM See, Robot Do: Human Demo Video to Robot Action Plan via Vision Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08792): Converts human demonstration videos into robot task plans through keyframe selection, visual perception, and VLM reasoning.
- [iTeach: In the Wild Interactive Teaching for Failure-Driven Adaptation of Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09072): Uses co-located human interaction, gaze and voice labeling, and propagated RGB-D supervision to adapt robot perception after deployment failures.
- [The Ingredients for Robotic Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10088): Identifies architectural choices for diffusion transformer policies and demonstrates long-horizon bimanual ALOHA control with language-annotated demonstrations.
- [PIVOT-R: Primitive-Driven Waypoint-Aware World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10394): Predicts primitive-driven task waypoints with a waypoint-aware world model and decodes actions through an asynchronous hierarchical executor.
- [Generalizable Humanoid Manipulation with Improved 3D Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10803): Combines full-upper-body teleoperation data, LiDAR observations, and improved 3D diffusion policies for real humanoid manipulation in varied scenes.
- [M2Diffuser: Diffusion-based Trajectory Optimization for Mobile Manipulation in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11402): Generates scene-conditioned whole-body mobile-manipulation trajectories from 3D scans and uses differentiable cost guidance to reduce physical violations.
- [OKAMI: Teaching Humanoid Robots Manipulation Skills through Single Video Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11792): Retargets a single RGB-D human video to humanoid manipulation plans with object-aware body and hand motion transfer.
- [Learning from 10 Demos: Generalisable and Sample-Efficient Policy Learning with Oriented Affordance Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12124): Uses oriented affordance frames and self-progress prediction to compose manipulation sub-policies from only ten demonstrations.
- [Harmon: Whole-Body Motion Generation of Humanoid Robots from Language Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12773): Initializes humanoid motions from human motion priors and refines them with VLM commonsense reasoning to match language descriptions.
- [In-Context Learning Enables Robot Action Prediction in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12782): Converts keyframes, end-effector actions, object poses, and instructions into text demonstrations so an off-the-shelf LLM can predict robot actions.
- [Just Add Force for Contact-Rich Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13124): Adds gripper force feedback to a small public grasping dataset and trains diffusion policies that improve delicate object grasping.
- [ALOHA Unleashed: A Simple Recipe for Robot Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13126): Shows that large-scale ALOHA 2 demonstrations plus expressive diffusion policies can learn deformable and contact-rich bimanual dexterity tasks.
- [Steering Your Generalists: Improving Robotic Foundation Models via Value Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13816): Reranks actions from frozen generalist robot policies with an offline-RL value function, improving deployment without fine-tuning policy weights.
- [Diff-Dagger: Uncertainty Estimation With Diffusion Policy for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.14868): Uses the diffusion-policy training objective to estimate uncertainty for robot-gated DAgger, reducing expert queries and improving failure prediction.
- [Cage: Causal Attention Enables Data-Efficient Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.14974): Combines DINOv2, LoRA, causal Perceiver token compression, and a diffusion action head for data-efficient RGB and RGB-D manipulation.
- [Semantically Safe Robot Manipulation: From Semantic Scene Understanding to Motion Safeguards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15185): Builds semantic maps, uses LLMs to infer unsafe object relations and poses, and certifies manipulation actions with control-barrier safety filters.
- [EVA: An Embodied World Model for Future Video Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15461): Combines Reflection-of-Generation reasoning strategies with EVA-Bench and a multistage video-anticipation world model for in-domain and OOD embodied future prediction.
- [GRS: Generating Robotic Simulation Tasks from Real-World Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15536): Turns a single RGB-D observation into a digital-twin simulation by using SAM2 segmentation, VLM object descriptions, asset matching, generated tasks, and test-suite refinement.
- [MSGField: A Unified Scene Representation Integrating Motion, Semantics, and Geometry for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15730): Represents dynamic manipulation scenes with 2D Gaussians enriched by semantic attributes and compact motion bases for real-time language-guided robot action.
- [MotionGlot: A Multi-Embodied Motion Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16623): Adapts language-model instruction tuning to generate motions across embodiments with different action dimensions, including quadruped locomotion and human motion prompts.
- [EnvBridge: Bridging Diverse Environments with Cross-Environment Knowledge Transfer for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16919): Stores successful LLM-generated robot control code from source environments and transfers it to RLBench, MetaWorld, and CALVIN target environments.
- [Proleptic Temporal Ensemble for Improving the Speed of Robot Tasks Generated by Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16981): Applies temporal ensembling to pretrained imitation policies so robots execute future actions earlier and speed up task completion without extra demonstrations.
- [Learning Precise, Contact-Rich Manipulation through Uncalibrated Tactile Skins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.17246): Uses ViSk, a transformer policy that treats low-dimensional magnetic skin readings as tactile tokens alongside vision, improving contact-rich manipulation such as swiping and insertions.
- [GenDP: 3D Semantic Fields for Category-Level Generalizable Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.17488): Conditions diffusion policies on 3D descriptor and semantic fields built from multi-view RGB-D observations and foundation vision models for category-level manipulation generalization.
- [SPIRE: Synergistic Planning, Imitation, and Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18065): Uses task-and-motion planning to decompose long-horizon manipulation, then combines imitation learning and reinforcement learning on the resulting subproblems.
- [Data Scaling Laws in Imitation Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18647): Empirically measures how imitation-policy generalization changes as robot demonstrations scale across objects and environments within the same manipulation category.
- [Diffusion for Multi-Embodiment Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18835): Uses equivariant diffusion with gripper-agnostic scene encoding and gripper-aware pose decoding so grasp strategies transfer across different end-effectors.
- [SkillMimicGen: Automated Demonstration Generation for Efficient Skill Learning and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18907): Segments a few demonstrations into manipulation skills, adapts them to new contexts, stitches transit motions, and trains hybrid skill policies for deployment.
- [Versatile Demonstration Interface: Toward More Flexible Robot Demonstration Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.19141): Provides a collaborative-robot attachment for collecting teleoperation, kinesthetic, and natural demonstrations through vision, force sensing, and state tracking; this is mainly data-collection infrastructure for learning from demonstration rather than a policy model.
- [Learning Diffusion Policies from Demonstrations For Compliant Contact-rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.19235): Trains DIPCOM diffusion policies for compliant contact-rich control, targeting force-intensive manipulation tasks such as powder grinding.
- [Da-Vil: Adaptive Dual-Arm Manipulation with Reinforcement Learning and Variable Impedance Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.19712): Combines feedback-driven policy learning with gradient-based optimization to learn variable-impedance gains for adaptive dual-arm manipulation.
- [On-Robot Reinforcement Learning with Goal-Contrastive Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.19989): Learns dense goal-contrastive rewards from passive videos, giving on-robot RL a scalable signal for exploration under sparse task success feedback.
- [EfficientEQA: An Efficient Approach to Open-Vocabulary Embodied Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.20263): Couples semantic-value-weighted frontier exploration with free-form answer generation for open-vocabulary embodied question answering by mobile robots.
- [HOVER: Versatile Neural Whole-Body Controller for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.21229): Distills multiple humanoid control modes into one whole-body policy using full-body kinematic motion imitation as a shared abstraction.
- [One-Step Diffusion Policy: Fast Visuomotor Policies via Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.21257): Distills pretrained diffusion policies into a single-step action generator, reducing iterative denoising latency for real-time visuomotor control.
- [Precise and Dexterous Robotic Manipulation via Human-in-the-Loop Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.21845): Integrates demonstrations, human corrections, and efficient vision-based RL to train dynamic manipulation, precision assembly, and dual-arm coordination policies in hours.
- [Analyzing Multimodal Interaction Strategies for LLM-Assisted Manipulation of 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.22177): Off-axis for embodied/VLA: studies user interaction patterns in LLM-assisted 3D scene editing rather than training a robot policy, world model, or affordance mechanism.
- [Local Policies Enable Zero-Shot Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.22332): Uses pose-invariant local policies from ManipGen with foundation models for vision, language, and planning to enable zero-shot long-horizon sim-to-real manipulation.
- [A Large Recurrent Action Model: xLSTM enables Fast Inference for Robotics Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.22391): Evaluates xLSTM and Mamba as offline sequence-modeled action policies, aiming for transformer-level robot behavior with faster recurrent inference.
- [Multi-Task Interactive Robot Fleet Learning with Visual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.22689): Uses a visual world model to forecast robot-fleet action outcomes, trigger anomaly predictors, and request human corrections during multi-task deployment.
- [VisualPredicator: Learning Abstract World Models with Neuro-Symbolic Predicates for Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.23156): Invents neuro-symbolic predicates online and learns abstract world models for robot planning across simulated manipulation and navigation domains.
- [KALM: Keypoint Abstraction Using Large Models for Object-Relative Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.23254): Uses large models to extract object-relative keypoints from demonstrations, giving imitation policies a compact representation that transfers across object configurations.
- [Sparsh: Self-supervised touch representations for vision-based tactile sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.24090): Pretrains general-purpose touch encoders with self-supervised objectives across vision-based tactile sensors, replacing task-specific tactile perception models.
- [3D-ViTac: Learning Fine-Grained Manipulation with Visuo-Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.24091): Fuses dense tactile sensing and vision into a unified 3D representation and couples it with diffusion policies for fine-grained bimanual manipulation.
- [pi0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.24164): A vision-language-action flow model for general robot control, making flow matching a central VLA action-generation design.
- [DexMimicGen: Automated Data Generation for Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation via Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.24185): Generates large-scale simulated demonstrations for bimanual dexterous manipulation, reducing manual data collection for humanoid-style imitation learning.
- [EgoMimic: Scaling Imitation Learning via Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.24221): Captures egocentric human video with 3D hand tracking, aligns it to a low-cost bimanual robot, and co-trains manipulation policies on human and robot data.
- [Learning to Look Around: Enhancing Teleoperation and Learning with a Human-like Actuated Neck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00704): Adds a five-DoF actuated neck to teleoperation, improving natural viewpoint control and supplying demonstrations for imitation-learned whole-body manipulation policies.
- [IGOR: Image-GOal Representations are the Atomic Control Units for Foundation Models in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00785): Learns image-goal latent action representations as a shared action space for transfer among human activities and robot embodiments.
- [SPOT: SE(3) Pose Trajectory Diffusion for Object-Centric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00965): Represents tasks as target-relative SE(3) object pose trajectories and uses diffusion imitation learning to decouple manipulation goals from robot embodiment.
- [The Role of Domain Randomization in Training Diffusion Policies for Whole-Body Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01349): Studies how dataset size, diversity, and domain randomization affect diffusion policies for simulated whole-body humanoid control.
- [So You Think You Can Scale Up Autonomous Robot Data Collection?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01813): Analyzes autonomous imitation-learning data collection, where robots expand beyond seed demonstrations to reduce human supervision and environment instrumentation.
- [ManiBox: Enhancing Embodied Spatial Generalization via Scalable Simulation Data Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01850): Uses bounding-box-guided simulation data generation to decouple perception from policy generalization and improve spatial robustness in manipulation.
- [RoboCrowd: Scaling Robot Data Collection Through Crowdsourcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01915): Builds a crowdsourced ALOHA demonstration pipeline with incentive design so robot policy data collection can scale beyond expert teleoperators.
- [DeeR-VLA: Dynamic Inference of Multimodal Large Language Models for Efficient Robot Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02359): Adds dynamic early exits to robotic MLLMs so CALVIN manipulation uses 5.2-6.5x less LLM compute and 2-6x less LLM memory without performance loss.
- [Digitizing Touch with an Artificial Multimodal Fingertip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02479): Digitizes touch with a multimodal fingertip sensor, expanding tactile perception signals for embodied agents and robots.
- [NeRF-Aug: Data Augmentation for Robotics with Neural Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02482): Uses neural radiance fields to synthesize augmented robot observations, expanding visual diversity for policy training.
- [RT-Affordance: Affordances are Versatile Intermediate Representations for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02704): Plans key robot poses as affordances from task language, then conditions a manipulation policy on those affordance plans using web and robot supervision.
- [LEGATO: Cross-Embodiment Imitation Using a Grasping Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.03682): Uses a handheld gripper to unify action and observation spaces, then trains visuomotor policies with motion-invariant losses for cross-embodiment transfer.
- [Object-Centric Dexterous Manipulation from Human Motion Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04005): Learns a hierarchical robot policy from human hand motion data, using an object-centric trajectory generator to bridge human-to-robot dexterous manipulation.
- [DexH2R: Task-oriented Dexterous Manipulation from Human to Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04428): Combines human hand-motion retargeting with a task-oriented residual action policy to transfer dexterous skills to robotic hands.
- [Enhancing Robustness in Language-Driven Robotics: A Modular Approach to Failure Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.05474): Uses a modular LLM-driven robotics architecture to ground plans in physical state and align actions with robot capabilities, reducing execution failures.
- [Robust Adaptive Safe Robotic Grasping with Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07833): Uses tactile contact estimates with control-barrier-function safety filters to regulate fingertip forces and prevent object damage or slippage during grasping.
- [AD-DINO: Attention-Dynamic DINO for Distance-Aware Embodied Reference Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.08451): Extends virtual touch lines into distance-aware attention-dynamic touch lines, jointly predicting a pointed object's box and the gesture attention source.
- [VidMan: Exploiting Implicit Dynamics from Video Diffusion Model for Effective Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.09153): Trains a two-stage video-diffusion manipulation model that exploits implicit visual dynamics while relating observations to robot actions.
- [Motion Before Action: Diffusing Object Motion as Manipulation Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.09658): Runs cascaded diffusion processes that first predict future object pose trajectories and then condition robot action generation on the inferred object motion.
- [‘What Did the Robot Do in My Absence?’ Video Foundation Models to Enhance Intermittent Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.10016): Uses video foundation models to summarize long robot-team observation streams as storyboards, short videos, and text for intermittent human supervision.
- [VLN-Game: Vision-Language Equilibrium Search for Zero-Shot Semantic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11609): Constructs a 3D object-centric spatial map and applies vision-language equilibrium search for zero-shot navigation to object names and descriptive language targets.
- [Robot Collapse: Supply Chain Backdoor Attacks Against VLM-based Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11683): Defines TrojanRobot, a supply-chain backdoor that inserts malicious modules into modular robotic policies and compromises the LLM-to-VLM manipulation pathway.
- [sMoRe: Enhancing Object Manipulation and Organization in Mixed Reality Spaces with LLMs and Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11752): Combines LLM command interpretation, spatial mapping, and text-to-3D generation to place and manage virtual objects in mixed-reality spaces.
- [Generative World Explorer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11844): Lets agents mentally explore unseen parts of an egocentric scene with generative world modeling, revising beliefs before physical navigation under partial observation.
- [GLOVER: Generalizable Open-Vocabulary Affordance Reasoning for Task-Oriented Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.12286): Fine-tunes LLMs to predict graspable part affordances in RGB feature space from open-vocabulary task specifications and human-object interaction data.
- [I Can Tell What I am Doing: Toward Real-World Natural Language Grounding of Robot Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.12960): Builds RONAR, an LLM system that turns multimodal robot logs into natural-language narratives for behavior announcement, failure analysis, and recovery.
- [AsymDex: Asymmetry and Relative Coordinates for RL-based Bimanual Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.13020): Assigns complementary hand roles and relative observation-action spaces so RL can learn asymmetric bimanual dexterity without demonstrations.
- [Bimanual Dexterity for Complex Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.13677): Builds a low-cost bimanual dexterous teleoperation setup with motion-capture gloves and teacher arms to collect high-quality data for complex tasks.
- [EasyHOI: Unleashing the Power of Large Models for Reconstructing Hand-Object Interactions in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14280): Off-axis but embodied-adjacent: reconstructs single-view hand-object interactions with segmentation, inpainting, and 3D foundation-model priors rather than training robot actions.
- [Aim My Robot: Precision Local Navigation to Any Object](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14770): Trains a local navigation system that uses multimodal perception and precise action prediction to reach objects at desired relative poses with centimeter-level accuracy.
- [BIP3D: Bridging 2D Images and 3D Perception for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14869): Builds an image-centric 3D perception model with pretrained 2D vision features, explicit 3D position encoding, and a spatial enhancer for embodied scene understanding.
- [WildLMa: Long Horizon Loco-Manipulation in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15131): Combines a traversability-aware whole-body controller, CLIP-conditioned visuomotor skill library, and LLM skill planner for long-horizon quadruped loco-manipulation.
- [FoAR: Force-Aware Reactive Policy for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15753): Fuses high-frequency force-torque sensing with vision through a future-contact predictor so reactive policies handle contact-rich manipulation.
- [TopV-Nav: Unlocking the Top-View Spatial Reasoning Potential of MLLM for Zero-shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16425): Prompts an MLLM to reason directly over top-view spatial maps, preserving geometry for zero-shot object navigation in unfamiliar scenes.
- [Online Episodic Memory Visual Query Localization with Egocentric Streaming Object Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16934): Defines online visual-query localization for egocentric streams and uses object discovery, tracking, and compact memory to answer queries without full video access.
- [g3D-LF: Generalizable 3D-Language Feature Fields for Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17030): Pretrains generalizable 3D-language feature fields from posed RGB-D views for novel-view prediction, BEV mapping, and language target queries in embodied tasks.
- [Prediction with Action: Visual Policy Learning via Joint Denoising Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18179): Shares a diffusion denoising process between future-image prediction and action generation so visual dynamics and robot control reinforce each other.
- [G3Flow: Generative 3D Semantic Flow for Pose-aware and Generalizable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18369): Combines 3D generative digital twins, vision-foundation semantic features, and pose tracking into a real-time object-centric semantic flow for manipulation.
- [DexHandDiff: Interaction-aware Diffusion Planning for Adaptive Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18562): Uses a dual-phase diffusion process over joint state-action dynamics, aligning contacts before generating goal-directed dexterous manipulation controls.
- [Lift3D Foundation Policy: Lifting 2D Large-Scale Pretrained Models for Robust 3D Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18623): Progressively augments 2D foundation models with implicit and explicit 3D robot representations to build robust 3D manipulation policies.
- [ForgerySleuth: Empowering Multimodal Large Language Models for Image Manipulation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19466): Off-axis for embodied/VLA: uses MLLMs for image-tamper clue fusion and segmentation, not robot manipulation or action-feedback policy learning.
- [GREAT: Geometry-Intention Collaborative Inference for Open-Vocabulary 3D Object Affordance Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19626): Grounds arbitrary-language 3D object affordances by jointly reasoning over invariant geometry and inferred interaction intentions.
- [CogACT: A Foundational Vision-Language-Action Model for Synergizing Cognition and Action in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19650): Introduces a componentized VLA architecture with a specialized action module and cognition-action routing, moving beyond simple action quantization for manipulation.
- [RoboMatrix: A Skill-centric Hierarchical Framework for Scalable Robot Task Planning and Execution in Open-World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00171): Uses a skill-centric hierarchy where an LLM scheduler decomposes tasks, a meta-skill layer composes reusable skills, and low-level policies execute open-world robot tasks.
- [SOLAMI: Social Vision-Language-Action Modeling for Immersive Interaction with 3D Autonomous Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00174): Builds a social VLA architecture for 3D autonomous characters, pairing multimodal interaction data with speech-and-motion action generation.
- [ARMOR: Egocentric Perception for Humanoid Robot Collision Avoidance and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00396): Combines wearable-like depth sensors with a transformer imitation policy trained from AMASS motions for humanoid dynamic collision avoidance.
- [Unleashing In-context Learning of Autoregressive Models for Few-shot Image Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01027): Off-axis for embodied VLA: an autoregressive image editor learns few-shot visual manipulation operations from text and examples, without a robot action loop.
- [OmniGuard: Hybrid Manipulation Localization via Augmented Versatile Deep Image Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01615): Off-axis for embodied VLA: hybrid image watermarking localizes AI edits and extracts copyright signals, but it is media-authenticity work rather than robot manipulation.
- [Dadu‐E: Rethinking the Role of Large Language Model in Robotic Computing Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01663): Builds a closed-loop robot planning pipeline around a lightweight LLM, encapsulated skills, feedback, and memory augmentation for embodied task execution.
- [Robot Learning with Super-Linear Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01770): Scales robot learning with a real-to-sim-to-real data flywheel that crowdsources digital twins, bootstraps simulation RL with demonstrations, and transfers policies to real robots.
- [DextrAH-RGB: Visuomotor Policies to Grasp Anything with Dexterous Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01791): Trains a privileged fabric-guided dexterous grasping policy with RL in simulation, then distills it into an RGB-only arm-hand visuomotor policy.
- [Planning-Guided Diffusion Policy Learning for Generalizable Contact-Rich Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02676): Generates planner demonstrations in high-fidelity physics simulation and behavior-clones a diffusion policy for contact-rich bimanual manipulation.
- [UniGraspTransformer: Simplified Policy Distillation for Scalable Dexterous Robotic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02699): Distills object-specific RL grasp trajectories into a universal Transformer policy for scalable dexterous robotic grasping across thousands of object poses.
- [RoboMD: Uncovering Robot Vulnerabilities through Semantic Potential Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02818): Learns a deep-RL vulnerability predictor over continuous vision-language semantic potential fields, letting manipulation policies be stress-tested in virtual variation space.
- [AffordDP: Generalizable Diffusion Policy with Transferable Affordance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03142): Conditions diffusion policy learning on transferable affordance priors that specify where and how to interact, improving manipulation generalization to unseen object categories.
- [Diffusion-VLA: Scaling Robot Foundation Models via Unified Diffusion and Autoregression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03293): Combines autoregressive next-token reasoning with a diffusion action decoder and reasoning-injection module for interpretable visuomotor policy generation.
- [Navigation World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03572): Trains a 1B-parameter Conditional Diffusion Transformer world model to predict future egocentric views from navigation actions and use simulated rollouts for planning.
- [Moto: Latent Motion Token as the Bridging Language for Learning Robot Manipulation from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04445): Learns latent motion tokens from videos for autoregressive pretraining, bridging action-free interaction videos and robot manipulation policies.
- [NaVILA: Legged Robot Vision-Language-Action Model for Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04453): Uses a two-level legged-robot VLA in which language-conditioned mid-level spatial actions drive a visual locomotion controller.
- [SAME: Learning Generic Language-Guided Visual Navigation with State-Adaptive Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05552): Uses a state-adaptive mixture-of-experts policy to share language-guided visual navigation across object search and instruction-following tasks.
- [AnyBimanual: Transferring Unimanual Policy for General Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06779): Transfers pretrained unimanual manipulation policies into a plug-and-play bimanual policy, reducing the need for expensive dual-arm demonstrations.
- [CARP: Visuomotor Policy Learning via Coarse-to-Fine Autoregressive Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06782): Replaces diffusion denoising with coarse-to-fine autoregressive action generation, using an action autoencoder for efficient visuomotor policy learning.
- [P3-PO: Prescriptive Point Priors for Visuo-Spatial Generalization of Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06784): Builds prescriptive point-prior state representations from vision and robot-learning cues so policies generalize across object instances and environments.
- [RoboTron-Mani: All-in-One Multimodal Large Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07215): Combines 3D-aware multimodal decoding, camera-parameter conditioning, occupancy supervision, and the RoboData dataset for robotic manipulation.
- [Mobile-TeleVision: Predictive Motion Priors for Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07773): Decouples humanoid upper-body manipulation from locomotion by conditioning RL lower-body control on CVAE predictive motion priors.
- [FLIP: Flow-Centric Generative Planning for General-Purpose Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08261): Plans manipulation in visual space with a flow-generation action proposal model, flow-conditioned video dynamics, and a vision-language value model.
- [Physical Informed Driving World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08410): Trains DrivePhysica as a physically constrained multi-view driving video world model for autonomous-driving perception data.
- [From Multimodal LLMs to Generalist Embodied Agents: Methods and Lessons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08442): Trains GEA with a multi-embodiment action tokenizer, cross-domain embodied data, and online RL for generalist embodied control.
- [Bootstrapping Language-Guided Navigation Learning with Self-Refining Data Flywheel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08467): Creates a self-refining data flywheel where a generator and navigator iteratively filter and regenerate instruction-trajectory pairs for language-guided navigation.
- [Multi-GraspLLM: A Multimodal LLM for Multi-Hand Semantic Guided Grasp Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08468): Aligns point-cloud and text features, emits grasp-bin tokens with an LLM, and maps them to grasp poses for multiple robotic hands.
- [Diffusion Predictive Control with Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09342): Injects model-based projections and constraint tightening into diffusion denoising, yielding predictive-control trajectories that satisfy novel state and action constraints.
- [GEAL: Generalizable 3D Affordance Learning with Cross-Modal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09511): Uses Gaussian splatting plus 2D-3D consistency alignment to transfer pretrained 2D semantics into robust 3D affordance grounding.
- [Should We Learn Contact-Rich Manipulation Policies From Sampling-Based Planners?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09743): Uses sampling-based planning and optimization to generate consistent contact-rich demonstrations, then trains a goal-conditioned diffusion behavior-cloning policy.
- [RLDG: Robotic Generalist Policy Distillation via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09858): Generates task-specific RL data for fine-tuning generalist robot policies, improving precise manipulation over human-demonstration distillation.
- [TraceVLA: Visual Trace Prompting Enhances Spatial-Temporal Awareness for Generalist Robotic Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10345): Finetunes OpenVLA with visual trace prompting over 150K trajectories so generalist policies see state-action traces for spatial-temporal action prediction.
- [Embodied CoT Distillation From LLM To Off-the-shelf Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11499): Distills LLM embodied chain-of-thought into small-language-model reasoning and planning policies, aided by an embodied knowledge graph.
- [Instruction-based Image Manipulation by Watching How Things Move](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12087): Off-axis for embodied VLA: constructs video-frame edit-instruction data for image manipulation, not robot-control action learning.
- [Efficient Diffusion Transformer Policies with Mixture of Expert Denoisers for Multitask Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12953): Scales diffusion imitation policies with sparse mixture-of-denoising experts, noise-conditioned routing, and expert caching for multitask robot control.
- [ExBody2: Advanced Expressive Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13196): Trains humanoid whole-body tracking controllers from human motion capture and simulated teacher data, then transfers the student policy to real robots.
- [What matters in building vision–language–action models for generalist robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14058): Ablates VLM backbones, policy architectures, and cross-embodiment data to identify VLA design choices for generalist robot manipulation.
- [GraphEQA: Using 3D Semantic Scene Graphs for Real-time Embodied Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14480): Grounds VLM question answering in online 3D metric-semantic scene graphs used as multimodal memory for embodied exploration and planning.
- [Dream to Manipulate: Compositional World Models Empowering Robot Imitation Learning with Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14957): Builds a compositional digital-twin world model with Gaussian splatting and physics simulation to imagine robot demonstrations for imitation learning.
- [Predictive Inverse Dynamics Models are Scalable Learners for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15109): Pretrains predictive inverse-dynamics Transformers that forecast visual states and actions from large robot datasets, then fine-tunes for manipulation.
- [STRAP: Robot Sub-Trajectory Retrieval for Augmented Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15182): Retrieves sub-trajectories with vision foundation features and dynamic time warping, then trains task-adapted policies at deployment time.
- [BODex: Scalable and Efficient Robotic Dexterous Grasp Synthesis Using Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.16490): Synthesizes dexterous grasps through bilevel optimization with GPU-accelerated quadratic programming, creating benchmark data for Shadow, Allegro, and Leap hands.
- [CoA-VLA: Improving Vision-Language-Action Models via Visual-Textual Chain-of-Affordance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.20451): Adds visual and textual chain-of-affordance prompts plus a co-injection module so VLAs reason about objects, grasps, placement, and motion paths before acting.
- [Diffgrasp: Whole-Body Grasping Synthesis Guided by Object Motion Using a Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.20657): Uses a diffusion model with contact-aware losses to synthesize coordinated whole-body and hand grasp motion from object trajectories.
- [UnrealZoo: Enriching Photo-Realistic Virtual Worlds for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.20977): Provides over 100 Unreal Engine virtual worlds, playable robot and human entities, and APIs for embodied data collection, RL training, and benchmarking.
- [Imagine Before Go: Self-Supervised Generative Map for Object Goal Navigation](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52733.2024.01553): Learns self-supervised generative maps that infer likely contextual objects from partial ObjectNav observations before choosing navigation actions.
- [Reactive human-robot collaborative manipulation of deformable linear objects using a new topological latent control model](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.rcim.2024.102727): Uses a topological latent control model for reactive human-robot collaboration on deformable linear objects, adapting manipulation to shape changes.
- [GAMMA: Generalizable Articulation Modeling and Manipulation for Articulated Objects](https://doi.org/10.1109/icra57147.2024.10610652): Jointly learns articulation models and grasp-pose affordances for diverse articulated objects, then adapts manipulation through iterative model correction.
- [General-Purpose Sim2Real Protocol for Learning Contact-Rich Manipulation With Marker-Based Visuotactile Sensors](https://doi.org/10.1109/tro.2024.3352969): Uses FEM tactile simulation, marker-coordinate tactile features, and self-supervised pretraining to transfer contact-rich peg-in-hole RL policies to real sensors.
- [Robotic Manipulation of Hand Tools: The Case of Screwdriving](https://doi.org/10.1109/icra57147.2024.10610831): Derives a force-control loop for robotic screwdriving by chaining screwdriver pose estimation, applied wrench estimation, and fingertip torque commands.
- [Domain-Generalized Robotic Picking via Contrastive Learning-Based 6-D Pose Estimation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tii.2024.3366248): Trains 6D pose estimation for robotic picking on augmented synthetic RGB and point-cloud data with contrastive learning to generalize to cluttered real scenes.
- [SAGE: Bridging Semantic and Actionable Parts for GEneralizable Articulated-Object Manipulation under Language Instructions](https://doi.org/10.15607/rss.2024.xx.016): Connects semantic parts to actionable parts for language-guided articulated-object manipulation, grounding instructions in the object part that should move.
- [LiDAR-Based End-to-End Active SLAM Using Deep Reinforcement Learning in Large-Scale Environments](https://doi.org/10.1109/tvt.2024.3405483): Trains a 3D LiDAR end-to-end active SLAM policy with deep RL over LiDAR scans and SLAM maps plus intrinsic rewards for large-scale exploration.
- [Semantics-Aware Receding Horizon Planner for Object-Centric Active Mapping](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2024.3371873): Uses multi-layer object-centric volumetric maps and semantic, geometric, and kinematic costs in a receding-horizon planner for active mapping.
- [Lang2LTL-2: Grounding Spatiotemporal Navigation Commands Using Large Language and Vision-Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros58592.2024.10802696): Grounds navigation commands into temporal-logic specifications using LLM and VLM modules so robots can execute long-horizon tasks with formal constraints.
- [Enhancing Vision and Language Navigation With Prompt-Based Scene Knowledge](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2024.3401451): Injects prompt-based scene knowledge about objects, directions, and room types into VLN policies to improve natural-language route following.
- [Learning to Design 3D Printable Adaptations on Everyday Objects for Robot Manipulation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icra57147.2024.10610268): Learns to design 3D-printable object adaptations that make everyday objects easier for robots to grasp, move, or otherwise manipulate.
- [TARS: Tactile Affordance in Robot Synesthesia for Dexterous Manipulation](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2024.3505783): Maps visual affordances into tactile expectations so dexterous policies can exploit tactile-like cues before physical contact.
- [Human 0, MLLM 1: Unlocking New Layers of Automation in Language-Conditioned Robotics with Multimodal LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1109/me61309.2024.10789747): Uses multimodal LLMs to automate language-conditioned robotics decisions; public metadata exposes limited mechanism detail.
- [OFSLMs: Offline Fine-Tuned Small Language Models Based on Hybrid Synthetic Knowledge for Robot Introspective Decision-Making](https://doi.org/10.1109/robio64047.2024.10907647): Fine-tunes small language models offline on hybrid synthetic knowledge for robot introspective decision-making.
- [SceneFun3D: Fine-Grained Functionality and Affordance Understanding in 3D Scenes](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52733.2024.01377): Labels 3D scene objects and parts with fine-grained functionality and affordance cues, supporting interaction-oriented 3D scene understanding.
- [Localization and Manipulation of Immoral Visual Cues for Safe Text-to-Image Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/wacv57701.2024.00461): Localizes morally unsafe visual cues in text-to-image outputs and edits those regions to reduce inappropriate content while preserving generation quality.
- [CondDG: Enhancing Autonomous Navigation in Adverse Weather with Conditional Diffusion Synthetic Data](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccr64365.2024.10927434): Generates adverse-weather synthetic data with conditional diffusion to improve autonomous-navigation perception under rain, fog, and other weather shifts.
- [Vision-language model-driven scene understanding and robotic object manipulation](https://doi.org/10.1109/case59546.2024.10711845): Uses VLM-driven scene understanding to parse language instructions, identify unknown objects, and support robotic manipulation in unfamiliar environments.
- [Language-Guided Dexterous Functional Grasping by LLM Generated Grasp Functionality and Synergy for Humanoid Manipulation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tase.2024.3524426): Uses an LLM to infer grasp functionality and hand synergies, then guides humanoid dexterous grasps from language instructions.
- [Visual–tactile learning of robotic cable-in-duct installation skills](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.autcon.2024.105905): Learns cable-in-duct installation from visual and tactile signals, targeting contact-rich construction manipulation with deformable cables.
- [Forgetting in Robotic Episodic Long-Term Memory](https://doi.org/10.1109/icra57147.2024.10610299): Studies forgetting in robotic episodic long-term memory to reduce unbounded working-memory transfer while preserving task-relevant retrieval.
- [Unconstrained Model Predictive Control for Robot Navigation under Uncertainty](https://doi.org/10.1109/icra57147.2024.10610531): Formulates robot navigation under uncertainty as unconstrained MPC with a closed-form collision-probability approximation for uncertainty propagation.
- [Location Reasoning of Target Objects Based on Human Common Sense and Robot Experiences](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcds.2024.3442862): Combines human common-sense object priors with robot experience to infer likely locations of target objects that are not directly observable.
- [Fly by Book: How to Train a Humanoid Robot to Fly an Airplane using Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros58592.2024.10802288): Uses LLMs to convert flight manuals, rules, and cockpit context into high-level procedures for training a humanoid robot to operate an aircraft cockpit.
- [Efficient Reinforcement Learning Method for Multi-Phase Robot Manipulation Skill Acquisition via Human Knowledge, Model-Based, and Model-Free Methods](https://doi.org/10.1109/tase.2024.3451296): Combines human knowledge, model-based learning, and model-free RL for multi-phase manipulation skills such as tight-tolerance peg-in-hole insertion.
- [Meta Reinforcement Learning of Locomotion Policy for Quadruped Robots With Motor Stuck](https://doi.org/10.1109/tase.2024.3424328): Uses meta reinforcement learning to adapt quadruped locomotion policies when one motor is stuck, improving fault-tolerant control.
- [Robust Dual-Filter Safety Control for Mobile Robots in Dynamic Multiobstacle Environments](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmech.2024.3521038): Combines RGB-D perception with dynamic control barrier functions in a dual-filter architecture for real-time mobile-robot obstacle avoidance.
- [A Dexterous and Compliant (DexCo) Hand Based on Soft Hydraulic Actuation for Human-Inspired Fine In-Hand Manipulation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tro.2024.3508932): Evidence-needed: Crossref confirms the IEEE T-RO title but exposes no abstract, so the soft-hydraulic dexterous-hand mechanism needs paper verification.
- [OV-HHIR: Open Vocabulary Human Interaction Recognition Using Cross-modal Integration of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00432): Off-axis for embodied VLA: recognizes human-human interactions with open-vocabulary LLM-generated descriptions, but lacks robot actions or embodied control.
- [An Immersive Virtual Reality Bimanual Telerobotic System With Haptic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00822): Builds an isomorphic bimanual teleoperation system with dual dexterous hands plus visual and haptic force feedback for complex robot operation.
- [Prior Lessons of Incremental Dialogue and Robot Action Management for the Age of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00953): Off-axis for Model cleanup: surveys incremental dialogue management requirements for human-robot interaction rather than introducing a robot policy or VLA.
- [Attribute-Based Robotic Grasping With Data-Efficient Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.02149): Pretrains a visual-text attribute embedding with gated attention and self-supervised object persistence, then adapts grasp affordances with one-grasp or adversarial updates.
- [OmniManip: Towards General Robotic Manipulation via Object-Centric Interaction Primitives as Spatial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.03841): Uses object-centric interaction primitives as spatial constraints for general robotic manipulation.
- [Learning to Transfer Human Hand Skills for Robot Manipulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.04169): Learns a shared motion manifold over human hand motion, robot actions, and object movement to transfer human hand skills to robot manipulation.
- [RoboPanoptes: The All-seeing Robot with Whole-body Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.05420): Combines whole-body vision with a visuomotor policy so robots can use distributed sensing and body contacts for dexterous manipulation.
- [Shake-VLA: Vision-Language-Action Model-Based System for Bimanual Robotic Manipulations and Liquid Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.06919): Integrates vision detection, speech recognition, RAG recipe retrieval, LLM instruction generation, force-torque liquid measurement, and anomaly handling for bimanual robot cocktail mixing.
- [FDPP: Fine-Tune Diffusion Policy with Human Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08259): Fine-tunes diffusion policies with learned human-preference rewards, aligning manipulation behavior while preserving task success.
- [DAViD: Modeling Dynamic Affordance of 3D Objects Using Pre-Trained Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08333): Learns 3D dynamic affordances from synthetic 4D human-object interaction samples generated with pretrained video diffusion models.
- [Social-LLaVA: Enhancing Robot Navigation through Human-Language Reasoning in Social Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.09024): Fine-tunes a VLM on the SNEI social-navigation VQA dataset so mobile robots can reason about perception, prediction, action, and explanation in crowds.
- [FAST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.09747): Introduces efficient action tokenization for VLA policies, compressing continuous robot actions for faster language-model decoding.
- [GeoManip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.09783): Uses geometric constraints as a training-free interface, deriving constraints from language and scene parsing to guide robot manipulation.
- [You Only Teach Once: Learn One-Shot Bimanual Robotic Manipulation from Video Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.14208): Learns one-shot bimanual manipulation from human demonstration videos, using video-conditioned policy learning to avoid task-specific robot demos.
- [SKIL: Semantic Keypoint Imitation Learning for Generalizable Data-efficient Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.14400): Uses vision foundation models to extract semantic keypoints and trains imitation policies on keypoint descriptors for data-efficient long-horizon manipulation.
- [An Atomic Skill Library Construction Method for Data-Efficient Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.15068): Builds motor-policy skill libraries for data-efficient embodied manipulation; it belongs in Model, not the repository Skill section.
- [Inductive biases for zero-shot systematic generalization in language-informed reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.15270): Adds modularity, sparsity, and memory feedback via Neural Production Systems to ground language in RL policies for BabyAI systematic generalization.
- [SpatialVLA: Exploring Spatial Representations for Visual-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.15830): Adds Ego3D position encoding and adaptive action grids to a VLA, representing spatial movement actions for cross-robot manipulation control.
- [Controllable Hand Grasp Generation for HOI and Efficient Evaluation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.15839): Off-axis for embodied VLA robotics: uses higher-order graph representations and 2D-conditioned diffusion to generate human hand-object grasp poses, not robot policies.
- [Improving Vision-Language-Action Model with Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.16664): Applies online RL to VLA models after supervised robot-data fine-tuning, addressing instability while improving interaction-time manipulation policies.
- [Mobile Robot Navigation Using Hand-Drawn Maps: A Vision Language Model Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00114): Uses selective visual association prompting and a predictive plan parser so VLMs can navigate from inaccurate hand-drawn topological maps.
- [Efficient Online Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00361): Reweights denoising score matching to train diffusion policies from online RL feedback without backpropagating through the full diffusion process.
- [Inference-Time Enhancement of Generative Robot Policies via Predictive World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00622): Adds Generative Predictive Control, using an action-conditioned world model at deployment to rank and refine frozen diffusion-policy action proposals.
- [Improving Transformer World Models for Data-Efficient RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01591): Improves transformer world-model RL with Dyna warmup, nearest-neighbor image-token codebooks, and block teacher forcing for data-efficient planning.
- [Flow-based Domain Randomization for Learning and Sequencing Robotic Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01800): Learns normalizing-flow domain-randomization distributions with entropy-regularized RL, then uses them for robust skill learning and uncertainty-aware planning.
- [From Foresight to Forethought: VLM-In-the-Loop Policy Steering via Latent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01828): Introduces FOREWARN, aligning latent predictions of low-level robot-action consequences with VLM-verifiable representations for runtime policy steering.
- [SPARK: Safe Protective and Assistive Robot Kit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03132): Off-axis for Model: provides a humanoid safety benchmark and toolbox with modular safe controllers and teleoperation interfaces rather than a learned VLA.
- [Action-Free Reasoning for Policy Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03729): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, inference strategy, or algorithmic capability for embodied and VLA systems.
- [Behavior-Regularized Diffusion Policy Optimization for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04778): Derives behavior-regularized offline RL for diffusion policies by computing KL regularization along reverse-time diffusion transitions.
- [ConRFT: A Reinforced Fine-tuning Method for VLA Models via Consistency Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05450): Uses consistency-policy reinforced fine-tuning to improve VLA robot policies, adding a post-training recipe for embodied action alignment.
- [Atom: Adaptive Theory-of-Mind-Based Human Motion Prediction in Long-Term Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05792): Models human internal beliefs with a game-theoretic theory-of-mind predictor and updates them via an Unscented Kalman Filter for robot navigation planning.
- [DexVLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05855): Adds a plug-in diffusion action expert and embodiment-aware training to a VLM, targeting long-horizon robot control across embodiments.
- [Habitizing Diffusion Planning for Efficient and Effective Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.06401): Distills slow diffusion planners into fast habitual decision models, reaching high-frequency action selection for offline RL control tasks.
- [Diffusion Suction Grasping with Large-Scale Parcel Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07238): Pairs Parcel-Suction-Dataset with a conditional diffusion model that turns point-cloud observations into suction grasp score maps for cluttered parcels.
- [Robotic In-Hand Manipulation for Large-Range Precise Object Movement: The RGMC Champion Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07472): Uses kinematic trajectory optimization for in-grasp object movement, achieving large-range precise in-hand manipulation without pretraining or object geometry.
- [DMWM: Dual-Mind World Model with Long-Term Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07591): Combines an RSSM world model with a logic-integrated System-2 network and feedback so long-horizon imagination follows environment rules.
- [Key-Step Human Skill Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08234): Defines key-step skill generation and learns human skill generators for multi-step embodied behavior videos, supplying action-generation modeling rather than robot control.
- [CordViP: Correspondence-based Visuomotor Policy for Dexterous Manipulation in Real-World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08449): Builds interaction-aware point clouds from object 6D pose estimates and robot proprioception so dexterous policies can learn hand-object spatial correspondences.
- [Robot Data Curation with Mutual Information Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08623): Uses mutual-information estimators for robot data curation, relevant to data-centric VLA model training.
- [DexTrack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.09614): Curates robot tracking trajectories from human-object references and trains a neural controller for generalizable dexterous manipulation.
- [Video2Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.09886): Reconstructs manipulation tasks from internet RGB videos, uses LLM-generated rewards in simulation, and transfers RL-trained skills to robots.
- [Manual2Skill: Learning to Read Manuals and Acquire Robotic Skills for Furniture Assembly Using Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10090): Learns robotic assembly skills from manuals with VLMs, connecting document understanding to embodied policy acquisition.
- [USER-VLM 360: Personalized Vision Language Models with User-aware Tuning for Social Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10636): Adds user-aware tuning, bias-aware preference optimization, and 360-degree socio-emotive datasets for personalized VLMs in social robot interaction.
- [REGNav: Room Expert Guided Image-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10785): Pretrains an unsupervised room expert and fuses room-style predictions with image-goal navigation policies to reduce cross-room wandering.
- [AdaManip: Adaptive Articulated Object Manipulation Environments and Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11124): Builds adaptive articulated-object environments and trains a 3D visual diffusion imitation policy from trial-and-error demonstrations.
- [Maximum Entropy Reinforcement Learning with Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11612): Uses a diffusion policy as the multimodal action distribution for maximum-entropy RL, improving exploration beyond Gaussian SAC policies.
- [VLP: Vision-Language Preference Learning for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11918): Learns a vision-language preference model and synthetic preference dataset to provide reward feedback for language-conditioned manipulation.
- [IMLE Policy: Fast and Sample Efficient Visuomotor Policy Learning via Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12371): Uses implicit maximum likelihood behavior cloning for single-step visuomotor action generation, improving low-data imitation speed versus diffusion policies.
- [Pre-training Auto-regressive Robotic Models with 4D Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.13142): Robotic auto-regressive pre-training method using 4D representations.
- [Generative Predictive Control: Flow Matching Policies for Dynamic and Difficult-to-Demonstrate Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.13406): Uses simulation-supervised generative predictive control to train flow-matching robot policies for fast dynamics, then warm-starts inference so action sequences stay temporally consistent under high-frequency feedback.
- [VLAS: Vision-Language-Action Model With Speech Instructions For Customized Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.13508): Adds speech instructions to VLA models for customized manipulation, broadening embodied control beyond text-only commands.
- [ChatVLA: Unified Multimodal Understanding and Robot Control with Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14420): Unifies multimodal understanding and robot control in a vision-language-action model.
- [Humanoid-VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14795): Aligns language, egocentric vision, and whole-body motion control so humanoid robots can interact with objects and explore environments under natural-language goals.
- [DDAT: Diffusion Policies Enforcing Dynamically Admissible Robot Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15043): Projects diffusion trajectory predictions onto reachable-set manifolds during training and inference, producing dynamically admissible one-shot robot motion for quadcopter, MuJoCo, and Unitree experiments.
- [Learning Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation Skills via Privileged Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15442): Uses simulation-only relaxed constraints and virtual-force privileged actions with curriculum RL so long-horizon contact-rich manipulation policies can explore sparse-reward tasks and transfer to real robots.
- [COMPASS: Cross-embodiment Mobility Policy via Residual RL and Skill Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16372): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for embodied and VLA agents. Core idea: focus on cross-embodiment mobility policy via residual rl and skill synthesis as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [A low-cost and lightweight 6 DoF bimanual arm for dynamic and contact-rich manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16908): Builds ARMADA, a low-inertia 6-DoF bimanual robot, and demonstrates sim-to-real RL plus human motion shadowing for dynamic contact-rich tasks.
- [DemoGen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16932): Turns a single demonstration into spatially augmented synthetic demonstrations, improving data-efficient visuomotor policy learning across manipulation configurations.
- [SET-PAiREd: Designing for Parental Involvement in Learning with an AI-Assisted Educational Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17623): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, inference strategy, or algorithmic capability for embodied and VLA systems.
- [Task Graph Maximum Likelihood Estimation for Procedural Activity Understanding in Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17753): Learns procedural task graphs from egocentric video by maximizing step-transition likelihood, making the entry closer to embodied activity modeling than to robot control.
- [$\texttt{SPIN}$: distilling $\texttt{Skill-RRT}$ for long-horizon prehensile and non-prehensile manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18015): Distills Skill-RRT demonstrations with applicability checks, intermediate object-pose sampling, and connector policies into a long-horizon manipulation policy.
- [Enhancing Reusability of Learned Skills for Robot Manipulation via Gaze and Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18121): Learns reusable manipulation motions from gaze-annotated demonstrations, using gaze targets and motion bottlenecks so imitation policies generalize when object and end-effector poses shift.
- [Attention-Guided Integration of CLIP and SAM for Precise Object Masking in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18842): Adds a CLIP-and-SAM integration method for robotic manipulation perception.
- [Hi Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.19417): Uses hierarchical VLM reasoning to interpret complex prompts and feedback, then delegates the selected next step to low-level robot actions across three platforms.
- [Efficient Reinforcement Learning by Guiding Generalist World Models with Non-Curated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.19544): Uses experience rehearsal and execution guidance so reward-free, mixed-quality, cross-embodiment offline data can accelerate online RL through world models.
- [Sensor-Invariant Tactile Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.19638): Learns transformer-based tactile representations that transfer across optical tactile sensors, supporting embodied manipulation beyond vision-only policies.
- [Multi-Keypoint Affordance Representation for Functional Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20018): Represents functional dexterous grasping with multiple affordance keypoints so grasp posture is constrained beyond coarse contact regions.
- [3D-AffordanceLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20041): Uses LLMs for open-vocabulary affordance detection in 3D worlds, connecting spatial perception with embodied action choices.
- [HiFAR: Multi-Stage Curriculum Learning for High-Dynamics Humanoid Fall Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20061): Trains humanoid fall recovery with a multi-stage curriculum for high-dynamics recovery in dynamic and unstructured environments.
- [InterMimic: Towards Universal Whole-Body Control for Physics-Based Human-Object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20390): Learns one whole-body human-object interaction policy from imperfect MoCap by first training subject-specific teacher policies and then distilling them into a student controller.
- [Point Policy: Unifying Observations and Actions with Key Points for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20391): Trains robot policies from offline human demonstration videos by converting human hand poses and object states into semantic keypoints that unify observations and robot actions.
- [Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Vision-Based Dexterous Manipulation on Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20396): Provides a practical reinforcement-learning recipe for vision-based, contact-rich bimanual manipulation on humanoid robots.
- [RoboBrain: A Unified Brain Model for Robotic Manipulation from Abstract to Concrete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.21257): Builds a unified robotic brain model for manipulation from abstract planning to concrete action.
- [Do Emotions Really Affect Argument Convincingness? A Dynamic Approach with LLM-based Manipulation Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00024): Uses LLM-based manipulation checks to test how perceived emotional intensity changes argument convincingness across languages, domains, and models.
- [Flying on Point Clouds with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00496): Trains a 50 Hz sim-to-real quadrotor RL policy from lightweight 3D LiDAR point-cloud surrogates for low-latency obstacle avoidance in clutter.
- [Never too Prim to Swim: An LLM-Enhanced RL-based Adaptive S-Surface Controller for AUVs under Extreme Sea Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00527): Combines an LLM-enhanced adaptive S-surface controller with reinforcement learning for autonomous underwater vehicle control under extreme sea disturbances.
- [Space-Time Graphs of Convex Sets for Multi-Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00583): Plans multi-robot motion by reserving trajectories as spatiotemporal obstacles in graphs of convex sets, improving collision-free continuous planning.
- [General Force Sensation for Tactile Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01058): Studies general force sensation for tactile robots.
- [KineSoft: Learning Proprioceptive Manipulation Policies with Soft Robot Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01078): Combines strain-sensor shape estimation with a shape-conditioned low-level controller, grounding diffusion imitation policies for kinesthetically taught soft robot hands.
- [FABG : End-to-end Imitation Learning for Embodied Affective Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01363): Collects VR demonstrations whose operator view and motions match a 25-DoF humanoid, then trains an end-to-end policy with latency compensation for facial affect, gaze, and gesture behaviors.
- [AVR: Active Vision-Driven Precise Robot Manipulation with Viewpoint and Focal Length Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01439): Adds head-tracked gimbal viewpoint control and optical zoom to teleoperation and RoboTwin-style demonstrations, improving imitation policies for precise bimanual manipulation under occlusion and clutter.
- [TactStyle: Generating Tactile Textures with Generative AI for Digital Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02007): Fine-tunes an image-generation model to produce tactile heightfields for 3D-printable textures, then evaluates perceived haptic qualities.
- [Natural Selection via Foundation Models for Soft Robot Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02249): Uses foundation models as selection signals in soft-robot evolution, linking generative priors with morphology search.
- [PD-VLA: Accelerating Vision-Language-Action Model Integrated with Action Chunking via Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02310): Parallel-decoding method for accelerating VLA action chunking.
- [ROCKET-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02505): Steers visuomotor policies through cross-view goal alignment for embodied action control.
- [Bridging VLM and KMP: Enabling Fine-grained robotic manipulation via Semantic Keypoints Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02748): Transfers VLM decisions into Kernelized Movement Primitives through semantic keypoint constraints for fine-grained manipulation under ambiguity.
- [Reactive Diffusion Policy: Slow-Fast Visual-Tactile Policy Learning for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02881): Pairs the TactAR tactile-feedback teleoperation system with a slow-fast visual-tactile diffusion policy so contact-rich manipulation can react during action-chunk execution.
- [SafeVLA: Towards Safety Alignment of Vision-Language-Action Model via Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.03480): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, inference strategy, or algorithmic capability for embodied and VLA systems.
- [Curating Demonstrations using Online Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.03707): Lets robots self-curate heterogeneous demonstrations by training a rollout-success classifier from online experience, filtering low-quality strategies before final imitation learning.
- [CREStE: Scalable Mapless Navigation with Internet Scale Priors and Counterfactual Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.03921): Distills visual foundation models into open-set BEV perception and uses counterfactual IRL from trajectory demonstrations to infer navigation costs for kilometer-scale mapless outdoor driving.
- [Robotic Compliant Object Prying Using Diffusion Policy Guided by Vision and Force Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.03998): Fuses force observations with images inside a diffusion policy for battery-prying, preserving contact feedback that a vision-only policy misses in contact-rich disassembly.
- [VLA Model-Expert Collaboration for Bi-directional Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04163): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for embodied and VLA agents. Core idea: focus on for bi-directional manipulation learning as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [SRSA: Skill Retrieval and Adaptation for Robotic Assembly Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04538): Retrieves assembly skills by predicting zero-shot transfer success from object geometry, dynamics, and expert actions, then fine-tunes the selected policy with fewer transition samples.
- [Object-Centric World Model for Language-Guided Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06170): Uses slot-attention object-centric representations and language conditioning to predict future manipulation states in a compact planning space.
- [How to Train Your Robots? The Impact of Demonstration Modality on Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07017): Compares kinesthetic, VR-controller, and spacemouse demonstrations for tabletop imitation learning and proposes mixing a few clean kinesthetic demos with teleoperation data.
- [VidBot: Learning Generalizable 3D Actions from In-the-Wild 2D Human Videos for Zero-Shot Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07135): Recovers metric 3D hand trajectories and affordance representations from monocular human videos, then transfers them through a coarse-to-fine zero-shot robot manipulation policy.
- [AffordDexGrasp: Open-Set Language-Guided Dexterous Grasp With Generalizable-Instructive Affordance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07360): Introduces a generalizable-instructive affordance representation that bridges language intent and low-level dexterous grasp generation for unseen object categories.
- [AutoSpatial: Visual-Language Reasoning for Social Robot Navigation through Efficient Spatial Reasoning Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07557): Trains structured spatial reasoning for social robot navigation with VQA auto-labeling.
- [MoRE: Unlocking Scalability in Reinforcement Learning for Quadruped Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08007): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, inference strategy, or algorithmic capability for embodied and VLA systems.
- [DexGrasp Anything: Towards Universal Robotic Dexterous Grasping with Physics Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08257): Injects physical constraints into both diffusion-model training and sampling, backed by a 3.4M-pose dexterous grasp dataset over more than 15K objects.
- [Reasoning in Visual Navigation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08306): analyzes visual-navigation reasoning in end-to-end trained agents through a dynamical-systems lens.
- [Trinity: A Modular Humanoid Robot AI System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08338): Organizes humanoid autonomy as modular perception, planning, and control components, connecting language-level task reasoning with whole-body locomotion and manipulation execution.
- [LiPS: Large-Scale Humanoid Robot Reinforcement Learning with Parallel-Series Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08349): Supports large-scale humanoid RL by modeling parallel-series mechanical structures during training rather than deferring conversion until sim-to-real transfer.
- [MoE-Loco: Mixture of Experts for Multitask Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08564): Uses a mixture-of-experts policy to reduce multitask gradient conflict and specialize locomotion behaviors across terrains and quadruped or biped gaits.
- [Cross-Embodiment Robotic Manipulation Synthesis via Guided Demonstrations through CycleVAE and Human Behavior Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08622): Aligns human and robot motion latent spaces with CycleVAE and uses a human behavior transformer to synthesize robot manipulation trajectories from human demonstrations.
- [Disentangled World Models: Learning to Transfer Semantic Knowledge from Distracting Videos for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08751): Pretrains an action-free video prediction model, distills its disentangled latent semantics into an action-and-reward world model, and fine-tunes it for visual RL.
- [SICNav-Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08858): Couples diffusion-based crowd-trajectory prediction with bilevel model-predictive control for safe, interaction-aware robot navigation.
- [FP3: A 3D Foundation Policy for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08950): Introduces a 3D foundation policy for manipulation, making 3D spatial policy representation a model-side embodied capability.
- [Natural Humanoid Robot Locomotion with Generative Motion Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09015): Retargets human motions to a humanoid, trains a conditional VAE motion prior, and uses it as frozen trajectory-level supervision for natural locomotion policy learning.
- [Sequential Multi-Object Grasping with One Dexterous Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09078): Synthesizes simulator-validated single-object grasps over subsets of Allegro Hand links, merges them into multi-object configurations, and trains a point-cloud-conditioned diffusion grasp model.
- [GarmentPile: Point-Level Visual Affordance Guided Retrieval and Adaptation for Cluttered Garments Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09243): Learns dense point-level garment affordances and uses an affordance-guided adaptation module to reorganize entangled piles into states that a retrieval policy can manipulate.
- [CombatVLA: An Efficient Vision-Language-Action Model for Combat Tasks in 3D Action Role-Playing Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09527): Vision-language-action model for real-time 3D game action control, fitting embodied action-model coverage.
- [Adaptive Anomaly Recovery for Telemanipulation: A Diffusion Model Approach to Vision-Based Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09632): Detects anomalous telemanipulation video segments with frame differences and reconstructs occluded or corrupted clips using diffusion models to stabilize vision-based command tracking.
- [NuExo: A Wearable Exoskeleton Covering all Upper Limb ROM for Outdoor Data Collection and Teleoperation of Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10554): Builds a wearable exoskeleton that covers full upper-limb motion and collects multimodal teleoperation data for humanoid robots outdoors.
- [HybridVLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10631): Integrates autoregressive and diffusion action prediction in one VLA model, using collaborative training and action ensembling for robot manipulation.
- [Spatial-Temporal Graph Diffusion Policy with Kinematic Modeling for Bimanual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10743): Uses spatial-temporal graph diffusion policies for bimanual robotic manipulation.
- [Prof. Robot: Differentiable Robot Rendering Without Static and Self-Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11269): Adds collision-aware differentiable robot rendering through a neural collision classifier and Eikonal regularization, improving image-supervised robotic action optimization.
- [TASTE-Rob: Advancing Video Generation of Task-Oriented Hand-Object Interaction for Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11423): Model candidate for embodied and VLA agents: Generates task-oriented hand-object interaction videos for robot manipulation.
- [Sketch-to-Skill: Bootstrapping Robot Learning with Human Drawn Trajectory Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11918): Translates human-drawn 2D trajectory sketches into 3D guidance for robot manipulation RL, adding a low-demonstration supervision route for embodied policy learning.
- [Diffusion Dynamics Models with Generative State Estimation for Cloth Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11999): Uses transformer diffusion models for full cloth-state reconstruction from partial observations and for action-conditioned dynamics prediction inside MPC-based cloth folding.
- [D4orm: Multi-Robot Trajectories with Dynamics-aware Diffusion Denoised Deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12204): Optimizes multi-robot trajectories by diffusion-style denoising over rollout-feasible deformations guided by robot dynamics and a fitness function, enabling zero-shot multirotor deployment.
- [DART: Dual-level Autonomous Robotic Topology for Efficient Exploration in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12782): Builds low- and high-level topological graphs of convex regions and uses local artificial potential fields for efficient unknown-environment exploration.
- [LIVEPOINT: Fully Decentralized, Safe, Deadlock-Free Multi-Robot Control in Cluttered Environments with High-Dimensional Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13098): Synthesizes universal control barrier functions over point clouds for decentralized multi-robot navigation with collision and deadlock avoidance.
- [VARP: Reinforcement Learning from Vision-Language Model Feedback with Agent Regularized Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13817): Uses VLM preference feedback plus agent-regularized preference learning for continuous-control robot policies, reducing reliance on hand-crafted rewards.
- [FlexVLN: Flexible Adaptation for Diverse Vision-and-Language Navigation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13966): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, inference strategy, or algorithmic capability for embodied and VLA systems.
- [PANDORA: Diffusion Policy Learning for Dexterous Robotic Piano Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14545): Learns high-dimensional piano-playing actions with a FiLM-conditioned diffusion U-Net, LLM semantic reward feedback, audio-fidelity rewards, and residual inverse-kinematics refinement.
- [GR00T N1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14734): An open foundation model for generalist humanoid robots, extending VLA-style training to humanoid manipulation and whole-body action settings.
- [A Unified Framework for Real-Time Failure Handling in Robotics Using Vision-Language Models, Reactive Planner and Behavior Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15202): Combines VLM scene graphs, a reactive planner, and behavior trees to verify tasks before execution and repair robot failures online.
