- [Geometrically-Aware One-Shot Skill Transfer of Category-Level Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15371): Transfers object manipulation from one human demonstration by mapping object-centric interaction functions across shapes with functional maps and ScLERP paths.
- [JARVIS-VLA: Post-Training Large-Scale Vision Language Models to Play Visual Games with Keyboards and Mouse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16365): Post-trains large vision-language models to play visual games with keyboard and mouse actions, extending VLA-style control to interactive game environments.
- [Do Visual Imaginations Improve Vision-and-Language Navigation Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16394): Uses text-to-image subgoal imaginations as navigation cues and auxiliary supervision for VLN agents.
- [World Knowledge from AI Image Generation for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16579): Uses images synthesized for a robot's current environment to infer plausible object arrangements and fill in underspecified control decisions.
- [DyWA: Dynamics-Adaptive World Action Model for Generalizable Non-Prehensile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16806): Builds a dynamics-adaptive world action model that predicts future states while adapting from trajectory history to mass and friction changes in non-prehensile manipulation.
- [Physical Plausibility-aware Trajectory Prediction via Locomotion Embodiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17267): Regularizes human trajectory prediction with a differentiable locomotion-value proxy and filters physically implausible predictions at inference.
- [LLM+MAP: Bimanual Robot Task Planning using Large Language Models and Planning Domain Definition Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17309): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, inference strategy, or algorithmic capability for embodied and VLA systems.
- [PRIMAL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17544): Learns a physically reactive generative motor model for interactive avatars, supporting real-time impulse response and target-reaching adaptation.
- [Decentralized Navigation of a Cable-Towed Load using Quadrupedal Robot Team via MARL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18221): Trains decentralized multi-agent RL planners for quadruped teams that tow a cable-connected load through clutter while handling taut and slack states.
- [RoboEngine: Plug-and-Play Robot Data Augmentation with Semantic Robot Segmentation and Background Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18738): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, inference strategy, or algorithmic capability for embodied and VLA systems.
- [Dita: Scaling Diffusion Transformer for Generalist Vision-Language-Action Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19757): Diffusion-transformer VLA policy model; generated Survey label was a false positive.
- [Body Discovery of Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19941): Defines a body-discovery challenge for recognizing embodiments and neural signal functions, evaluated with causal inference algorithms in simulated environments.
- [Robust Deep Reinforcement Learning in Robotics via Adaptive Gradient-Masked Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20844): Uses gradient-masked adversarial attacks to identify critical state dimensions in DRL robot controllers and train more robust policies.
- [Modeling Multiple Normal Action Representations for Error Detection in Procedural Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22405): Predicts multiple valid next procedural actions and reconstructs normal action representations so AR-assisted or robotic systems can detect execution errors.
- [Empirical Analysis of Sim-and-Real Cotraining of Diffusion Policies For Planar Pushing from Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22634): Studies diffusion-policy cotraining on simulated and real demonstrations, isolating how simulated-data scale, real-data ceilings, and physical domain gaps affect planar pushing.
- [Event Camera Meets Mobile Embodied Perception: Abstraction, Algorithm, Acceleration, Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22943): Surveys event-camera mobile embodied perception from 2014 to 2025, covering event abstraction, algorithms, acceleration, and applications such as odometry and 3D reconstruction.
- [Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation with Uncertainty Alignment for Robotic Scene Understanding in Indoor Building Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23105): Combines VLM and LLM open-vocabulary scene segmentation with Segment-Detect-Select reasoning so assistive robots can map rooms and functional areas from casual human instructions.
- [ZeroMimic: Distilling Robotic Manipulation Skills from Web Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23877): Distills robotic manipulation from web videos, adding a data-scaling route for VLA and imitation-learning models.
- [Sim-and-Real Co-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.24361): Studies how to mix simulation and real data for vision-based robotic manipulation across robot-arm and humanoid settings.
- [Slot-Level Robotic Placement via Visual Imitation from Single Human Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.01959): Proposes SLeRP, combining visual foundation models with Slot-Net to identify pick objects, placement slots, and relative poses from a single human demonstration video.
- [Unified World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02792): Couples action and video diffusion in a unified transformer so pretraining can use robot action data and action-free videos for policy and world modeling.
- [AuDeRe: Automated Strategy Decision and Realization in Robot Planning and Control via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.03015): Lets an LLM select planning and control APIs from task descriptions and performance feedback instead of directly generating trajectories or code.
- [GROVE: A Generalized Reward for Learning Open-Vocabulary Physical Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04191): Learns a generalized reward for open-vocabulary physical skills, giving embodied policies reusable reward supervision across physical tasks.
- [Tool-as-Interface: Learning Robot Policies from Human Tool Usage through Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04612): Learns robot tool-use policies from human video through imitation learning across embodiment gaps.
- [Rewind Egocentric Motion Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04956): generates real-time egocentric whole-body motion with exemplar-based identity conditioning.
- [Wavelet Policy: Imitation Learning in the Scale Domain with World Prior Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04991): Uses imitation learning in the scale domain with world-prior memory for embodied policies.
- [RobustDexGrasp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05287): trains zero-shot dynamic dexterous grasping policies for general objects with teacher-policy and curriculum learning.
- [MAPLE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06084): Encodes dexterous robotic-manipulation priors learned from egocentric videos for embodied policy learning.
- [ViTaMIn: Learning Contact-Rich Tasks Through Robot-Free Visuo-Tactile Manipulation Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06156): Collects contact-rich demonstrations with a handheld visuo-tactile gripper and pretrains tactile representations to improve policy data efficiency and robustness.
- [Two by Two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06961): Learns generalizable robot assembly across daily object pairs, connecting geometric alignment and functional relations in manipulation policies.
- [Fast Adaptation with Behavioral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07896): Improves zero-shot behavioral foundation models with a few online interaction steps, adapting the inferred task embedding and policy for reward-specified downstream control.
- [CATCH-FORM-ACTer: Compliance-Aware Tactile Control and Hybrid Deformation Regulation-Based Action Transformer for Viscoelastic Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08232): Augments compliance-aware tactile control for viscoelastic object manipulation with action chunking transformers learned from teleoperation sequences.
- [Ego4o](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08449): captures and understands egocentric human motion from multimodal input, giving embodied models richer first-person motion supervision.
- [Endowing Embodied Agents with Spatial Reasoning Capabilities for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08806): Model candidate for embodied and VLA agents: Adds spatial reasoning capabilities to embodied vision-and-language navigation agents.
- [Development of a PPO-Reinforcement Learned Walking Tripedal Soft-Legged Robot using SOFA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09242): Trains a PPO walking controller for a tripedal soft-legged robot in SOFA simulation and evaluates sequential goal-reaching navigation.
- [Does Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10041): uses denoising diffusion bridge models for visual navigation.
- [Flying Hand: End-Effector-Centric Framework for Versatile Aerial Manipulation Teleoperation and Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10334): Uses an end-effector-centric aerial manipulation interface with whole-body MPC and high-level policy learning to decouple platform control from task decisions.
- [ZeroGrasp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10857): Uses zero-shot shape reconstruction to enable robotic grasping, linking spatial reconstruction with embodied manipulation.
- [Zero-Shot Whole-Body Humanoid Control via Behavioral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11054): Regularizes unsupervised RL with unlabeled behavior trajectories through forward-backward representations and conditional-policy imitation for zero-shot humanoid control.
- [3DAffordSplat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11218): Uses 3D Gaussian representations and affordance supervision to reason about task-relevant functional regions for embodied manipulation.
- [LangTraj](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11521): combines a diffusion model and dataset for language-conditioned trajectory simulation.
- [Crossing the Human-Robot Embodiment Gap with Sim-to-Real RL using One Human Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12609): Sim-to-real RL method for crossing the human-robot embodiment gap from one human demonstration.
- [Krysalis Hand: A Lightweight, High-Payload, 18-DoF Anthropomorphic End-Effector for Robotic Learning and Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12967): Builds an 18-DoF anthropomorphic hand with self-locking joints and high passive payload for dexterous learning and teleoperation.
- [Modality Selection and Skill Segmentation via Cross-Modality Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14573): Applies cross-modality attention to select informative tactile, audio, and visual streams at each timestep and segment primitive skills for hierarchical contact-rich policies.
- [Solving New Tasks by Adapting Internet Video Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15369): Studies how to adapt internet-pretrained video generative models with in-domain robot videos so text-conditioned video priors can supervise novel robot tasks.
- [pi0.5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.16054): Extends VLA models toward open-world generalization for practical real-world robot tasks outside narrow lab settings.
- [ForesightNav: Learning Scene Imagination for Efficient Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.16062): Learns scene-imagination modules that predict unseen occupancy and semantic context so embodied navigation agents can choose better long-term exploration goals.
- [Latent Diffusion Planning for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.16925): Uses latent diffusion for imitation-learning planning, connecting generative trajectory modeling with embodied policy learning.
- [Cracking the Code of Action: a Generative Approach to Affordances for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.17282): Learns affordances as generative action knowledge for reinforcement learning.
- [RSRNav: Reasoning Spatial Relationship for Image-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.17991): Builds cross-correlations between image-goal and egocentric observations, then refines them with fine-grained and direction-aware correlation before the ImageNav action policy.
- [STDArm: Transferring Visuomotor Policies From Static Data Training to Dynamic Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.18792): Transfers static-data visuomotor policies to moving platforms with a real-time action manager, lightweight disturbance predictor, and online latency estimator.
- [Dexonomy: Synthesizing All Dexterous Grasp Types in a Grasp Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.18829): Synthesizes contact-rich, penetration-free grasps for any taxonomy grasp type by fitting objects to one human template per hand and grasp type, then refining hand poses in simulation.
- [PRISM-DP: Spatial Pose-based Observations for Diffusion-Policies via Segmentation, Mesh Generation, and Pose Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20359): Converts open-set object segmentation, mesh generation, and pose tracking into compact spatial pose observations for smaller diffusion policies.
- [LangWBC: Language-directed Humanoid Whole-Body Control via End-to-end Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.21738): Trains an end-to-end language-directed humanoid whole-body policy with reinforcement learning, distillation, and CVAE motion diversity.
- [DeCo: Task Decomposition and Skill Composition for Zero-Shot Generalization in Long-Horizon 3D Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.00527): Decomposes demonstrations into gripper-object atomic skills, then uses a VLM to parse instructions, retrieve skills, and spatially chain them for long-horizon manipulation.
- [Physics-Conditioned Grasping for Stable Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01399): Selects tool grasps by predicting interaction wrench along task-conditioned trajectories, using torque, slip, and alignment costs in SDG-Net for stable hammering, sweeping, and reaching.
- [SkillMimic-V2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02094): Uses trajectory stitching and transition-field augmentation to train robust interaction policies from sparse, noisy demonstrations.
- [Interleave-VLA: Enhancing Robot Manipulation with Interleaved Image-Text Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02152): Improves robot manipulation with interleaved image-text instructions, extending VLA training beyond simple language-only commands.
- [Prompt-Responsive Object Retrieval with Memory-Augmented Student-Teacher Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02232): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [GraspVLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03233): Pretrains a grasping VLA on SynGrasp-1B synthetic action data, combining autoregressive perception and flow-matching action generation for open-vocabulary grasping.
- [OpenHelix: A Short Survey, Empirical Analysis, and Open-Source Dual-System VLA Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03912): Compares dual-system VLA design choices through empirical ablations and releases a low-cost open-source dual-system model for robotic manipulation.
- [PARC: Physics-based Augmentation with Reinforcement Learning for Character Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04002): Uses physics-based augmentation with reinforcement learning for character controllers, adding a simulation-grounded embodied policy route.
- [SToLa: Self-Adaptive Touch-Language Framework with Tactile Commonsense Reasoning in Open-Ended Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04201): Introduces a self-adaptive touch-language MoE framework with tactile commonsense reasoning for open-ended scenarios.
- [Model-Based AI planning and Execution Systems for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04493): Frames robot execution around explicit model-based planning, where task plans are checked against environment and robot-state models before low-level controllers act.
- [Merging and Disentangling Views in Visual Reinforcement Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04619): Merges multi-view visual features for sample-efficient Q-learning while disentangling single-view features so manipulation policies remain robust to camera failures.
- [Morphologically Symmetric Reinforcement Learning for Ambidextrous Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.05287): Uses bilateral symmetry and equivariant networks to train per-hand bimanual manipulation policies, then distills them into an ambidextrous global policy.
- [Learning to Drive Anywhere With Model-Based Reannotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.05592): Uses model-based reannotation to turn passive teleoperation and web-video data into action labels for LogoNav, a long-horizon embodied navigation policy.
- [Let Humanoids Hike! Integrative Skill Development on Complex Trails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06218): Trains vision-equipped humanoids to hike by combining temporal vision-transformer local-goal anticipation with latent joint-pattern transfer from privileged learning.
- [DAPPER: Discriminability-Aware Policy-to-Policy Preference-Based Reinforcement Learning for Query-Efficient Robot Skill Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06357): Improves preference-based RL for robot skills by comparing trajectories from multiple policies and sampling queries with high learned preference discriminability.
- [LLM-Flock: Decentralized Multi-Robot Flocking via Large Language Models and Influence-Based Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06513): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for embodied and VLA agents. Core idea: focus on decentralized multi-robot flocking via large language models and influence-based consensus as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [FALCON: Learning Force-Adaptive Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06776): Learns force-adaptive humanoid loco-manipulation with coordinated lower-body locomotion and upper-body end-effector control under force curricula.
- [UniDiffGrasp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06832): Integrates VLM reasoning with part-level diffusion to support open-vocabulary constrained dual-arm grasping.
- [X-Sim: Cross-Embodiment Learning via Real-to-Sim-to-Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07096): Reconstructs simulation from human RGB-D videos, trains RL policies with object-motion rewards, distills them into image-conditioned diffusion policies, and adapts online to real observations.
- [Learning to Reason and Navigate: Parameter Efficient Action Planning with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07500): Fine-tunes an LLM action planner with supervised learning and DPO so REVERIE agents generate single-step navigation instructions from goals and observations.
- [What Matters for Batch Online Reinforcement Learning in Robotics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08078): Systematically studies which algorithmic choices matter when robot policies improve from large batches of autonomously collected data.
- [MA-ROESL: Motion-aware Rapid Reward Optimization for Efficient Robot Skill Learning from Single Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08367): Selects motion-aware frames from a single video, optimizes VLM-generated rewards, and fine-tunes policies for efficient locomotion skill learning.
- [Adaptive Diffusion Policy Optimization for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08376): Fine-tunes diffusion policies for robotic control with Adam-based adaptive-gradient RL and benchmarks against six diffusion-RL baselines.
- [Latent Theory of Mind: A Decentralized Diffusion Architecture for Cooperative Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.09144): Uses decentralized diffusion policies with ego and consensus embeddings plus sheaf-inspired alignment so manipulators can infer partners' latent state during cooperation.
- [Unleashing Humanoid Reaching Potential via Real-World-Ready Skill Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.10918): Builds a real-world-ready humanoid reaching skill space from primitive skills, improving whole-body reachability for object interaction.
- [Parkour in the Wild: Learning a General and Extensible Agile Locomotion Policy Using Multi-expert Distillation and RL Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11164): Distills terrain-specialized locomotion experts into a depth-conditioned foundation policy with DAgger, then fine-tunes it by RL on broader terrain scans.
- [Dynam3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11383): uses dynamic layered 3D tokens to strengthen VLMs for vision-and-language navigation.
- [LaDi-WM: A Latent Diffusion-based World Model for Predictive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11528): Uses latent diffusion as a world model for predictive manipulation, helping embodied policies anticipate action outcomes.
- [EgoDex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11709): Learns dexterous manipulation from large-scale egocentric video, turning scalable human video into robot policy supervision.
- [Zero-Shot Visual Generalization in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11719): Scales disentangled representation learning and associative-memory ideas to make vision-based manipulation policies robust to visual distribution shifts.
- [OneTwoVLA: A Unified Vision-Language-Action Model with Adaptive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11917): Unifies VLA action generation with adaptive reasoning, making reasoning-conditioned control a direct model design axis.
- [H2R: A Human-to-Robot Data Augmentation for Robot Pre-training from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11920): Converts egocentric human videos into robot-centric visual data for robot pretraining.
- [Incentivizing Multimodal Reasoning in Large Models for Direct Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12744): Uses incentive design to improve multimodal reasoning in direct manipulation policies.
- [Enhancing Robot Navigation Policies with Task-Specific Uncertainty Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13837): Uses task-specific uncertainty maps with reinforcement learning so robot navigation policies adapt localization precision to obstacle- and goal-dependent contexts.
- [Adaptive Visuo-Tactile Fusion with Predictive Force Attention for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13982): Uses force-guided attention to adaptively weight visual and tactile features, with self-supervised future-force prediction strengthening contact-rich dexterous manipulation.
- [DORA: Object Affordance-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14819): Guides dexterous RL with object affordance maps, voting-based grasp classification, and an affordance-aware reward for cube, jug, and hammer manipulation.
- [Cascaded Diffusion Models for Neural Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15157): Uses hierarchical diffusion policies for global motion prediction, local trajectory refinement, and online plan repair so navigation and manipulation paths remain collision free.
- [GCNT: Graph-Based Transformer Policies for Morphology-Agnostic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15211): Combines graph convolution with Transformers to build morphology-agnostic RL policies that handle robots with different state and action dimensions.
- [FLARE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15659): Adds implicit world modeling to robot policies by aligning current representations with future latent observations.
- [SwarmDiff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15679): Uses a diffusion transformer to generate risk-aware swarm-robot trajectories in cluttered environments.
- [EasyInsert: A Data-Efficient and Generalizable Insertion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16187): Casts insertion as relative-pose regression and uses a multi-phase visual policy trained from minimal teleoperation plus automated data collection for cluttered zero-shot insertion.
- [ManipLVM-R1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16517): Uses reinforcement learning with affordance perception and trajectory-matching rewards to improve embodied manipulation reasoning and physical action plausibility.
- [3D Equivariant Visuomotor Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16969): Uses spherical projection to bring 3D equivariant diffusion-policy learning to eye-in-hand RGB robot manipulation.
- [Interactive Post-Training for VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17016): Studies post-training VLA policies through interaction, directly targeting embodied policy improvement.
- [H2-COMPACT: Human-Humanoid Co-Manipulation via Adaptive Contact Trajectory Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17627): Separates haptic intent inference from full-body control: force-torque behavior cloning outputs planar velocity, while randomized Isaac Gym RL maps it to humanoid joint trajectories.
- [VLA-RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18719): Applies online RL to pretrained autoregressive VLAs, adds a robotic process reward model from task segments, and improves OpenVLA-7B on LIBERO.
- [OpenHOI: Open-World Hand-Object Interaction Synthesis with Multimodal Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18947): Synthesizes open-world hand-object interactions with a multimodal large language model, linking generative modeling to embodied object-use reasoning.
- [ReFineVLA: Reasoning-Aware Teacher-Guided Transfer Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19080): Uses reasoning-aware teacher-guided transfer fine-tuning for robot policies, adding a VLA transfer recipe.
- [MaskedManipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19086): Distills a generative full-body manipulation controller from large-scale motion tracking into high-level objective-conditioned control.
- [TeViR: Text-to-Video Reward With Diffusion Models for Efficient Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19769): Uses diffusion-based text-to-video rewards to make reinforcement learning more sample-efficient for language-conditioned action policies.
- [What Can RL Bring to VLA Generalization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19789): Compares reinforcement learning with supervised fine-tuning for VLA generalization under visual, semantic, and execution shifts, and proposes a PPO-based recipe.
- [EgoZero: Robot Learning from Smart Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20290): Extracts robot-executable actions and morphology-agnostic states from Project Aria egocentric human demonstrations, enabling closed-loop manipulation policy learning with zero robot data.
- [VLM Can Be a Good Assistant: Enhancing Embodied Visual Tracking with Self-Improving Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20718): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for embodied and VLA agents. Core idea: focus on enhancing embodied visual tracking with self-improving vision-language models as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [Learning Generalizable Robot Policy with Human Demonstration Video as a Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20795): Trains a two-stage policy that takes a human demonstration video as a prompt and performs new tasks without task-specific robot teleoperation or fine-tuning.
- [Spatial RoboGrasp: Generalized Robotic Grasping Control Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20814): Fuses domain-randomized RGB, monocular depth, and a depth-aware 6-DoF grasp prompt, then uses a diffusion imitation policy to produce spatially grounded grasp actions.
- [Object-Centric Action-Enhanced Representations for Robot Visuo-Motor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20962): Fine-tunes a slot-attention object-centric encoder on human action videos so semantic segmentation and representation learning improve simulated robot RL and imitation policies.
- [EquAct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21351): Adds SE(3)-equivariant structure to language-conditioned 3D transformer policies for open-loop robotic manipulation.
- [CoDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21437): Optimizes coordinated diffusion noise for whole-body manipulation of articulated objects, linking human-motion generation with robotics-style object interaction.
- [Active-O3: Empowering Multimodal Large Language Models with Active Perception via GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21457): Trains MLLMs with GRPO, modular sensing actions, and dual-form rewards so they actively choose where to look during embodied perception.
- [ChatVLA-2: Vision-Language-Action Model with Open-World Embodied Reasoning from Pretrained Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21906): Combines pretrained knowledge with open-world embodied reasoning for VLA control.
- [Reinforced Reasoning for Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22050): Trains visual-language models with supervised fine-tuning and GRPO rewards to improve multi-step planning from observations, language goals, and environment feedback.
- [ForceVLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22159): Treats six-axis force feedback as a first-class VLA input through a force-aware mixture-of-experts fusion module and a synchronized vision, proprioception, and force-torque dataset for contact-rich manipulation.
- [Semantic Exploration and Dense Mapping of Complex Environments Using Ground Robot With Panoramic LiDAR-Camera Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22880): Combines geometric coverage, multi-view semantic inspection, a priority-driven local viewpoint sampler, and safe aggressive exploration for dense semantic target mapping with a ground robot.
- [RoboTransfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23171): Generates geometry-consistent multi-view robot videos with controllable background and object edits, then uses the synthetic data to improve manipulation-policy transfer to unseen scenes.
- [Towards a Generalizable Bimanual Foundation Policy via Flow-based Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24156): Fine-tunes text-to-video models into text-to-flow and flow-to-video components, then trains a lightweight diffusion action policy for coordinated bimanual manipulation.
- [SignBot: Learning Human-to-Humanoid Sign Language Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24266): Retargets human sign-language motion to humanoid kinematics, trains robust gesture-tracking control, and adds translation, response, and sign generation modules for embodied sign interaction.
- [Robot-R1: Reinforcement Learning for Enhanced Embodied Reasoning in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00070): Reinforces LVLM reasoning traces that predict the next task keypoint state from scene images and metadata, improving low-level spatial and movement reasoning for robot control.
- [RoboMoRe: LLM-based Robot Co-design via Joint Optimization of Morphology and Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00276): Uses LLM diversity reflection to generate morphology-reward pairs, then alternates reward shaping and morphology-gradient updates for robot body and controller co-design.
- [Improving Keystep Recognition in Ego-Video via Dexterous Focus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00827): Stabilizes egocentric video around the hands so existing recognition models can identify fine-grained procedural keysteps without architecture changes; adjacent to robot policy learning.
- [GraphPad: Inference-Time 3D Scene Graph Updates for Embodied Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01174): Provides a mutable scene-graph memory, navigation log, and task scratchpad that an embodied QA agent updates through API calls at inference time.
- [OG-VLA: Orthographic Image Generation for 3D-Aware Vision-Language Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01196): Combines VLA generalization with 3D-aware policy robustness by generating orthographic views from RGB-D observations for language-conditioned actions.
- [SmolVLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01844): Trains a compact community-data VLA for single-GPU training and consumer-device deployment, with asynchronous perception-action inference and chunked actions for higher control rates.
- [Feel the Force: Contact-Driven Learning from Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01944): Learns force-sensitive manipulation from tactile-glove human demonstrations, retargeting predicted contact forces to a Franka gripper through shared visual and action representations.
- [Fast-in-Slow: A Dual-System Foundation Model Unifying Fast Manipulation within Slow Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01953): Combines fast manipulation with slower reasoning in one foundation model, matching the page boundary between VLA models and embodied control.
- [Sign Language: Towards Sign Understanding for Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02556): Defines navigational sign understanding as parsing locations and directions from public-space signs, with VLM baselines and metrics for robot wayfinding.
- [Rodrigues Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02618): Injects kinematics-aware inductive bias through a learnable Neural Rodrigues Operator and evaluates RodriNet in diffusion-policy imitation learning and 3D hand reconstruction.
- [Fusing Cross-Domain Knowledge from Multimodal Data to Solve Problems in the Physical World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03155): Model candidate for embodied and VLA agents: Fuses cross-domain multimodal knowledge for physical-world problem solving.
- [Robustness-Aware Tool Selection and Manipulation Planning with Learned Energy-Informed Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03362): Optimizes tool choice and contact-rich manipulation trajectories with an energy-based robustness metric that favors disturbance-resilient tool-use plans.
- [Splatting Physical Scenes: End-to-End Real-to-Sim from Imperfect Robot Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04120): Merges Gaussian-splat rendering, explicit object meshes, differentiable physics, and pose calibration to build physics-ready simulations from noisy ALOHA robot trajectories.
- [OWMM-Agent: Open World Mobile Manipulation With Multi-modal Agentic Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04217): Combines multi-view state tracking, function-call robot control, and agentic data synthesis to train OWMM-VLM for open-world mobile manipulation.
- ["Don't Do That!": Guiding Embodied Systems through Large Language Model-based Constraint Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04500): Uses LLMs to translate natural-language forbidden behaviors into executable Python constraint functions for point-cloud robot navigation planners.
- [DemoSpeedup: Accelerating Visuomotor Policies via Entropy-Guided Demonstration Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05064): Uses a trained generative policy's per-frame action entropy to accelerate demonstration playback while preserving precision-sensitive segments.
- [Where Do We Look When We Teach? Analyzing Human Gaze Behavior Across Demonstration Devices in Robot Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05808): Shows that robot-emulating demonstration devices alter human gaze cues, while natural gaze data can substantially improve imitation-policy success under environment shifts.
- [Spatially-enhanced recurrent memory for long-range mapless navigation via end-to-end reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05997): Adds spatially enhanced recurrent units and attention to mapless RL navigation, improving long-horizon spatial memorization from a forward-facing stereo camera.
- [3DFlowAction: Learning Cross-Embodiment Manipulation from 3D Flow World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06199): Trains a language-conditioned 3D optical-flow world model on ManiFlow-110k and converts predicted object motion into cross-embodiment manipulation constraints.
- [Enhancing Robot Safety via MLLM-Based Semantic Interpretation of Failure Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06570): Discovers interpretable failure taxonomies from raw deployment logs by generating multimodal failure explanations and clustering them for targeted data collection and runtime monitoring.
- [BridgeVLA: Input-Output Alignment for Efficient 3D Manipulation Learning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07961): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for embodied and VLA agents. Core idea: Recently, leveraging pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) for building vision-language-action (VLA) models has emerged as a promising approach to effective robot manipulation learning.
- [TensorTouch: Calibration of Tactile Sensors for High Resolution Stress Tensor and Deformation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08291): Combines finite-element analysis with deep learning to recover pixel-level stress tensors, deformation fields, and force distributions from optical tactile sensors.
- [A Gait Driven Reinforcement Learning Framework for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08416): Runs a dynamics-aware hybrid inverted-pendulum gait planner inside humanoid RL training and uses gait-specific rewards to learn periodic bipedal locomotion.
- [Bayesian Inverse Physics for Neuro-Symbolic Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08756): Combines Bayesian inverse physics with neuro-symbolic robot learning so agents can infer latent physical causes behind observed interactions.
- [Robot-Gated Interactive Imitation Learning with Adaptive Intervention Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09176): Learns a proxy Q-function that requests expert demonstrations only when agent and expert actions diverge, reducing human takeover cost in interactive imitation learning.
- [UAD: Unsupervised Affordance Distillation for Generalization in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09284): Distills large-vision and vision-language foundation-model affordance cues into task-conditioned affordance decoders without manual annotations.
- [Analytic Task Scheduler: Recursive Least Squares Based Method for Continual Learning in Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09623): Keeps a library of task-specific robot models and uses a recursive-least-squares scheduler to map language instructions to models without replaying past data.
- [Chain-of-Action: Trajectory Autoregressive Modeling for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09990): Uses trajectory autoregressive modeling for robotic manipulation, adding a sequence-modeling route for embodied action.
- [EQA-RM: A Generative Embodied Reward Model with Test-time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10389): Trains a generative reward model for embodied question answering with C-GRPO so it can emit scores or structured critiques of spatial, temporal, and logical grounding.
- [ExoStart: Efficient learning for dexterous manipulation with sensorized exoskeleton demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.11775): Collects robot-free human hand demonstrations with a low-cost sensorized exoskeleton, filters them through simulation dynamics, and bootstraps sparse-reward dexterous-hand RL.
- [mimic-one: a Scalable Model Recipe for General Purpose Robot Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.11916): Presents a scalable model recipe for general-purpose robot dexterity, fitting the VLA and robot-policy Model section.
- [SAIL: Faster-than-Demonstration Execution of Imitation Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.11948): Speeds up imitation policies with consistency-preserving action inference, controller-invariant target tracking, adaptive speed modulation, and latency-aware action scheduling.
- [RL from Physical Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12769): Fine-tunes text-conditioned motion generators with rewards from a physics-based motion tracking policy and an instruction-alignment verifier for deployable humanoid motion.
- [From Experts to a Generalist: Toward General Whole-Body Control for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12779): Distills clustered expert controllers into a generalist humanoid whole-body policy, using motion descriptions and real-world delta-action adaptation.
- [VLM-SFD: VLM-Assisted Siamese Flow Diffusion Framework for Dual-Arm Cooperative Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13428): Generates two object-centric motion-flow streams with Siamese diffusion and uses a pretrained VLM to assign each flow to the appropriate arm over time.
- [CEED-VLA: Consistency Vision-Language-Action Model with Early-Exit Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13725): Distills VLA action-token prediction into multi-token consistency decoding, adds mixed-label supervision, and exits early when convergence is sufficient.
- [Touch begins where vision ends: Generalizable policies for contact-rich manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13762): Combines VLM-based object localization with reusable local visuo-tactile policies for contact-rich manipulation after the reaching phase.
- [TACS-Graphs: Traversability-Aware Consistent Scene Graphs for Ground Robot Localization and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14178): Uses ground-robot traversability to segment room layers in 3D scene graphs, improving topological consistency, loop-closure detection, and pose graph optimization.
- [ClutterDexGrasp: A Sim-to-Real System for General Dexterous Grasping in Cluttered Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14317): Uses a two-stage teacher-student sim-to-real pipeline with clutter-density curriculum learning for zero-shot closed-loop target dexterous grasping in clutter.
- [Latent Action Diffusion for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14608): Learns contrastively aligned latent action spaces across anthropomorphic hands, a human hand, and a parallel gripper, then co-trains diffusion policies for multi-robot manipulation.
- [Tactile Beyond Pixels: Multisensory Touch Representations for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14754): Pretrains Sparsh-X on image, audio, motion, and pressure signals from Digit 360 contacts, improving tactile adaptation and physical-property inference for manipulation.
- [CDP: Towards Robust Autoregressive Visuomotor Policy Learning via Causal Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14769): Conditions a transformer diffusion policy on historical action sequences and caches attention states to make autoregressive visuomotor control more coherent and efficient.
- [GMT: General Motion Tracking for Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14770): Trains a single humanoid whole-body motion-tracking policy with adaptive sampling and a motion mixture-of-experts architecture, targeting broad real-world motion coverage.
- [What Do Latent Action Models Actually Learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15691): Analyzes when latent action models recover controllable action changes rather than exogenous video noise, linking the problem to PCA and data-policy conditions.
- [ControlVLA: Few-shot Object-centric Adaptation for Pre-trained Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16211): Adapts pretrained VLA models with few-shot object-centric control, adding a practical model-side adaptation mechanism.
- [A Scalable Post-Processing Pipeline for Large-Scale Free-Space Multi-Agent Path Planning with PiBT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16748): Extends PiBT to eight-connected grids and applies safety-aware string-pulling plus SIPP fallback to smooth real-time paths for hundreds of agents.
- [Dex1B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17198): Produces one billion dexterous grasping and articulation demonstrations with a generative model constrained for feasibility and diversity, then validates policies in simulation and on real robots.
- [SViP: Sequencing Bimanual Visuomotor Policies with Object-Centric Motion Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18825): Integrates visuomotor policies with TAMP by segmenting bimanual demonstrations with scene graphs and switching to parameterized motion primitives under distribution shift.
- [UniTac-NV: A Unified Tactile Representation For Non-Vision-Based Tactile Sensors *](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19699): Uses sensor-specific encoders and a shared latent space to transfer non-optical tactile data across uSkin and PapillArray sensors for contact-geometry estimation.
- [CronusVLA: Transferring Latent Motion Across Time for Multi-Frame Prediction in Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19816): Extends single-frame VLA models with multi-frame post-training, feature chunking, and a disturbance benchmark for robust temporal manipulation prediction.
- [ManiGaussian++: General Robotic Bimanual Manipulation with Hierarchical Gaussian World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19842): Models bimanual scene dynamics with task-oriented Gaussian splats and a leader-follower world model that separates stabilizing-arm and acting-arm effects.
- [Unified Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19850): Models vision, language, and actions as unified discrete token sequences and adds world-model post-training to transfer causal video dynamics into robot policies.
- [DemoDiffusion: One-Shot Human Imitation using pre-trained Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20668): Retargets one human hand-motion demonstration into a rough robot trajectory, then lets a pretrained diffusion policy project it into plausible robot actions.
- [WorldVLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21539): Couples an autoregressive action model with an image-predictive world model so action generation and future visual prediction improve each other within one VLA framework.
- [4D-VLA: Spatiotemporal Vision-Language-Action Pretraining with Cross-Scene Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.22242): Adds depth, temporal RGB-D history, coordinate-system alignment, and memory-bank frame sampling to reduce state chaos in VLA pretraining.
- [Evo-0: Vision-Language-Action Model with Implicit Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00416): Plugs geometry-foundation features into RGB-only VLA models so they gain depth-aware scene representations without explicit point clouds or depth sensors.
- [GaussianVLM: Scene-Centric 3D Vision-Language Models Using Language-Aligned Gaussian Splats for Embodied Reasoning and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00886): Embeds language features directly into Gaussian primitives and sparsifies them into task-guided global and local tokens for scene-centric 3D VLM reasoning.
- [SE(3)-Equivariant Diffusion Policy in Spherical Fourier Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.01723): Embeds diffusion-policy states, actions, and denoising in spherical Fourier space to improve 3D transformation generalization in manipulation.
- [AC-DiT: Adaptive Coordination Diffusion Transformer for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.01961): Conditions whole-body action prediction on mobile-base motion and dynamically weights 2D images and 3D point clouds according to the manipulation stage.
- [The Sound of Simulation: Learning Multimodal Sim-to-Real Robot Policies with Generative Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02864): Uses generative audio conditioned on simulation video to train audiovisual robot-pouring policies that transfer zero-shot to real containers and liquids.
- [Personalised Explanations in Long-term Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03049): Maintains user knowledge-memory models so LLM-based HRI explanation systems can adjust detail levels for hospital-patrol and kitchen-assistant robots.
- [RwoR: Generating Robot Demonstrations from Human Hand Collection for Policy Learning without Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03930): Translates human-hand demonstrations into robot-gripper observations with a generative hand-to-gripper model, enabling policy learning without a robot during data collection.
- [Efficient Learning of A Unified Policy For Whole-body Manipulation and Locomotion Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.04229): Guides quadruped-arm RL with an explicit manipulator kinematic model, improving exploration for unified locomotion and manipulation on an X20 plus Z1 platform.
- [Hybrid Diffusion Policies with PGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05695): Injects projective geometric algebra into diffusion policies through a geometric state encoder and action decoder, improving spatial learning efficiency in robot manipulation.
- [Spatial-Temporal Aware Visuomotor Diffusion Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.06710): Introduces DP4, guiding diffusion-policy imitation learning with a dynamic Gaussian world model for 3D spatial and 4D spatiotemporal awareness.
- [ULC: A Unified and Fine-Grained Controller for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.06905): Trains a unified humanoid loco-manipulation controller that jointly tracks root, torso, and dual-arm commands with residual action refinement.
- [UniTracker: Learning Universal Whole-Body Motion Tracker for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07356): Uses a CVAE-based universal student policy and adaptation stage to track diverse whole-body humanoid motions on real hardware.
- [MoSE: Skill-by-Skill Mixture-of-Experts Learning for Embodied Autonomous Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07818): Uses skill-oriented routing and expert specialization so embodied systems can learn and reason skill by skill instead of training a monolithic MoE.
- [Reinforcement Learning with Action Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07969): Runs temporal-difference RL directly in a chunked action space, using offline action sequences for exploration and unbiased multi-step backups on sparse-reward manipulation.
- [Multi-critic Learning for Whole-body End-effector Twist Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.08656): Decouples locomotion and manipulation rewards with a multi-critic actor and tracks end-effector twists for quadruped-arm whole-body control.
- [Learning human-to-robot handovers through 3D scene reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.08726): Trains human-to-robot handover policies from sparse-view Gaussian-splat reconstructions that convert simulated gripper-camera pose changes into robot demonstrations.
- [Tactile-VLA: Unlocking Vision-Language-Action Model's Physical Knowledge for Tactile Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.09160): Deeply fuses tactile sensing into VLA control through a hybrid position-force controller and tactile-feedback reasoning module for contact-rich tasks.
- [MP1: MeanFlow Tames Policy Learning in 1-step for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.10543): Generates point-cloud-conditioned manipulation trajectories in one network evaluation by learning MeanFlow interval-averaged velocities with classifier-free guidance.
- [rt-RISeg: Real-Time Model-Free Robot Interactive Segmentation for Active Instance-Level Object Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.10776): Segments unseen objects during robot interaction by tracking body-frame-invariant rotational and linear velocity features, then prompts vision foundation models with the masks.
- [Diffusion-Based Imaginative Coordination for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11296): Jointly optimizes future-video latent prediction and action diffusion for bimanual policies, using action-conditioned video imagination during training but not inference.
- [Robot Drummer: Learning Rhythmic Skills for Humanoid Drumming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11498): Converts drum scores into rhythmic contact chains, segments long songs, and trains one RL policy for timed multi-limb humanoid drumming.
- [EgoVLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12440): Pretrains VLA models on egocentric human videos to predict wrist and hand actions, then retargets them with inverse kinematics and few robot demonstrations.
- [AnyPos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12768): Learns task-agnostic embodiment dynamics from automated bimanual exploration, decoupling arm and end-effector motions for reuse across high-level manipulation policies.
- [Vidar: Embodied Video Diffusion Model for Generalist Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12898): Introduces an embodied video-diffusion model for generalist manipulation, connecting generative dynamics and bimanual control.
- [VITA: Vision-to-Action Flow Matching Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13231): Defines a noise-free flow-matching policy that maps visual latents directly to structured latent actions through an action autoencoder.
- [EdgeVLA: Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.14049): Speeds VLA inference for edge robots by removing autoregressive end-effector position prediction and using small language-model backbones.
- [MorphIt: Flexible Spherical Approximation of Robot Morphology for Representation-driven Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.14061): Optimizes spherical approximations of robot morphology as a task-dependent accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for collision detection, contact simulation, and confined navigation.
- [GR-3 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.15493): Reports a generalist robot foundation model line, useful as a high-level VLA model anchor for embodied manipulation.
- [ThinkAct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.16815): Trains an MLLM to create action-aligned visual latent plans from reinforced embodied reasoning, then conditions a low-level action model for long-horizon execution.
- [VLA-Touch: Enhancing Vision-Language-Action Models with Dual-Level Tactile Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.17294): Adds dual-level tactile feedback to VLA models, strengthening physical grounding in robot manipulation.
- [Ag2x2: Robust Agent-Agnostic Visual Representations for Zero-Shot Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19817): Builds coordination-aware visual representations from object states and hand-motion patterns for zero-shot bimanual manipulation without expert rewards or demonstrations.
- [CAPE: A CLIP-Aware Pointing Ensemble of Complementary Heatmap Cues for Embodied Reference Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.21888): Ensembles head-to-fingertip and wrist-to-fingertip Gaussian ray heatmaps with CLIP guidance to localize objects referred to by pointing and language.
- [From Seeing to Experiencing: Scaling Navigation Foundation Models with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22028): Post-trains navigation foundation models with RL so video-pretrained agents learn action consequences, obstacle avoidance, and safe urban navigation behavior.
- [Temporally Consistent Unsupervised Segmentation for Mobile Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22194): Clusters DINOv2 superpixel features over mobile-robot video and enforces temporal consistency to discover terrain frontiers without labels.
- [UniLegs: Universal Multi-Legged Robot Control through Morphology-Agnostic Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22653): Distills morphology-specific teacher controllers into one Transformer student policy that controls legged robots with different limb configurations and transfers to hardware.
- [In-between Motion Generation for Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.23053): Uses a CVAE motion generator with physical constraints and phase-manifold continuity to synthesize multi-style quadruped gaits for imitation-policy training.
- [On-Device Diffusion Transformer Policy for Efficient Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00697): Compresses diffusion-policy denoising networks and applies consistency distillation to reach real-time manipulation inference on mobile devices.
- [UniEgoMotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01126): Unifies egocentric motion reconstruction, forecasting, and generation with a head-centric diffusion model trained on EgoExo4D-derived 3D motion annotations.
- [VLH: Vision-Language-Haptics Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01361): Fine-tunes an OpenVLA backbone with LoRA to map visual context and language commands into aerial-robot motion plus mid-air force and vibration haptic actions.
- [FedVLA: Federated Vision-Language-Action Learning with Dual Gating Mixture-of-Experts for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02190): Introduces federated VLA learning with dual-gating mixture-of-experts for robot manipulation.
- [CO-RFT: Efficient Fine-Tuning of Vision-Language-Action Models through Chunked Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02219): Extends temporal-difference offline RL to action chunks so VLA policies can be fine-tuned efficiently and stably from limited robot data.
- [QuaDreamer: Controllable Panoramic Video Generation for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02512): Generates quadruped-specific panoramic videos with vertical-jitter encoding, scene-object control, and panoramic enhancement for training 360-degree perception models.
- [Multimodal Human-Intent Modeling for Contextual Robot-to-Human Handovers of Arbitrary Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02982): Combines verbal and nonverbal intent cues to select distant objects, generate human-preference-aware grasps, and execute compliant robot-to-human handovers.
- [Point2Act: Efficient 3D Distillation of Multimodal LLMs for Zero-Shot Context-Aware Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03099): Distills MLLM task understanding into 3D relevancy fields by aggregating lightweight 2D point guidance across views for zero-shot grasp-point selection.
- [Language as Cost: Proactive Hazard Mapping using VLM for Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03138): Converts VLM scene interpretations into language-derived navigation costs that anticipate dynamic hazards before they appear in a geometric obstacle map.
- [DiWA: Diffusion Policy Adaptation with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03645): Fine-tunes diffusion robot skills offline with RL through a world model trained once on play interactions, avoiding millions of real environment steps.
- [Intention: Inferring Tendencies of Humanoid Robot Motion Through Interactive Intuition and Grounded VLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04931): Combines grounded VLM scene reasoning with an interaction-driven memory graph so humanoids can infer manipulation tendencies from prior task interactions.
- [MAG-Nav: Language-Driven Object Navigation Leveraging Memory-Reserved Active Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05021): Adds perspective-based active grounding and memory backtracking to VLM object navigation, enabling zero-shot quadruped deployment in unseen environments.
- [ASkDAgger: Active Skill-level Data Aggregation for Interactive Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05310): Queries teachers on the novice's planned skill-level actions and uses sensitivity-aware gating plus foresight replay to reduce interactive imitation effort.
- [Information-Theoretic Graph Fusion with Vision-Language-Action Model for Policy Reasoning and Dual Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05342): Builds GF-VLA, extracting Shannon-information hand-object cues into temporal scene graphs that a language-conditioned transformer turns into behavior trees and Cartesian commands.
- [Towards Generalizable Safety in Crowd Navigation via Conformal Uncertainty Handling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05634): Model candidate for embodied and VLA agents: Uses conformal uncertainty handling for safer crowd navigation.
- [Affordance-R1: Reinforcement Learning for Generalizable Affordance Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06206): Trains affordance grounding with GRPO rewards for format, perception, and cognition, yielding zero-shot reasoning about action-relevant object regions.
- [Imaginative World Modeling with Scene Graphs for Embodied Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06990): Uses scene graphs for embodied navigation world modeling, linking spatial prediction with action planning.
- [Bridging Semantic Logic Gaps: A Cognition-Inspired Multimodal Boundary-Preserving Network for Image Manipulation Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07216): Off-axis for robot VLA; uses LLM semantic prompts, image-text ambiguity weighting, and boundary-preserving decoding for manipulated-image localization.
- [GraphCoT-VLA: A 3D Spatial-Aware Reasoning Vision-Language-Action Model for Robotic Manipulation with Ambiguous Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07650): Adds 3D spatial-aware graph chain-of-thought reasoning to VLA manipulation under ambiguous instructions.
- [MolmoAct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07917): Encodes depth-aware perception tokens, generates editable spatial trajectory traces, and predicts low-level robot actions as an open action-reasoning VLA.
- [ReconDreamer-RL: Enhancing Reinforcement Learning via Diffusion-based Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08170): Applies video-diffusion scene reconstruction, dynamic adversary agents, and cousin trajectories to closed-loop autonomous-driving RL; adjacent to embodied simulation.
- [BeyondMimic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08241): Combines scalable humanoid motion tracking with a guided latent diffusion model for motion inpainting, joystick teleoperation, obstacle avoidance, and zero-shot hardware transfer.
- [OmniVTLA: Vision-Tactile-Language-Action Model with Semantic-Aligned Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08706): Adds tactile sensing to vision-language-action modeling, broadening VLA models from visual grounding to multimodal physical feedback.
- [Towards Safe Imitation Learning via Potential Field-Guided Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08707): Learns a flow-matching imitation policy and an obstacle potential field from demonstrations, then modulates generated motions at inference for collision reduction.
- [LLM-Prior Flow Imagination for Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09423): Distills LLM-derived spatial priors into a flow model that completes uncertain semantic maps for ObjectGoal navigation in unseen indoor environments.
- [Interpretable Robot Control via Structured Behavior Trees and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09621): Translates natural-language HRI commands into behavior-tree actions through LLM planning and domain-specific plugins for perception functions such as tracking and gestures.
- [Physical Autoregressive Model for Robotic Manipulation without Action Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09822): Builds a robotic manipulation model without action pretraining, adding an alternative action-generation architecture.
- [Toward Human-Robot Teaming: Learning Handover Behaviors from 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09855): Uses sparse-view Gaussian-splat reconstructions of handover scenes to synthesize gripper-camera image-action demonstrations for stable, collision-aware human-to-robot handovers.
- [ReconVLA: Reconstructive Vision-Language-Action Model as Effective Robot Perceiver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10333): Adds a diffusion-transformer reconstruction head that forces VLA attention toward manipulated target regions and pretrains on more than 100k robot trajectories.
- [CorrectNav: Self-Correction Flywheel Empowers Vision-Language-Action Navigation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10416): Uses a self-correction flywheel to mine navigation-error trajectories and generate perception/action correction data for VLA navigation post-training.
- [MASH: Cooperative-Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Single Humanoid Robot Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10423): Treats one humanoid's limbs as heterogeneous MARL agents with a shared critic to train coordinated whole-body locomotion.
- [MLM: Learning Multi-Task Loco-Manipulation Whole-Body Control for Quadruped Robot With Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10538): Learns quadruped-arm loco-manipulation with real and simulated trajectories, curriculum sampling, and a trajectory-velocity prediction network for zero-shot deployment.
- [Human-in-Context: Unified Cross-Domain 3D Human Motion Modeling via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10897): Off-axis for robot VLA; unifies pose and mesh tasks with in-context prompting and dual-branch context injection for 3D human motion modeling.
- [Multi-Group Equivariant Augmentation for Reinforcement Learning in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.11204): Applies independent spatial and temporal group transformations inside a POMDP formulation to augment offline RL for more sample-efficient manipulation.
- [ExploreVLM: Closed-Loop Robot Exploration Task Planning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.11918): Uses a self-reflective dual-stage VLM planner, object-centric spatial relation graph, and execution validator for closed-loop robot exploration and replanning.
- [Scale-Disentangled spatiotemporal Modeling for Long-term Traffic Emission Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.11923): Off-axis for robot VLA; forecasts urban traffic emissions with Koopman lifting, gated wavelet decomposition, and scale-disentangled spatiotemporal fusion.
- [MAPF-World: Action World Model for Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.12087): Builds an action world model for multi-agent path finding, relevant to embodied multi-agent planning and simulation.
- [Robot Trains Robot: Automatic Real-World Policy Adaptation and Learning for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.12252): Uses a robotic-arm teacher to protect, perturb, reset, reward, and schedule long real-world humanoid RL while adapting a sim policy through one dynamics latent variable.
- [RoboRetriever: Single-Camera Robot Object Retrieval via Active and Interactive Perception with Dynamic Scene Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.12916): Maintains a dynamic hierarchical scene graph from a wrist RGB-D camera and uses reasoning VLM prompts to choose task-aware 6-DoF camera and interaction actions.
- [MimicFunc: Imitating Tool Manipulation from a Single Human Video via Functional Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13534): Builds function-centric coordinate frames from one RGB-D human tool-use video, transfers the skill to novel tools, and uses generated rollouts for visuomotor training.
- [SimGenHOI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.14120): Combines a DiT humanoid-object interaction generator with a contact-aware RL whole-body controller and mutual fine-tuning for physically plausible long-horizon interactions.
- [FBI: Learning Dexterous In-hand Manipulation with Dynamic Visuotactile Shortcut Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.14441): Links tactile signals to object motion through a dynamics-aware latent model and trains a one-step diffusion policy for real-time visuotactile in-hand manipulation.
- [TRUST-Planner: Topology-guided Robust Trajectory Planner for AAVs with Uncertain Obstacle Spatial-temporal Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.14610): Combines dynamic visible probabilistic roadmaps, UTF-MINCO trajectories, dynamic distance fields, and multi-branch management for robust aerial obstacle avoidance.
- [Neural Robot Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.15755): Replaces analytical low-level dynamics and contact solvers with robot-specific neural dynamics models that generalize across tasks and can be fine-tuned from real data.
- [Spatial Policy: Guiding Visuomotor Robotic Manipulation with Spatial-Aware Modeling and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.15874): Adds explicit spatial modeling to visuomotor manipulation via spatial-conditioned video generation, flow-based action prediction, and spatial-reasoning replanning.
- [Do What? Teaching Vision-Language-Action Models to Reject the Impossible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.16292): Introduces IVA, training VLAs to detect false-premise instructions, ask for clarification or correction, and ground feasible alternatives before acting.
- [Fiducial Marker Splatting for High-Fidelity Robotics Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.17012): Inserts Gaussian-splat fiducial markers such as AprilTags into cluttered photorealistic simulation scenes to improve localization and control cues.
- [GWM: Towards Scalable Gaussian World Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.17600): Builds scalable Gaussian world models for robotic manipulation, extending embodied world-model coverage.
- [Integration of Robot and Scene Kinematics for Sequential Mobile Manipulation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.18627): Builds an augmented configuration space that integrates robot and articulated-scene kinematics, then plans long-horizon mobile manipulation with symbolic, refinement, and motion layers.
- [Enhancing Video-Based Robot Failure Detection Using Task Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.18705): Improves robot failure detection by adding performed-action and task-object annotations to video models, plus variable-frame-rate augmentation and test-time augmentation.
- [From Tabula Rasa to Emergent Abilities: Discovering Robot Skills via Real-World Unsupervised Quality-Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.19172): Extends quality-diversity actor-critic into URSA, enabling a real quadruped to discover locomotion skill repertoires and reuse them for damage adaptation.
- [Discrete-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.20095): Decomposes continuous multi-robot motion planning with discrete MAPF guidance, constrained diffusion, and lightweight repair to scale collision-free planning to 100 robots.
- [Learning Fast, Tool-Aware Collision Avoidance for Collaborative Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.20457): Filters robot and tool point-cloud components, predicts occluded collision risk, and uses constrained RL for sub-10-millisecond tool-aware avoidance control.
- [SPGrasp: Spatiotemporal Prompt-Driven Grasp Synthesis in Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.20547): Extends SAMv2 with user prompts and spatiotemporal video context to produce temporally consistent low-latency grasps for moving objects.
- [Can LLMs Generate Behaviors for Embodied Virtual Agents Based on Personality Traits?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.21087): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for embodied and VLA agents. Core idea: focus on can llms generate behaviors for embodied virtual agents based on personality traits? as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [TReF-6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.00310): Infers a 6-DoF task-relevant frame from one demonstration and grounds it with a vision-language model for one-shot robot manipulation generalization.
- [Robix: A Unified Model for Robot Interaction, Reasoning and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.01106): Unifies robot interaction, reasoning, and planning in a vision-language architecture.
- [Constrained Decoding for Safe Robot Navigation Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.01728): Constrains action-token decoding for navigation foundation models so generated robot trajectories satisfy safety rules before low-level execution.
- [Fail2Progress: Learning from Real-World Robot Failures with Stein Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.01746): Uses Stein variational inference to synthesize failure-targeted training cases from real robot mistakes, improving long-horizon skill-effect models.
- [Non-conflicting Energy Minimization in Reinforcement Learning based Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.01765): Projects policy gradients between task and energy objectives, reducing locomotion energy use without reward-weight tuning or sacrificing task performance in sim-to-real quadruped control.
- [ManiFlow: A General Robot Manipulation Policy via Consistency Flow Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.01819): Applies consistency-flow training to build a general robot manipulation policy.
- [Reactive In-Air Clothing Manipulation with Confidence-Aware Dense Correspondence and Visuotactile Affordance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.03889): Combines confidence-aware dense visual correspondence with tactile affordance feedback so dual-arm robots can reactively manipulate hanging garments under occlusion.
- [EMMA for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.04443): Scales mobile-manipulation policies by co-training on egocentric human mobile-manipulation data and robot data.
- [RoboBallet: Planning for multirobot reaching with graph neural networks and reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.05397): Plans multi-robot reaching with graph neural networks and reinforcement learning, extending embodied multi-agent control models.
- [Photorealistic Mapping for Robot Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.06433): builds real-time photorealistic maps for situational awareness in robot teleoperation.
- [LLaDA-VLA: Vision Language Diffusion Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.06932): Vision-language diffusion action model for embodied VLA coverage.
- [Graph-Fused Vision-Language-Action for Policy Reasoning in Multi-Arm Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.07957): Fuses task-relevant RGB-D hand-object scene graphs with a language-conditioned transformer to generate behavior trees, Cartesian primitives, and cross-arm assignments.
- [TA-VLA: Elucidating the Design Space of Torque-aware Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.07962): Maps torque-aware design choices for vision-language-action models.
- [PegasusFlow: Parallel Rolling-Denoising Score Sampling for Robot Diffusion Planner Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.08435): Uses parallel rolling-denoising score sampling for robot diffusion planners, improving flow-matching action generation efficiency.
- [VLA-Adapter: An Effective Paradigm for Tiny-Scale Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09372): Adapter paradigm for tiny-scale VLA models.
- [SimpleVLA-RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09674): Scales VLA reinforcement learning with a simplified training recipe for post-training robot action models beyond behavior cloning.
- [MimicDroid: In-Context Learning for Humanoid Robot Manipulation from Human Play Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09769): Adapts humanoid manipulation from human play videos by using in-context examples rather than task-specific robot demonstrations.
- [Self-Augmented Robot Trajectory: Efficient Imitation Learning via Safe Self-augmentation with Demonstrator-annotated Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09893): Starts from one human demonstration with annotated precision boundaries, then safely self-generates collision-free trajectory variants for imitation learning.
- [ImMimic: Cross-Domain Imitation from Human Videos via Mapping and Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.10952): Bridges human-video and robot-demonstration domains for manipulation by mapping retargeted hand trajectories to robot joints and interpolating paired human-robot trajectories during co-training.
- [SAMP: Spatial Anchor-based Motion Policy for Collision-Aware Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11185): Encodes both manipulator geometry and environment obstacles as SDFs on shared spatial anchors, training a neural motion policy for smooth collision-aware trajectories.
- [DVDP: An End-to-End Policy for Mobile Robot Visual Docking with RGB-D Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13024): Trains an RGB-D end-to-end visual docking policy on Unity and real robot data so a mobile robot directly outputs feasible docking paths.
- [Empowering Multi-Robot Cooperation via Sequential World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13095): Uses autoregressive agent-wise world models for multi-robot MBRL, sharing predicted intentions to improve Bi-DexHands and Multi-Quadruped cooperation.
- [StageACT: Stage-Conditioned Imitation for Robust Humanoid Door Opening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13200): Conditions imitation policies on door-opening stages so humanoids can switch behaviors across reaching, contact, pulling, and recovery phases.
- [Embracing Bulky Objects with Humanoid Robots: Whole-Body Manipulation with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13534): Trains whole-body humanoid reinforcement-learning policies for carrying and stabilizing bulky objects with coordinated arms, torso, and locomotion.
- [I, Robot? Exploring Ultra-Personalized AI-Powered AAC; an Autoethnographic Account](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13671): Introduces model-side work on i, Robot? Exploring Ultra-Personalized AI-Powered AAC; an Autoethnographic Account, fitting the repository's training, architecture, post-training, verifier, or synthetic-data coverage.
- [Dual-Actor Fine-Tuning of VLA Models: A Talk-and-Tweak Human-in-the-Loop Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13774): Fine-tunes VLA policies with a primary actor, a latent-space refinement actor, and talk-and-tweak human corrections converted into language-grounded training data.
- [Behavior Foundation Model for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13780): Pretrains a generative behavior model for humanoid whole-body control, enabling multiple control modes and fast adaptation to new behaviors.
- [GeoAware-VLA: Implicit Geometry Aware Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14117): Adds implicit geometry awareness to vision-language-action models, improving spatial grounding for embodied control.
- [CLAW: A Vision-Language-Action Framework for Weight-Aware Robotic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14143): Decouples weight-threshold monitoring from action generation by using a fine-tuned CLIP prompt generator to guide a flow-based VLA grasping policy.
- [Gen2Real: Towards Demo-Free Dexterous Manipulation by Harnessing Generated Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14178): Uses generated video as a substitute for human demonstrations in dexterous manipulation, linking video generation to robot policy learning.
- [DreamControl: Human-Inspired Whole-Body Humanoid Control for Scene Interaction via Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14353): Uses guided diffusion for whole-body humanoid control during scene interaction.
- [CRAFT: Coaching Reinforcement Learning Autonomously using Foundation Models for Multi-Robot Coordination Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14380): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for embodied and VLA agents. Core idea: focus on coaching reinforcement learning autonomously using foundation models for multi-robot coordination tasks as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [exUMI: Extensible Robot Teaching System with Action-aware Task-agnostic Tactile Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14688): Pairs extensible UMI-style tactile collection hardware with Tactile Prediction Pretraining over more than 1M tactile frames for action-aware contact representations.
- [AnoF-Diff: One-Step Diffusion-Based Anomaly Detection for Forceful Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.15153): Applies one-step diffusion scoring to force-torque time series, enabling online anomaly detection during forceful tool-use tasks despite noisy nonstationary sensor data.
- [Self-Improving Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.15155): Targets self-improvement for embodied foundation models, directly relevant to model-side post-training and adaptation.
- [Improving Robotic Manipulation with Efficient Geometry-Aware Vision Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.15880): This work improves robotic manipulation with a geometry-aware vision encoder, adding a reusable perception backbone for embodied policy learning.
- [Compose by Focus: Scene Graph-based Atomic Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16053): Learns atomic skills with task-focused scene graphs, graph neural networks, and diffusion imitation policies to improve compositional manipulation under distractors.
- [Efficient Detection of Objects Near a Robot Manipulator via Miniature Time-of-Flight Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16122): Models expected arm-mounted miniature ToF readings for the robot alone, then subtracts self-detections to localize nearby objects for collision avoidance and safe interaction.
- [KungfuBot2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16638): Trains a unified whole-body controller with mixture-of-experts specialization for diverse and dynamic humanoid motion skills.
- [Segment-to-Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16677): Uses label-noise-robust action-prompted video segmentation toward embodied intelligence.
- [HDMI: Learning Interactive Humanoid Whole-Body Control from Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16757): Retargets monocular human-object videos into structured motion data and trains an RL humanoid controller with object representations, residual actions, and interaction rewards.
