- [Ratatouille: Imitation Learning Ingredients for Real-world Social Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17204): Studies offline imitation-learning design choices for real-world social robot navigation, improving collision and success behavior over naive behavior cloning.
- [Learning Dexterous Manipulation with Quantized Hand State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17450): Quantizes dexterous hand states while relaxing arm actions continuously, letting a diffusion policy coordinate arm and hand motions without the hand dominating the action space.
- [MotionTrans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17759): Transfers motion-level knowledge from human VR data into robotic manipulation policies.
- [M3ET: Efficient Vision-Language Learning for Robotics based on Multimodal Mamba-Enhanced Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18005): Combines Mamba modules with semantic adaptive attention for lightweight multimodal fusion, alignment, and modality reconstruction on resource-limited robotic platforms.
- [VLA-LPAF: Lightweight Perspective-Adaptive Fusion for Vision-Language-Action to Enable More Unconstrained Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18183): Adds a lightweight perspective-adaptive fusion module that fine-tunes from one view and fuses multi-view observations in latent space for VLA manipulation.
- [Conversational Orientation Reasoning: Egocentric-to-Allocentric Navigation with Multimodal Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18200): Teaches navigation models to translate egocentric observations into allocentric directions through multimodal chain-of-thought orientation reasoning.
- [Fine-Tuning Robot Policies While Maintaining User Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18311): Introduces model-side work on fine-Tuning Robot Policies While Maintaining User Privacy, fitting training, architecture, post-training, synthetic-data, verifier, reward, or world-model coverage.
- [PrioriTouch: Adapting to User Contact Preferences for Whole-Arm Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18447): Learns to rank multiple contact-control objectives and executes them with hierarchical operational space control, adapting whole-arm pHRI to user force preferences.
- [Robotic Skill Diversification via Active Mutation of Reward Functions in Reinforcement Learning During a Liquid Pouring Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18463): Actively mutates reinforcement-learning reward functions to diversify liquid-pouring behaviors instead of optimizing a single hand-designed objective.
- [LCMF: Lightweight Cross-Modality Mambaformer for Embodied Robotics VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18576): A lightweight cross-modality Mambaformer for embodied robotics VQA. Core idea: improve efficient multimodal fusion for resource-constrained embodied robots.
- [SPiDR: A Simple Approach for Zero-Shot Safety in Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18648): Uses pessimistic domain randomization to bake sim-to-real uncertainty into safety constraints, giving scalable safe RL transfer to real robot platforms.
- [DexSkin: High-Coverage Conformable Robotic Skin for Learning Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18830): Adds calibratable conformable tactile skin to gripper fingers, enabling demonstration learning, sensor-instance transfer, and online RL for contact-rich manipulation.
- [Bi-VLA: Bilateral Control-Based Imitation Learning via Vision-Language Fusion for Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18865): Fuses bilateral-control joint angle, velocity, torque, vision, and language through SigLIP and FiLM so one imitation model generates actions for multiple real-robot tasks.
- [World4RL: Diffusion World Models for Policy Refinement with Reinforcement Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19080): Refines imitation-learned manipulation policies by running RL inside high-fidelity diffusion world models instead of collecting unsafe real-robot rollouts.
- [A Multimodal Stochastic Planning Approach for Navigation and Multi-Robot Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19168): Introduces model-side work on a Multimodal Stochastic Planning Approach for Navigation and Multi-Robot Coordination, fitting training, architecture, post-training, synthetic-data, verifier, reward, or world-model coverage.
- [Residual Off-Policy RL for Finetuning Behavior Cloning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19301): Embodied/VLA model-side RL fine-tuning method for behavior-cloned policies.
- [ROPA: Synthetic Robot Pose Generation for RGB-D Bimanual Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19454): Fine-tunes diffusion models to synthesize third-person RGB-D bimanual robot observations and paired joint-action labels, adding a scalable data-augmentation route for embodied manipulation policies.
- [OmniVLA: An Omni-Modal Vision-Language-Action Model for Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19480): Trains a VLA navigation policy with randomized fusion across 2D pose goals, egocentric image goals, language goals, and their combinations.
- [Generalist Robot Manipulation beyond Action Labeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19958): Uses unlabeled human and robot videos by extracting dynamic 3D point clouds at hands or grippers and training a 3D dynamics predictor for self-supervision.
- [Parse-Augment-Distill: Learning Generalizable Bimanual Visuomotor Policies from Single Human Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20286): Learns bimanual visuomotor policies from a single human video through parsing, task-and-motion-planning augmentation, and distillation. Core idea: reduce robot demonstration requirements while preserving executable action structure for manipulation policies.
- [VisualMimic: Visual Humanoid Loco-Manipulation via Motion Tracking and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20322): Combines egocentric vision, a task-agnostic keypoint tracker, and high-level policy learning for sim-to-real humanoid loco-manipulation.
- [Joint Flow Trajectory Optimization For Feasible Robot Motion Generation from Video Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20703): Generates feasible robot grasp and object trajectories from video demonstrations by combining flow matching with kinematic and collision constraints.
- [Normalizing Flows are Capable Models for Bi-manual Visuomotor Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21073): Uses normalizing flows as expressive visuomotor policies for bimanual control, modeling multimodal action distributions without diffusion sampling.
- [Cross-Modal Instructions for Robot Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21107): Generates robot motion from rough cross-modal instructions such as sketches and text labels. Core idea: use a VLM and a fine-grained pointing model to synthesize 3D motion trajectories, then use them to initialize policies refined by reinforcement learning.
- [Embodied Representation Alignment with Mirror Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21136): Maps observed-action and executed-action representations into a shared latent space and aligns them with contrastive learning to improve embodied generalization.
- [KV-Efficient VLA: A Method to Speed up Vision Language Models with RNN-Gated Chunked KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21354): Improves VLA efficiency with an RNN-gated chunked KV cache.
- [VLBiMan: Vision-Language Anchored One-Shot Demonstration Enables Generalizable Bimanual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21723): Decomposes a single bimanual demonstration into invariant skill anchors and uses vision-language grounding plus geometric feasibility to adapt adjustable components.
- [Actions as Language: Fine-Tuning VLMs into VLAs Without Catastrophic Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22195): Represents low-level robot actions as natural-language tokens and uses LoRA fine-tuning to turn VLMs into VLAs while preserving pretrained reasoning.
- [MimicDreamer: Aligning Human and Robot Demonstrations for Scalable VLA Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22199): Aligns human videos to robot-usable supervision with an H2R video-diffusion aligner plus viewpoint and action alignment for scalable VLA policy training.
- [EMMA Generative Visual Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22407): Augments real-world VLA manipulation policies through generative visual transfer across object appearances.
- [WoW: Towards a World omniscient World model Through Embodied Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22642): Builds a world model through embodied interaction rather than passive observation.
- [GZSL-MoE: Apprentissage G{é}n{é}ralis{é} Z{é}ro-Shot bas{é} sur le M{é}lange d'Experts pour la Segmentation S{é}mantique de Nuages de Points 3DAppliqu{é} {à} un Jeu de Donn{é}es d'Environnement de Collaboration Humain-Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22708): Applies mixture-of-experts generalized zero-shot learning to 3D point-cloud semantic segmentation in human-robot collaboration environments.
- [Robot Learning from Any Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22970): Turns a single in-the-wild image into a physics-enabled interactive scene, generating large visuomotor demonstration sets for manipulation and humanoid learning.
- [UniPrototype: Humn-Robot Skill Learning with Uniform Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23021): Learns human-robot skills with uniform prototype representations, aligning demonstrations and robot executions through shared skill prototypes.
- [HeLoM: Hierarchical Learning for Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation in Hexapod Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23651): Uses a hierarchical hexapod loco-manipulation policy, with a high-level pushing planner and low-level stability controller, to move heavy or irregular objects by coordinated contacts.
- [LocoFormer: Generalist Locomotion via Long-context Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23745): Adapts generalist locomotion policies with long-context signals.
- [Mash, Spread, Slice! Learning to Manipulate Object States via Visual Spatial Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24129): Proposes SPARTA, representing object-state-change tasks as spatially progressing actionable-to-transformed regions for manipulation such as mashing, spreading, and slicing.
- [SCOPE: Semantic Conditioning for Sim2Real Category-Level Object Pose Estimation in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24572): Conditions diffusion pose estimation on DINOv2 semantic priors and point-normal noise modeling to reduce sim-to-real gaps for category-level pose and unseen-object grasping.
- [U-DiT Policy: U-shaped Diffusion Transformers for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24579): Introduces model-side work on u-DiT Policy: U-shaped Diffusion Transformers for Robotic Manipulation, fitting the repository's training, architecture, post-training, verifier, or synthetic-data coverage.
- [Stabilizing Humanoid Robot Trajectory Generation via Physics-Informed Learning and Control-Informed Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24697): Adds physics-informed losses during humanoid imitation learning and PI control-informed steering at inference to reduce drift and contact violation in generated trajectories.
- [Fidelity-Aware Data Composition for Robust Robot Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24797): Optimizes real-synthetic robot data mixtures with Coherent Information Fidelity Tuning and multi-view video augmentation to avoid shortcut learning under distribution shift.
- [Annotation-Free One-Shot Imitation Learning for Multi-Step Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24972): Uses a single unannotated human demonstration and pretrained visual features to execute multi-step manipulation without extra model training or manual subtask labels.
- [VLA Model Post-Training via Action-Chunked PPO and Self Behavior Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25718): A VLA post-training method using action-chunked PPO and self behavior cloning. Core idea: make reinforcement learning feedback denser and temporally smoother for vision-language-action policies.
- [Reinforced Embodied Planning with Verifiable Reward for Real-World Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25852): Trains RoboFarseer under REVER, using dense verifiable rewards to fine-tune VLMs for long-horizon real-world manipulation planning.
- [Towards Intuitive Human-Robot Interaction through Embodied Gesture-Driven Control with Woven Tactile Skins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25951): Uses woven tactile skins to map embodied human gestures into robot control signals for more intuitive physical human-robot interaction.
- [ISyHand: A Dexterous Multi-Finger Robot Hand with an Articulated Palm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26236): Presents a multi-finger hand with an articulated palm, expanding dexterous manipulation hardware for policies that need palm reconfiguration.
- [ExoPredicator: Learning Abstract Models of Dynamic Worlds for Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26255): Learns symbolic state abstractions and causal endogenous and exogenous process models with variational Bayesian inference plus LLM proposals for long-horizon planning.
- [OmniRetarget: Interaction-Preserving Data Generation for Humanoid Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation and Scene Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26633): Generates interaction-preserving humanoid trajectories by retargeting human motions while maintaining contact and spatial relations among robot, terrain, and objects.
- [MLA: A Multisensory Language-Action Model for Multimodal Understanding and Forecasting in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26642): Builds a multisensory language-action model for robotic understanding and forecasting.
- [VLA-RFT: Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Fine-tuning with Verified Rewards in World Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00406): VLA reinforcement fine-tuning method using verified rewards in world simulators.
- [Hybrid Training for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00600): Trains VLAs to benefit from embodied chain-of-thought supervision while allowing thought generation to be skipped at inference for faster robot control.
- [Compose Your Policies!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01068): Improves diffusion-based and flow-based robot policies through test-time distribution-level composition.
- [Online Hierarchical Policy Learning using Physics Priors for Robot Navigation in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01519): Combines a sparse global connectivity graph with a neural-field local planner that solves the Eikonal PDE for online navigation in unknown indoor environments.
- [VLA-R1: Enhancing Reasoning in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01623): Uses reasoning-oriented training to enhance VLA models, fitting the model-side reasoning and post-training axis.
- [Symskill: Symbol and Skill Co-Invention for Data-Efficient and Real-Time Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01661): Jointly learns predicates, operators, and goal-oriented skills from unsegmented demonstrations, then composes them with a symbolic planner for real-time long-horizon recovery.
- [LangGrasp: Leveraging Fine-Tuned LLMs for Language Interactive Robot Grasping with Ambiguous Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02104): Fine-tunes LLMs to infer implicit grasping intents from ambiguous language and pairs them with 2D-part-guided point-cloud localization for part-level grasping.
- [Retargeting Matters: General Motion Retargeting for Humanoid Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02252): Studies how humanoid retargeting quality affects downstream policy learning and proposes General Motion Retargeting to reduce physically infeasible references.
- [UMI-on-Air: Embodiment-Aware Guidance for Embodiment-Agnostic Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02614): Guides a high-level UMI diffusion policy with gradient feedback from a low-level embodiment-specific controller, adapting handheld-gripper demonstrations to aerial manipulation.
- [Learning to Act Through Contact: A Unified View of Multi-Task Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03599): Represents tasks as contact goals over positions, timing, and active end-effectors, then trains a goal-conditioned RL policy shared across locomotion and bimanual manipulation embodiments.
- [EmbodiSwap for Zero-Shot Robot Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03706): Generates photorealistic robot overlays on human videos and trains a V-JEPA-backed closed-loop manipulation policy from the synthetic robot-video data.
- [SITCOM: Scaling Inference-Time COMpute for VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04041): Scales inference-time compute for vision-language-action models.
- [Flexible Locomotion Learning with Diffusion Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04234): Uses a generative diffusion model as a dynamics prior for MPC, jointly denoising future states and actions while applying reward planning and constraint projection at test time.
- [ResMimic: From General Motion Tracking to Humanoid Whole-body Loco-Manipulation via Residual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05070): Converts general humanoid motion tracking into object-aware whole-body loco-manipulation through residual learning and contact-aware rewards.
- [Active Next-Best-View Optimization for Risk-Averse Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06481): Optimizes next-best viewpoints for risk-averse path planning, coupling active perception with navigation under uncertain scene geometry.
- [RLinf-VLA: A Unified and Efficient Framework for Reinforcement Learning of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06710): Provides a unified and efficient reinforcement-learning framework for vision-language-action models.
- [Trajectory Conditioned Cross-embodiment Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.07773): Transfers manipulation skills across embodiments by conditioning robot video and action generation on sparse human-motion trajectories, visual input, and language. Core idea: use embodiment-agnostic trajectory cues to learn robot actions from human demonstration videos without paired robot data.
- [Towards Proprioception-Aware Embodied Planning for Dual-Arm Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.07882): Targets dual-arm humanoid planning with proprioception-aware embodied policies, fitting VLA and robot-planning model coverage.
- [A Multimodal Depth-Aware Method For Embodied Reference Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08278): Improves embodied reference understanding by combining language instructions, pointing cues, depth maps, LLM-based data augmentation, and a depth-aware decision module for cluttered-scene referent disambiguation.
- [R2RGEN: Real-to-Real 3D Data Generation for Spatially Generalized Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08547): Generates real-to-real 3D data for spatially generalized manipulation, relevant to embodied synthetic-data scaling.
- [Dream to Recall: Imagination-Guided Experience Retrieval for Memory-Persistent Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08553): Uses a language-conditioned world model to imagine future VLN states as retrieval queries over viewpoint memory containing observations and behavior histories.
- [NovaFlow: Zero-Shot Manipulation via Actionable Flow from Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08568): Uses actionable flow from generated videos for zero-shot manipulation, connecting video priors to robot action.
- [Geometry-aware Policy Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08787): Injects geometric structure into imitation policies so robot actions preserve spatial relations rather than only matching demonstration trajectories.
- [Adaptive Motion Planning via Contact-Based Intent Inference for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08811): Infers human intent from physical contact and adapts robot motion plans online during collaborative manipulation.
- [When a Robot is More Capable than a Human: Learning from Constrained Demonstrators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09096): Introduces model-side work on when a Robot is More Capable than a Human: Learning from Constrained Demonstrators, fitting training, architecture, post-training, synthetic-data, verifier, reward, or world-model coverage.
- [Glovity: Learning Dexterous Contact-Rich Manipulation via Spatial Wrench Feedback Teleoperation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09229): Collects contact-rich teleoperation with wrench feedback and calibrated tactile glove signals, then feeds wrench observations into DP-R3M imitation policies for dexterous manipulation.
- [X-VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10274): Uses soft-prompted Transformers to scale a cross-embodiment VLA model across robot forms without hard-coding embodiment-specific action heads.
- [UniJEPA: Enhancing Robot Policy via Unified Continuous and Discrete Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10642): Learns unified continuous and discrete representations for robot policies, adding a representation-learning route for embodied control.
- [Vlaser: Vision-Language-Action Model with Synergistic Embodied Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11027): Builds a vision-language-action model with synergistic embodied reasoning.
- [PhysHSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11072): Builds a deployable humanoid-scene interaction system that combines simulation training, scene perception, and real-world execution.
- [Spatial Forcing: Implicit Spatial Representation Alignment for Vision-language-action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12276): Spatial representation-alignment method for VLA models.
- [Autonomous Legged Mobile Manipulation for Lunar Surface Operations via Constrained Reinforcement Learning*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12684): Trains constrained RL for quadruped mobile manipulation under lunar collision, stability, power, and rough-terrain constraints.
- [ERA / Embodied Reasoning Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12693): Turns smaller VLMs into embodied agents with a two-stage recipe. Core idea: first distill trajectory-augmented, environment-anchored, and external-knowledge priors, then run online RL with stability measures for long-horizon sparse-reward interaction, reducing dependence on very large proprietary VLMs.
- [T(R,O) Grasp: Efficient Graph Diffusion of Robot-Object Spatial Transformation for Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12724): Represents hand-object spatial transforms as a graph, applies graph diffusion for cross-hand grasp synthesis, and uses inverse kinematics for fast closed-loop dexterous grasping.
- [VLA-0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13054): Shows that representing actions directly as text can produce strong VLA performance without modifying the VLM vocabulary or adding specialized action heads.
- [RL-100: Performant Robotic Manipulation with Real-World Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14830): Unifies imitation and reinforcement learning for diffusion visuomotor policies with a clipped PPO denoising objective and one-step consistency distillation.
- [VT-Refine: Learning Bimanual Assembly with Visuo-Tactile Feedback via Simulation Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14930): Starts from small visuo-tactile demonstrations, refines a diffusion policy in tactile simulation with RL, and transfers bimanual assembly policies back to real hardware.
- [RM-RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15189): Uses role-model reinforcement learning to improve precise robot manipulation where small action errors matter.
- [MoS-VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16617): Learns a basis skill space from Open X-Embodiment data and adapts VLA control to new manipulation tasks from one demonstration without gradient updates.
- [Bridging Embodiment Gaps: Deploying Vision-Language-Action Models on Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.17369): A VLA deployment study for soft continuum manipulators. Core idea: test whether language-guided VLA control transfers beyond rigid serial-link robot embodiments.
- [MemER: Scaling Up Memory for Robot Control via Experience Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.20328): Trains a hierarchical robot policy whose high-level module retrieves relevant experience keyframes and conditions low-level VLA control instructions on them.
- [VAMOS: A Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action Model for Capability-Modulated and Steerable Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.20818): Introduces a hierarchical vision-language-action model for capability-modulated and steerable navigation.
- [Generalizable Hierarchical Skill Learning via Object-Centric Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21121): Decomposes demonstrations into object-canonicalized skill primitives with foundation models, then maps predicted skill-object pairs back to world-frame actions.
- [Scalable Vision-Language-Action Model Pretraining for Robotic Manipulation with Real-Life Human Activity Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21571): Uses real-life human activity videos for VLA pretraining, adding a scalable video-data route for robot policies.
- [Embodied Navigation with Auxiliary Task of Action Description Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21809): Adds action-description prediction as an auxiliary training task so navigation policies learn language-aligned representations of their own movements.
- [ACG: Action Coherence Guidance for Flow-based VLA models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22201): Adds training-free test-time guidance for flow-based VLA policies to suppress jerks, pauses, and jitter from noisy demonstrations and improve action coherence.
- [ManiDP: Manipulability-Aware Diffusion Policy for Posture-Dependent Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23016): Extracts bimanual manipulability from demonstrations with Riemannian probabilistic models and conditions a diffusion policy on those posture features for dual-arm trajectories.
- [Awakening Facial Emotional Expressions in Human-Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23059): Models facial emotional expression generation for human-robot interaction, an embodied social-expression control problem.
- [DynaRend: Learning 3D Dynamics via Masked Future Rendering for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24261): Pretrains triplane robot representations with masked reconstruction and future prediction through differentiable volumetric rendering, then transfers them to action-value maps.
- [Affordance Representation and Recognition for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24459): Focuses on representing and recognizing affordances so autonomous agents can connect object properties with possible actions.
- [Don't Blind Your VLA: Aligning Visual Representations for OOD Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25616): Aligns visual representations to improve VLA out-of-distribution generalization, making perception quality a model-side robustness lever.
- [Learning to Plan & Schedule with Reinforcement-Learned Bimanual Robot Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25634): Learns bimanual skills with reinforcement learning and composes them through planning and scheduling for longer-horizon manipulation.
- [Robotic Assistant: Completing Collaborative Tasks with Dexterous Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25713): Adapts OpenVLA for dexterous collaboration with FiLM visual conditioning, an auxiliary human-intent head, and compact delta-action post-processing for hand commands.
- [SpikeATac: A Multimodal Tactile Finger with Taxelized Dynamic Sensing for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.27048): Combines dynamic PVDF taxels and capacitive sensing, then fine-tunes dexterous force modulation with human-feedback RL and tactile rewards for fragile in-hand manipulation.
- [Learning Generalizable Visuomotor Policy through Dynamics-Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.27114): Introduces Dynamics-Aligned Flow Matching Policy, where policy and dynamics models provide mutual corrective feedback during action generation for OOD manipulation robustness.
- [Toward a Multi-Embodied Grasping Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.27420): Builds a flow-based equivariant grasp synthesis model that conditions on gripper and scene geometry to handle multiple end-effector designs with shared training.
- [EBT-Policy: Energy Unlocks Emergent Physical Reasoning Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.27545): Scales energy-based transformers into robot policies, using learned energy landscapes to reduce exposure bias and stabilize physically grounded action inference.
- [Dual-Stream Diffusion for World-Model Augmented Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.27607): World-model-augmented VLA method using dual-stream diffusion for embodied action modeling.
- [Shared-Autonomy Arm-Hand VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.00139): Combines VR teleoperation with autonomous hand VLA policies to collect data for end-to-end dexterous arm-hand control.
- [BOOM / Bootstrap Off-policy with World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.00423): Integrates online planning and off-policy RL through a learned world model. Core idea: the policy initializes the planner, the planner improves actions through simulated futures, and a likelihood-free alignment loss plus soft value weighting bootstraps the policy from non-parametric planner behavior without letting planning data drift away from the policy.
- [A Unified Framework to Learn Collision-Free Loco-Manipulation via Adversarial Motion Priors](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11245884): Model and control move record; it combines trajectory-optimized loco-manipulation motion priors with an MPPI trajectory generator and vector follower for collision-free quadruped manipulation.
- [Enhancing multi-agent reinforcement learning via world model assisted single-agent population policies in multi-UAV cooperative-competitive scenario](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.knosys.2025.114534): Model and control move record; it combines a world model, single-agent population policies, and evolutionary search to improve MARL exploration in multi-UAV cooperative-competitive games.
- [H-RIL: A Hopf-based Robot Imitation Learning Framework for Modular Antenna Structure Assembly](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.knosys.2025.114576): Model and control move record; H-RIL records kinesthetic demonstrations, learns segmented adaptive oscillatory primitives, stitches them smoothly, and locally adjusts Hopf-based primitives for modular antenna assembly.
- [Knowing More, Acting Better: Hierarchical Representation for Embodied Decision-Making](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.findings-emnlp.1042): Uses hierarchical representations for embodied decision-making, connecting richer state knowledge with stronger action selection in embodied environments.
- [OmniVLA: Physically-Grounded Multimodal VLA with Unified Multi-Sensor Perception for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.01210): Grounds VLA policies in unified multi-sensor perception for robotic manipulation.
- [RobustVLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.01331): Applies robustness-aware reinforcement post-training to VLA models for reliability under visual and environment variation.
- [SE(3)-PoseFlow: Estimating 6D Pose Distributions for Uncertainty-Aware Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.01501): Uses flow matching on the SE(3) manifold to sample multimodal 6D pose distributions, supporting active perception and uncertainty-aware grasp synthesis.
- [Unified Diffusion VLA: Vision-Language-Action Model via Joint Discrete Denoising Diffusion Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.01718): Uses a joint discrete denoising diffusion process for vision-language-action modeling, extending diffusion language ideas to embodied control.
- [GenDexHand: Generative Simulation for Dexterous Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.01791): Generates dexterous-hand simulation tasks with VLM feedback over object placement and scale, then decomposes tasks for sequential RL training.
- [TWIST2: Scalable, Portable, and Holistic Humanoid Data Collection System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.02832): Provides a portable humanoid data-collection system and hierarchical egocentric visuomotor policy for whole-body dexterous and mobile skills.
- [BFM-Zero: A Promptable Behavioral Foundation Model for Humanoid Control Using Unsupervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.04131): Learns a shared latent space over motions, goals, and rewards so one Unitree G1 humanoid policy can be prompted for zero-shot and few-shot whole-body skills.
- [ReGen: Generative Robot Simulation via Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.04769): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for embodied and VLA agents. Core idea: focus on generative robot simulation via inverse design as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [Unified Humanoid Fall-Safety Policy from a Few Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07407): Learns a shared humanoid fall-safety policy from a small number of demonstrations, targeting recovery and injury-avoidance behaviors across falls.
- [Robot Learning from a Physical World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07416): Couples task-conditioned video generation with physical world reconstruction, then uses object-centric residual RL to ground generated pixel motion into robot actions.
- [CAVER: Curious Audiovisual Exploring Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07619): Uses a 3D-printed end-effector to excite object sounds and a curiosity objective over audiovisual uncertainty, building representations for material recognition and audio-only imitation.
- [Dynamic Sparsity: Challenging Common Sparsity Assumptions for Learning World Models in Robotic Reinforcement Learning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.08086): Re-examines sparsity assumptions in learned world models for robotic reinforcement learning benchmarks, clarifying model-design choices for embodied control.
- [PerspAct: Enhancing LLM Situated Collaboration Skills through Perspective Taking and Active Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.08098): Tests ReAct-style perspective taking with active visual exploration so LLMs can ground other agents' viewpoints in situated collaboration tasks.
- [TiDAR: Think in Diffusion, Talk in Autoregression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.08923): Evidence-needed for this Embodied/VLA placement: the visible arXiv abstract describes a hybrid diffusion/autoregressive text-generation architecture, not robot action learning.
- [WMPO: World Model-based Policy Optimization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.09515): Uses pixel-based world-model rollouts aligned with VLA visual features to run on-policy GRPO for robot policies without real-environment interaction.
- [SpatialActor: Exploring Disentangled Spatial Representations for Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.09555): Decouples semantic and geometric representations with a semantic-guided geometric module to improve precise manipulation under noisy real-world depth.
- [AffordBot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.10017): Uses multimodal large language models for fine-grained 3D embodied affordance reasoning.
- [Collaborative Multi-Robot Non-Prehensile Manipulation via Flow-Matching Co-Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.10874): Uses flow-matching co-generation to synthesize coordinated multi-robot pushing or sliding motions for non-prehensile manipulation.
- [AdaptPNP: Integrating Prehensile and Non-Prehensile Skills for Adaptive Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11052): Uses a VLM-empowered task-and-motion planner to select, sequence, and combine prehensile and non-prehensile manipulation primitives.
- [Rethinking Progression of Memory State in Robotic Manipulation: An Object-Centric Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11478): Reframes robotic manipulation memory progression around object-centric state.
- [Collaborative Representation Learning for Alignment of Tactile, Language, and Vision Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11512): Aligns tactile, language, and vision features with a CLIP-style model, sensor-aware modulation, tactile-irrelevant decoupling, and a unified bridging adapter.
- [Task-Aware 3D Affordance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11702): Uses 2D guidance and geometry refinement for task-aware 3D affordance segmentation, connecting spatial perception to action-relevant embodied reasoning.
- [High-Consistency Embodied World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.12882): Uses multi-view trajectory videos to improve embodied world-model consistency, targeting action-conditioned physical scene prediction.
- [SplatSearch: Instance Image Goal Navigation for Mobile Robots using 3D Gaussian Splatting and Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.12972): Builds 3D Gaussian-splat scene maps and diffusion-based search policies for mobile robots navigating to a target instance image.
- [Towards Deploying VLA Without Fine-Tuning: Plug-and-Play Inference-Time VLA Policy Steering via Embodied Evolutionary Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14178): Steers frozen VLA policies at inference time by using evolutionary diffusion to search action candidates against embodied task feedback, avoiding task-specific fine-tuning.
- [Continuous Vision-Language-Action Co-Learning with Semantic-Physical Alignment for Behavioral Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14396): Co-trains visual, language, and action representations with semantic-physical alignment so behavioral cloning policies preserve both instruction meaning and robot-motion feasibility.
- [Self-Supervised Multisensory Pretraining for Contact-Rich Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14427): Pretrains visual, tactile, proprioceptive, and force representations with self-supervised objectives before contact-rich robot reinforcement learning.
- [NORA-1.5: A Vision-Language-Action Model Trained using World Model- and Action-based Preference Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14659): Extends NORA with a flow-matching action expert and post-trains VLA behavior using both action-conditioned world-model rewards and action deviation rewards.
- [Look, Zoom, Understand: The Robotic Eyeball for Embodied Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.15279): Models language-guided active visual perception as pan-tilt-zoom camera control. Core idea: make sensing itself a VLA action so embodied agents can decide where and at what scale to look before reasoning or acting.
- [In-N-On: Scaling Egocentric Manipulation with in-the-wild and on-task Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.15704): Builds PHSD egocentric data and trains the language-conditioned Human0 flow-matching policy with domain adaptation from humans to humanoids.
- [Bi-AQUA: Bilateral Control-Based Imitation Learning for Underwater Robot Arms via Lighting-Aware Action Chunking with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16050): Combines bilateral-control imitation learning with lighting-aware action chunking transformers for robot-arm manipulation in challenging visual conditions.
- [EvoVLA: Self-Evolving Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16166): Addresses long-horizon VLA stage hallucination with contrastive stage-aligned rewards, pose-based object exploration, and gated long-horizon memory.
- [DynaMimicGen: A Data Generation Framework for Robot Learning of Dynamic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16223): Generates robot-learning data for dynamic tasks, extending demonstration synthesis beyond quasi-static manipulation.
- [InternData-A1: Pioneering High-Fidelity Synthetic Data for Pre-training Generalist Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16651): Builds high-fidelity synthetic data for pre-training generalist policies, adding a scalable data route for embodied model learning.
- [Dexterity from Smart Lenses: Multi-Fingered Robot Manipulation with In-the-Wild Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16661): Learns multi-fingered robot manipulation from in-the-wild human demonstrations, extending dexterous policy learning beyond curated lab demonstrations.
- [Efficient Robot Design With Multi-Objective Black-Box Optimization and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.17178): Combines LLM-guided proposals with multi-objective black-box optimization to search robot designs under competing performance constraints.
- [Agility Meets Stability: Versatile Humanoid Control with Heterogeneous Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.17373): Combines human motion data and synthetic balance motions to train one humanoid policy covering agile skills and stability-critical behaviors.
- [SFHand: Learning Embodied Manipulation by Streaming Egocentric 3D Hand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18127): Forecasts future 3D hand type, boxes, pose, and trajectory from streaming egocentric video plus language using an autoregressive architecture with ROI-enhanced memory.
- [Observer Actor: Active Vision Imitation Learning with Sparse View Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18140): Combines sparse-view Gaussian splatting with an active-vision imitation learner, letting the policy choose informative viewpoints before executing manipulation actions.
- [UNeMo: Collaborative Visual-Language Reasoning and Navigation via a Multimodal World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18845): Uses a multimodal world model to support collaborative visual-language reasoning and navigation across agents.
- [Robot-Powered Data Flywheels: Deploying Robots in the Wild for Continual Data Collection and Foundation Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19647): Deploys Scanford in a library to collect robot-labeled shelf images, then fine-tunes VLMs for book identification and multilingual OCR from that data.
- [Dual-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive and Cost-Aware Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.21083): Trains dual reinforcement-learning agents to adapt visual-inertial odometry while balancing localization accuracy against sensing or compute cost.
- [Sampling-Based Optimization with Parallelized Physics Simulator for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.21264): Uses parallel physics simulation inside sampling-based optimization to search feasible bimanual manipulation actions.
- [From Observation to Action: Latent Action-based Primitive Segmentation for VLA Pre-training in Industrial Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.21428): Uses a motion tokenizer and Latent Action Energy segmenter to turn unlabeled industrial videos into primitive clips and latent action sequences for VLA pretraining.
- [VacuumVLA: Boosting VLA Capabilities via a Unified Suction and Gripping Tool for Complex Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.21557): Extends VLA manipulation with a unified suction-and-gripper end effector that can switch or combine contact modes for tasks ordinary two-finger grippers fail.
- [Reinforcement Learning from Implicit Neural Feedback for Human-Aligned Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00050): Introduces model-side work on reinforcement Learning from Implicit Neural Feedback for Human-Aligned Robot Control, fitting training, architecture, post-training, synthetic-data, verifier, reward, or world-model coverage.
- [Sigma: The Key for Vision-Language-Action Models toward Telepathic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00783): A trained vision-language-action model that introduces an updatable mediating thought space between semantics and continuous control.
- [H-Zero: Cross-Humanoid Locomotion Pretraining Enables Few-shot Novel Embodiment Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00971): Pretrains locomotion across humanoid embodiments so a new body can adapt from only a few demonstrations or trials.
- [FOM-Nav: Frontier-Object Maps for Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01009): Represents unexplored frontiers together with object hypotheses in maps for object-goal navigation.
- [Modality-Augmented Fine-Tuning of Foundation Robot Policies for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation on GR1 and G1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01358): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [GR-RL: Going Dexterous and Precise for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01801): Trains dexterous long-horizon robotic manipulation policies with reinforcement learning.
- [SARL: Spatially-Aware Self-Supervised Representation Learning for Visuo-Tactile Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01908): Learns spatially aware visual-tactile representations with self-supervised objectives for contact-rich perception.
- [Learning Dexterous Manipulation Skills from Imperfect Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02011): Trains dexterous skills by using imperfect simulations to induce finger-gait primitives, collecting tactile teleoperation demos, and behavior-cloning tactile policies for nut-bolt and screwdriver tasks.
- [ManualVLA: A Unified VLA Model for Chain-of-Thought Manual Generation and Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02013): Unifies chain-of-thought manual generation and robotic manipulation, extending manual-grounded embodied models.
- [EfficientFlow: Efficient Equivariant Flow Policy Learning for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02020): Trains equivariant flow policies for embodied control, using symmetry structure to improve policy efficiency.
- [Steering Vision-Language-Action Models as Anti-Exploration: A Test-Time Scaling Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02834): Uses test-time scaling to steer vision-language-action models away from unproductive exploration.
- [Video2Act: A Dual-System Video Diffusion Policy with Robotic Spatio-Motional Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03044): Video2Act proposes a dual-system video diffusion policy for robotic spatio-motional action modeling.
- [PerFACT: Motion Policy with LLM-Powered Dataset Synthesis and Fusion Action-Chunking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03444): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for embodied and VLA agents. Core idea: focus on motion policy with llm-powered dataset synthesis and fusion action-chunking transformers as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [RoboScape-R: Unified Reward-Observation World Models for Generalizable Robotics Training via RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03556): Uses a world model as a general reward-observation proxy for RL, aiming to train embodied policies across diverse scenes without hand-crafted task rewards.
- [House of Dextra: Cross-embodied Co-design for Dexterous Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03743): Co-designs dexterous hands across embodiments, linking hand morphology choices with transferable manipulation policies.
- [FALCON: Actively Decoupled Visuomotor Policies for Loco-Manipulation with Foundation-Model-Based Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04381): Decouples locomotion and manipulation into modular diffusion policies coordinated by a vision-language foundation model and phase-progress head.
- [Open-Ended Goal Inference through Actions and Language for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04453): Infers open-ended human goals from observed actions and language so collaborative robots can adapt task assistance.
- [From Generated Human Videos to Physically Plausible Robot Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05094): Converts generated human videos into physically plausible humanoid robot trajectories.
- [STARE-VLA: Progressive Stage-Aware Reinforcement for Fine-Tuning Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05107): Uses progressive stage-aware reinforcement learning to fine-tune vision-language-action models.
- [VideoVLA: Video Generators Can Be Generalizable Robot Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.06963): Turns video generators into generalizable robot manipulators, linking video-generation priors with embodied action policies.
- [Hand Interaction Timeline Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07394): reconstructs objects along hand-interaction timelines in egocentric video, supporting embodied manipulation perception.
- [InterAgent: Physics-based Multi-agent Command Execution via Diffusion on Interaction Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07410): Uses an autoregressive diffusion transformer over interaction graphs to execute text-driven physics-based multi-humanoid coordination commands.
- [Ground Slow, Move Fast: A Dual-System Foundation Model for Generalizable Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08186): Uses a dual-system foundation model for generalizable vision-and-language navigation.
- [Semantic-Metric Bayesian Risk Fields: Learning Robot Safety from Human Videos with a VLM Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08233): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [Audio World Models for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08405): Learns robot manipulation policies from audio world models.
- [ViTA-Seg: Vision Transformer for Amodal Segmentation in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.09510): Applies a vision Transformer to amodal robotic segmentation so policies can reason about occluded object extents.
- [ReMoSPLAT: Reactive Mobile Manipulation Control on a Gaussian Splat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.09656): Uses a Gaussian-splat scene representation as the control substrate for reactive mobile manipulation.
- [HiF-VLA: Hindsight, Insight and Foresight through Motion Representation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.09928): Uses hindsight, insight, and foresight motion representations to strengthen vision-language-action models.
- [Iterative Compositional Data Generation for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10891): Factorizes robot-control transitions into robot, object, obstacle, and objective components with a compositional diffusion transformer, then validates synthetic data through offline RL.
- [WholeBodyVLA: Towards Unified Latent VLA for Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11047): Targets unified latent VLA control for whole-body loco-manipulation, expanding VLA beyond arm-only manipulation.
- [Mirror Skin: In Situ Visualization of Robot Touch Intent on Robotic Skin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11472): Visualizes intended touch directly on robot skin, making contact plans observable during physical human-robot interaction.
- [Cross-Entropy Optimization of Physically Grounded Task and Motion Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11571): Optimizes physically grounded task-and-motion plans with cross-entropy search.
- [BLURR: A Boosted Low-Resource Inference for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11769): Boosted low-resource inference for VLA models is an embodied/VLA model candidate, not a multilingual item.
- [Agile Flight Emerges from Multi-Agent Competitive Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11781): Studies agile flight emerging from multi-agent competitive racing, relevant to embodied action learning in executable environments.
- [AnchorDream: Repurposing Video Diffusion for Embodiment-Aware Robot Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11797): Conditions pretrained video diffusion on robot motion renderings, anchoring embodiment and kinematics while synthesizing diverse robot demonstrations from a few teleoperation examples.
- [Towards Accessible Physical AI: LoRA-Based Fine-Tuning of VLA Models for Real-World Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11921): Applies LoRA and quantization to adapt multi-billion-parameter VLA models to low-cost robots and consumer GPUs with limited embodiment-specific data.
- [Semantic Zone based 3D Map Management for Mobile Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12228): Manages mobile-robot 3D maps through semantic zones, giving navigation systems a structured scene-memory representation.
- [D3D-VLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12622): builds a dynamic 3D vision-language-planning model for embodied grounding and navigation.
- [Motus: A Unified Latent Action World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13030): Builds a unified latent action world model, directly connecting learned dynamics with embodied action generation.
- [PvP: Data-Efficient Humanoid Robot Learning with Proprioceptive-Privileged Contrastive Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13093): Uses proprioceptive-privileged contrastive representations for data-efficient humanoid robot learning, improving policy transfer from limited experience.
- [Computer vision training dataset generation for robotic environments using Gaussian splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13411): Uses Gaussian-splat reconstructions to generate labeled computer-vision training data for robotic environments.
- [ARCADE: Adaptive Robot Control with Online Changepoint-Aware Bayesian Dynamics Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14331): Learns Bayesian robot dynamics online with changepoint awareness so controllers can adapt when environment dynamics shift.
- [ISS Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15020): Adds implicit scene supervision to a 3D visuomotor diffusion policy so actions stay consistent with scene geometry over time.
- [Large Model Enabled Embodied Intelligence for 6G Integrated Perception, Communication, and Computation Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15109): Positions large models as the embodied-intelligence layer for 6G networks that integrate perception, communication, and computation.
- [Emergence: Overcoming Privileged Information Bias in Asymmetric Embodied Agents via Active Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15776): Contributes a model, training, inference, or data-generation approach for embodied agents and vision-language-action systems.
- [Few-Shot Inference of Human Perceptions of Robot Performance in Social Navigation Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16019): Introduces model-side work on few-Shot Inference of Human Perceptions of Robot Performance in Social Navigation Scenarios, fitting the repository's training, architecture, post-training, verifier, or synthetic-data coverage.
- [PhysBrain: Human Egocentric Data as a Bridge from Vision Language Models to Physical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16793): Uses egocentric human data as a bridge from VLMs to physical intelligence.
- [ReinforceGen: Hybrid Skill Policies with Automated Data Generation and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16861): Decomposes long-horizon manipulation into localized skills, generates demonstration data, initializes with imitation and motion planning, then fine-tunes components with RL.
- [Mitty: Diffusion-based Human-to-Robot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.17253): Uses a diffusion transformer for in-context human-to-robot video generation, fusing human-demo condition tokens with robot denoising tokens to avoid keypoint or trajectory bottlenecks.
- [Vidarc: Embodied Video Diffusion Model for Closed-loop Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.17661): Adds masked inverse dynamics to autoregressive embodied video diffusion so predicted videos are grounded for closed-loop robot-arm control.
- [STORM: Search-Guided Generative World Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.18477): Uses search-guided generative world models for robotic manipulation, linking world-model search with action policy learning.
- [Real2Edit2Real: Generating Robotic Demonstrations via a 3D Control Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19402): Reconstructs metric 3D scenes from multi-view RGB, edits point-cloud trajectories and robot poses, then synthesizes new demonstrations for spatial generalization.
- [Learning Skills from Action-Free Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.20052): Learns reusable robot skills from videos without action labels by inferring the latent actions needed to reproduce observed behavior.
- [ParaMaP: Parallel Mapping and Collision-free Motion Planning for Reactive Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22575): Runs mapping and collision-free motion planning in parallel so manipulation policies can react to changing scenes.
- [Dream-VL and Dream-VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22615): Builds open diffusion-language vision and VLA models, using a diffusion language-model backbone for visual planning and robotic action generation.
- [EgoReAct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22808): generates 3D human reactions from egocentric video, connecting first-person perception with embodied motion synthesis.
- [Unified Embodied VLM Reasoning with Robotic Action via Autoregressive Discretized Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24125): Connects unified embodied VLM reasoning with robotic action through autoregressive discretized pretraining.
- [Counterfactual VLA: Self-Reflective Vision-Language-Action Model with Adaptive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24426): Adds counterfactual self-reflection and adaptive reasoning to vision-language-action models.
- [Dual-Level Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Representation](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11247270): Builds instance-aware open-vocabulary 3D scene representations for robot navigation.
- [AffordStruct](https://doi.org/10.1109/TASE.2025.3586983): grounds affordances through spatial interaction and knowledge-aware weak supervision, linking 3D perception with action-relevant object use.
- [Learning Autonomous and Safe Quadruped Traversal of Complex Terrains Using Multi-Layer Elevation Maps](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2025.3595814): Model and control move record; a hierarchical quadruped controller uses multi-layer elevation maps, terrain augmentation, distillation, and shaped rewards for safe cluttered-terrain traversal.
- [Low-Rank Adaptation-Based All-Weather Removal for Autonomous Navigation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icra55743.2025.11127411): Model and perception move record; LoRA-Align adapts all-weather image-restoration models to unseen degradations while preserving performance on pre-trained weather conditions.
- [SynPER: Synthesized prioritized experience replay for USVs formation control in island-reef waters via multi-agent reinforcement learning](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.oceaneng.2025.124080): Model and control move record; SynPER formulates multi-USV formation as an MDP and combines a KAN-MLP hybrid network, asynchronous multi-agent hindsight replay, and agent-specific priority sampling.
- [TVT-Transformer](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.inffus.2025.102943): fuses tactile, visual, and textual signals for object recognition.
- [Hierarchical Diffusion Policy](https://doi.org/10.1109/TRO.2025.3547272): generates manipulation trajectories with contact-guided hierarchical diffusion.
- [HOZ++ Object Navigation](https://doi.org/10.1109/TPAMI.2025.3552987): uses hierarchical object-to-zone graphs for object navigation, linking scene structure with embodied navigation decisions.
- [Dexterous Twisting via Skill Decomposition](https://doi.org/10.1360/sst-2025-0295): learns dexterous twisting through motor-skill decomposition, fitting embodied policy models rather than packaged agent skills.
- [TactileAloha: Learning Bimanual Manipulation With Tactile Sensing](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2025.3585396): Adds tactile sensing to bimanual manipulation learning, strengthening contact-rich embodied policy training beyond vision-only imitation.
- [GPT-Driven Gestures: Leveraging Large Language Models to Generate Expressive Robot Motion for Enhanced Human-Robot Interaction](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2025.3547631): Uses large language models to generate expressive robot motions for human-robot interaction, adding an embodied motion-generation line beyond manipulation-only VLA policies.
- [What Drives Success in Physical Planning with Joint-Embedding Predictive World Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24497): Analyzes success factors for physical planning with joint-embedding predictive world models, relevant to embodied model design.
- [Exploring the Domain-Invariant Flow Representation in Vision-Based Tactile Sensors for Omni-Hardness Perception](https://doi.org/10.1109/icra55743.2025.11128719): Studies flow representations that transfer across vision-based tactile sensors for hardness perception, supporting tactile policy inputs beyond one sensor design.
- [PartGrasp: Generalizable Part-level Grasping via Semantic-Geometric Alignment](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11246282): Aligns semantic part cues with geometric grasp structure to generalize part-level grasping across objects.
- [Can Vision Feel Touch? Tactile-Aware Visual Grasping for Transparent Objects](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3598373): Injects tactile awareness into visual grasping so robots can handle transparent objects whose geometry is hard to infer from RGB alone.
- [Task Guided Multimodal Object Search for Service Robots via Reinforcement Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/tie.2025.3584983): Trains reinforcement-learning policies for service-robot object search using task guidance and multimodal observations.
- [PIDiff: Integrating a High-Performance Transformer Into Diffusion Models for Robust and Efficient Imitation Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2025.3640983): Integrates a high-performance Transformer into diffusion policies to improve robustness and inference efficiency in imitation learning.
- [Tacser and Action-Conditioned Latent Filter for Generalizable Robotic Surface Perception](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2025.3626240): Combines Tacser surface perception with an action-conditioned latent filter so tactile surface models generalize across robot interactions.
- [Self-Supervised Complementary Learning between Vision and Tactility by Probing Action into an Open-Mouth Container](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11245918): Uses probing actions into open-mouth containers to learn complementary visual and tactile representations without manual labels.
- [VLM-MSGraph: Vision Language Model-enabled Multi-hierarchical Scene Graph for robotic assembly](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.rcim.2025.102978): Builds multi-level scene graphs with VLM support so robotic assembly systems can reason over parts, relations, and task structure.
- [RACCOON: Grounding Embodied Question-Answering with State Summaries from Existing Robot Modules](https://doi.org/10.1109/icra55743.2025.11127843): Grounds embodied question answering by converting existing robot-module states into summaries that a language model can use.
- [Social-LLaVA: Enhancing Social Robot Navigation through Human-Language Reasoning](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11247618): Adapts LLaVA-style human-language reasoning to social robot navigation, where the policy must account for people and social context.
- [Toward the next frontier of embodied AI](https://doi.org/10.20517/ir.2025.44): Outlines embodied-AI model directions around integrating perception, action, and foundation-model reasoning rather than adding a single benchmark.
- [A mixed reality-assisted human-to-robot skill transfer approach for contact-rich assembly via visuomotor primitives](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.rcim.2025.103208): Uses mixed-reality teaching to transfer human contact-rich assembly skills into visuomotor primitives for robot execution.
- [Quart-Online: Latency-Free Multimodal Large Language Model for Quadruped Robot Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/icra55743.2025.11127693): Targets quadruped learning with a latency-free multimodal language model, connecting onboard perception and language-conditioned robot control.
- [The Role of Visual Augmentation on Embodied Skill Acquisition Across Perspectives and Body Representations](https://doi.org/10.1109/tvcg.2025.3616832): Studies how visual augmentation, viewpoint, and body representation affect embodied skill acquisition in immersive training settings.
- [AffordGen: Affordance-Based Dataset Generator for Robot Manipulation Learning*](https://doi.org/10.1109/icar65334.2025.11338672): Generates affordance-centered robot-manipulation datasets, making action-relevant object regions an explicit supervision signal for policy learning.
- [Automatic Real-to-Sim-to-Real System through Iterative Interactions for Robust Robot Manipulation Policy Learning with Unseen Objects](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11247488): Iterates between real interaction, simulation construction, and real-robot deployment to learn manipulation policies for unseen objects.
- [Scene Graph-Based Spatial Reasoning with VLM for High-Level Robotic Tasks](https://doi.org/10.1109/icarm65671.2025.11293747): Uses scene graphs with a vision-language model to structure spatial relations for high-level robotic task reasoning.
- [Adaptive Behavior Learning for Embodied Agents in Virtual Environments via Proximal Policy Optimization](https://doi.org/10.1109/iotaima66468.2025.11212750): Trains embodied-agent behaviors in virtual environments with PPO, making policy adaptation the core model-side contribution.
- [LLM-Enhanced Mobile Robot Navigation and Scene Description for Indoor Environments](https://doi.org/10.1109/orss66051.2025.11121634): Couples LLM reasoning with indoor mobile-robot navigation and scene description, linking route decisions to language-level environment summaries.
- [Foundation Models Meet Spatial Representations: Advancing Zero-Shot Navigation for Embodied AI](https://doi.org/10.1109/swc65939.2025.00227): Combines foundation-model priors with spatial representations to support zero-shot embodied navigation.
- [From a Binary Feature Matrix to Correlation Analysis: A Dual-Paradigm Classification of Global Robotics Research Objectives](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2025.3604842): Classifies global robotics research objectives with binary feature matrices and correlation analysis; this reads more like a survey or meta-analysis than a model method.
- [3D Gaussian Map with Open-Set Semantic Grouping for Vision-Language Navigation](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.00864): Groups open-set semantics inside 3D Gaussian maps so VLN agents can navigate with object-level scene structure.
- [Learning force-conditioned visuomotor diffusion policy from human demonstrations for complex robotic assembly tasks](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.mfglet.2025.06.171): Conditions visuomotor diffusion policies on force signals from human demonstrations for contact-rich assembly.
- [UDI-3DVT: A Novel Network for 3-D Reconstruction via Uncertainty-Driven Sampling and Incremental Visual-Tactile Fusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/jsen.2025.3531929): Reconstructs 3D shape with uncertainty-driven sampling and incremental visual-tactile fusion.
- [8-DoFs Cable Driven Parallel Robots for Bimanual Teleoperation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmech.2025.3610192): Designs an eight-DoF cable-driven parallel robot mechanism for bimanual teleoperation, mainly contributing embodied hardware.
- [I Love Lemurs! What’s Your Favorite Animal? : Generating Personality-Driven Conversations for the Tabletop Robot Haru](https://doi.org/10.1109/ro-man63969.2025.11217859): Generates personality-driven dialogue for the Haru tabletop robot, an embodied social-interaction model rather than manipulation policy work.
- [Physically-Grounded 3D Point Cloud Filtering and Clustering Based on Tactile Sensor Specifications](https://doi.org/10.1109/ist66504.2025.11268392): Filters and clusters 3D point clouds using tactile-sensor specifications so perception matches physically reachable contact regions.
- [Tactile-Driven Dexterous In-Hand Writing via Extrinsic Contact Sensing](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2025.3589168): Uses extrinsic tactile contact sensing to control dexterous in-hand writing motions.
- [Category-Level 6-D Object Pose Estimation With Learnable Prior Embeddings for Robotic Grasping](https://doi.org/10.1109/tie.2025.3555019): Learns prior embeddings for category-level 6D pose estimation used by robotic grasping policies.
- [ViTaDex: Vision–Tactile Fusion for 6-D Object-in-Hand Pose Estimation in Dexterous Anthropomorphic Manipulation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tii.2025.3646626): Fuses vision and tactile signals to estimate 6D in-hand object pose during dexterous anthropomorphic manipulation.
- [PACR: Point-Axis Constraint Reasoning for Enhanced Robotic Manipulation with Dexterity and Compliance](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11246460): Reasons over point-axis constraints so manipulation policies can combine dexterity with compliant contact.
- [Integrating With Multimodal Information for Enhancing Robotic Grasping With Vision-Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/tase.2025.3550360): Enhances VLM-based robotic grasping by integrating multimodal cues instead of relying on language-conditioned vision alone.
- [Large vision-language models enabled novel objects 6D pose estimation for human-robot collaboration](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.rcim.2025.103030): Uses large vision-language models to estimate 6D poses of novel objects for human-robot collaboration.
- [Perception-decision-execution coordination mechanism driven dynamic autonomous collaboration method for human-like collaborative robot based on multimodal large language model](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.rcim.2025.103167): Coordinates perception, decision, and execution with a multimodal LLM for dynamic human-like robot collaboration.
- [Robotic Assembly of Deformable Linear Objects via Curriculum Reinforcement Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2025.3553676): Uses curriculum reinforcement learning for robotic assembly of deformable linear objects.
- [VLM-GroNav: Robot Navigation Using Physically Grounded Vision-Language Models in Outdoor Environments](https://doi.org/10.1109/icra55743.2025.11128264): Grounds outdoor navigation decisions in physical constraints through a vision-language model.
- [Memory-MambaNav: Enhancing object-goal navigation through integration of spatial-temporal scanning with state space models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.imavis.2025.105522): Integrates spatial-temporal scanning with Mamba-style state-space models for object-goal navigation memory.
- [LLM-Powered Operator Advisor for Human-Robot Collaboration in Sustainable Assembly-Disassembly Cells](https://doi.org/10.1109/ichms65439.2025.11154334): Uses an LLM operator advisor to support human-robot collaboration in assembly and disassembly workcells.
- [An Interaction Specification Language for Robot Application Development](https://doi.org/10.1109/hri61500.2025.10973839): Defines an interaction specification language for robot application development; this is closer to programming infrastructure than a learned model.
- [A Continual Learning Method for Generalized Grasping Manipulation in a Musculoskeletal Robot](https://doi.org/10.1109/tase.2025.3569402): Applies continual learning to generalized grasping with a musculoskeletal robot embodiment.
- [VT-FSC: Vision-Tactile Fusion Semantic Communication for Remote Embodied Intelligence](https://doi.org/10.1109/lcomm.2025.3642793): Compresses and transmits fused visual-tactile semantics for remote embodied intelligence.
- [No Robot is an Island: An Always-On Cognitive Architecture for Social Context Awareness in Dynamic Environments*](https://doi.org/10.1109/icdl63968.2025.11204444): Proposes an always-on cognitive architecture for maintaining social context awareness in dynamic robot environments.
- [Seeing Eye Stretch: Robot-assisted Indoor Navigation for Blind or Low-Vision Individuals](https://doi.org/10.1109/ur65550.2025.11078138): Builds robot-assisted indoor navigation for blind or low-vision users, emphasizing embodied assistance rather than general VLA training.
- [My Robot, My Motion: Expressive Real-Time Teleoperation](https://doi.org/10.1109/hri61500.2025.10974103): Maps user motion into expressive real-time robot teleoperation.
- [Synthesizing Depowdering Trajectories for Robot Arms using Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/icra55743.2025.11128066): Synthesizes robot-arm depowdering trajectories with deep reinforcement learning.
- [$\mathcal{D}(\mathcal{R}, \mathcal{O})$ Grasp: A Unified Representation of Robot and Object Interaction for Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Grasping](https://doi.org/10.1109/icra55743.2025.11127754): Represents robot-object interactions jointly for cross-embodiment dexterous grasping.
- [CoRe: A Hybrid Approach of Contact-Aware Optimization and Learning for Humanoid Robot Motions](https://doi.org/10.1109/humanoids65713.2025.11203055): Combines contact-aware optimization with learned components for humanoid motion generation.
- [A monocular omnidirectional vision-based method for structured light strip extraction and robot target localization under complex interference](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.asej.2025.103959): Extracts structured-light strips from monocular omnidirectional vision for robot target localization under interference.
- [Robot Active Task Cognition: Situation-Aware Task Planning With Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3637229): Uses LLM-based situation awareness for active task cognition and high-level robot planning.
- [Large-Language-Model-Aided Assistive Robot for Single-Operator Bimanual Teleoperation: Introduction and Validation of a Flexible Assistance System](https://doi.org/10.1109/mra.2025.3642669): Adds LLM assistance to single-operator bimanual teleoperation, helping one human supervise flexible two-arm control.
- [M3Fusion: a unified multi-view multi-modality fusion framework for embodied 3D perception](https://doi.org/10.1360/ssi-2025-0293): Unifies multi-view and multimodal fusion for embodied 3D perception.
- [Leveraging Embodied Mechanical Intelligence for Learning Decluttering Tasks: Gripper Design Boosts Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/mra.2025.3639825): Shows how gripper mechanical design changes learning efficiency for robotic decluttering tasks.
- [Embodied Navigation in Unknown Environments With Implicit Scene Memory and Target-Aware Memory Retrieval](https://doi.org/10.1109/tai.2025.3618793): Uses implicit scene memory and target-aware retrieval to navigate unknown environments.
- [EIRM-RL: Epistemic Integrity Risk Monitoring Inspired Safe Reinforcement Learning for Trustworthy Autonomous Navigation](https://doi.org/10.1109/jiot.2025.3633765): Adds epistemic-integrity risk monitoring to safe reinforcement learning for autonomous navigation.
- [Category-controllable and high-fidelity 3D defect synthesis for Embodied Intelligence-based industrial inspection](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jii.2025.101016): Synthesizes category-controllable 3D defects for embodied industrial inspection training data.
- [Use Case Development for Robot-Assisted Nuclear Power Plant Operation and Maintenance Using Domain-Specific LLM](https://doi.org/10.1080/00295450.2025.2532870): Uses a domain-specific LLM to develop robot-assisted nuclear-plant operation and maintenance use cases; this is a narrow domain application.
- [Dependable Embodied Intelligence: Toward Trustworthy Integration of Large Language Models and World Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/dsa66321.2025.00071): Studies dependable integration of LLMs and world models for trustworthy embodied systems.
- [RDMM: Enhancing Household Robotics with On-Device Contextual Memory and Decision Making](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11246000): Adds on-device contextual memory and decision making for household robots.
- [Flow-Guided Policies: Overcoming Diffusion Limitations for Robust Robot Imitation Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccvw69036.2025.00261): Introduces model-side work on flow-Guided Policies: Overcoming Diffusion Limitations for Robust Robot Imitation Learning, fitting training, architecture, post-training, synthetic-data, verifier, reward, or world-model coverage.
- [AutoSkill: Hierarchical Open-Ended Skill Acquisition for Long-Horizon Manipulation Tasks via Language-Modulated Rewards](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcds.2025.3551298): Learns long-horizon manipulation skills hierarchically with language-modulated rewards and open-ended acquisition.
- [OVA-Fields: Weakly Supervised Open-Vocabulary Affordance Fields for Robot Operational Part Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.00602): Learns open-vocabulary affordance fields for detecting operational object parts under weak supervision.
- [Mamba2Diff: An enhanced diffusion framework for goal-conditioned imitation learning in robotic long-horizon action modeling](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.knosys.2025.114990): Combines Mamba-style sequence modeling with diffusion for goal-conditioned long-horizon robotic imitation learning.
- [Refer and Grasp: Vision-Language Guided Continuous Dexterous Grasping](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11247415): Uses vision-language references to guide continuous dexterous grasp generation.
- [RoG-SAM: A Language-Driven Framework for Instance-Level Robotic Grasping Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmm.2025.3557685): Couples language guidance with SAM-style instance segmentation for robotic grasp detection.
- [Autonomous Visual Navigation With Head Stabilization Control for a Salamander-Like Robot](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmech.2025.3607740): Adds head-stabilized visual navigation control for a salamander-like robot embodiment.
- [Learning Decentralized Multi-Robot PointGoal Navigation](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2025.3550798): Learns decentralized policies for multi-robot PointGoal navigation.
- [Semantics-Aware Hierarchical Decision Framework for Embodied Visual Room Rearrangement](https://doi.org/10.1109/tnnls.2025.3543438): Uses semantic hierarchy to decide rearrangement actions for embodied visual room reorganization.
- [ARC: Robots Adaptive Risk-aware Robust Control via Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11246723): Applies distributional reinforcement learning to adaptive risk-aware robust robot control.
- [Visual Scene Understanding and Interaction Design for Embodied Intelligence in Mobile Robots](https://doi.org/10.1109/cyber67662.2025.11168350): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for embodied and VLA systems.
- [Robot Navigation in Dynamic and Crowded Environments](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcst.2025.3575687): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for embodied and VLA systems.
- [Dynamic Conditional Importance Empowered Diffusion Model: Physical Adaptation and Temporal Optimization for Robot Action Generation in Multimodal Scenarios](https://doi.org/10.1109/mcai66356.2025.11381955): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for embodied and VLA systems.
- [Adversarial Augmentation for Task-Parameterized Underwater Skill Learning via Digital Twins*](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11247244): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for embodied and VLA systems.
- [A Resilient Decision-Making Framework for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Systems Under Dynamic Communication](https://doi.org/10.1109/eiect68017.2025.11331906): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for embodied and VLA systems.
