- [Deep Learning-based Proactive Hazard Prediction for Human-Robot Collaboration with Sensor Malfunctions](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11246277): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for embodied and VLA systems.
- [Multimodal Graph-Based Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Agent Autonomous Navigation](https://doi.org/10.1109/ictai66417.2025.00046): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for embodied and VLA systems.
- [Routing Manipulation of Deformable Linear Object Using Reinforcement Learning and Diffusion Policy](https://doi.org/10.1109/icra55743.2025.11127451): Trains separate RL agents for rope insertion and pulling, then uses diffusion policy to handle contact-rich deformable-linear-object routing.
- [Backwards Planning from Onward Task Demonstrations via Vision-Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/siu66497.2025.11111760): Learns backwards planning from task demonstrations using vision-language models.
- [SAGE: Scalable Automated Generation of Environments for Robotics](https://doi.org/10.1109/icar65334.2025.11338642): Automates scalable robotics environment generation, adding synthetic-environment coverage for embodied-agent training and evaluation.
- [Instruction-Based Spatial Information Enhancement in Lightweight Vision-Language-Action Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/eiect68017.2025.11331608): Evidence-needed: Crossref metadata confirms the lightweight VLA title but exposes no abstract, leaving the instruction-based spatial enhancement mechanism unverified.
- [MMVLA-EXT: Multi-Modal Vision-Language-Action Model with Efficient Cross-Platform Task Transfer](https://doi.org/10.1109/icar65334.2025.11338657): Evidence-needed: Crossref confirms the ICAR 2025 title but provides no abstract, so the cross-platform VLA transfer recipe needs paper verification.
- [Exploring Policy Distillation for Vision-Language-Action Generalist Robotic Policies](https://doi.org/10.1109/ictc66702.2025.11389105): Evidence-needed: Crossref confirms the ICTC 2025 title but gives no abstract, so the VLA policy-distillation setup remains unverified.
- [Event-Triggered Maps of Dynamics: A Framework for Modeling Spatial Motion Patterns in Non-Stationary Environments](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11247709): Models spatial motion patterns in non-stationary environments, giving embodied navigation policies a reusable dynamics-map representation.
- [Curiosity-driven embodied learning for cross-modal perception](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.inffus.2025.103882): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. - [Emergent Planning Capabilities in Embodied LLMs Through Memory-Constrained Symbolic Compression](https://doi.org/10.1109/ictbig68706.2025.11323801): Contributes a model, training, inference, or data-generation approach for embodied agents and vision-language-action systems.
- [Perception to Action with Vision-Language-Action Models for Fast and Reliable Decision Making in Dynamic Environments](https://doi.org/10.1109/ictc66702.2025.11388300): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [DGETP: Dynamic Graph Attention Network for Embodied Task Planning](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11245660): Contributes a model, training, inference, or data-generation approach for embodied agents and vision-language-action systems.
- [Temporal-Spatial Representation Fusion for Dexterous Manipulation Learning with Unpaired Visual-Action Data](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11247201): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [VLIN-RL: A Unified Vision-Language Interpreter and Reinforcement Learning Motion Planner Framework for Robot Dynamic Tasks](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11247258): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [HeStIa: Asynchronous Embodied Dynamic Locomotion Learning for Walking Robots through Multimodal Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/iros60139.2025.11247288): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for embodied and VLA agents. Core idea: Published in 2025 IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS); metadata title: HeStIa: Asynchronous Embodied Dynamic Locomotion Learning for Walking Robots through Multimodal Large Language Models.
- [FunGrasp: Functional Grasping for Diverse Dexterous Hands](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2025.3561573): Transfers a single RGB-D human functional grasp to diverse robot hands through human-to-robot retargeting, then trains sim-to-real RL policies for dynamic grasp execution.
- [TARAD: Task-Aware Robot Affordance-Centric Diffusion Policy Learned From LLM-Generated Demonstrations](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2025.3598998): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [VLAS: From Individual to Social Intelligence via Social Radar-Augmented VLA Agents](https://doi.org/10.1109/mesa68091.2025.11278880): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for embodied and VLA agents. Core idea: Published in 2025 21st IEEE International Conference on Mechatronic and Embedded Systems and Applications (MESA); metadata title: VLAS: From Individual to Social Intelligence via Social Radar-Augmented VLA Agents.
- [Exploring Unstructured Language Feedback for Robot Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/ro-man63969.2025.11217743): Evidence-needed: Crossref confirms the RO-MAN 2025 title but provides no abstract, leaving the exact language-feedback learning loop unverified.
- [QwenGrasp: Human-Robot Interactive 6-DoF Target-Oriented Grasping with Large Vision-Language Model](https://doi.org/10.1109/smc58881.2025.11343173): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [PointACT: Automatic Robot Manipulation with Cross-Modal Action Chunking Transformer on Image-Point Cloud](https://doi.org/10.1109/swc65939.2025.00225): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [GATGrasp: Learning Task-Aware Affordance Grasp for Robotic Tool Usage With Knowledge Graph Attention Mechanism](https://doi.org/10.1109/tase.2025.3644186): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [Semantically Guided Task Planning: Supervised Vision-Language-Action Model by Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3642702): Evidence-needed: Crossref confirms the TCSVT article title but gives no abstract, so the LLM-supervised VLA planning recipe still needs publisher or paper inspection.
- [ARViP: Adversarial Regularization in Visuomotor Policies for Robotic VLA Purpose](https://doi.org/10.1109/tii.2025.3643921): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [monoVLN: Bridging the Observation Gap between Monocular and Panoramic Vision and Language Navigation](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.00884): Model move record; monoVLN uses a 3DGS-based monocular VLN framework with implicit partial completion and uncertainty-aware active perception to compensate for missing panoramic observations.
- [RoboReward: General-Purpose Vision-Language Reward Models for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.00675): Introduces general-purpose vision-language reward models for robotics, useful for VLA post-training and policy selection.
- [Explicit World Models for Reliable Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01705): Argues that reliable HRI needs an accessible explicit world model as common ground between humans and robots, rather than only formal predictability guarantees.
- [SingingBot: An Avatar-Driven System for Robotic Face Singing Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02125): Drives robotic face singing by generating expressive singing avatars, mapping semantic facial features to the robot, and measuring emotional dynamic range.
- [Closing the Reality Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02778): Uses tactile simulation, torque sensing, and reinforcement learning for zero-shot sim-to-real dexterous force-based grasping and manipulation.
- [SOP: A Scalable Online Post-Training System for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03044): Describes a scalable online post-training system for VLA models, directly matching the Model taxonomy expansion.
- [FIRE-VLM: A Vision-Language-Driven Reinforcement Learning Framework for UAV Wildfire Tracking in a Physics-Grounded Fire Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03449): Adds a VLM-guided reinforcement-learning framework for embodied UAV tracking.
- [LooseRoPE: Content-aware Attention Manipulation for Semantic Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05127): Modulates diffusion-model RoPE attention with saliency to preserve pasted object identity while harmonizing it with a new image context; adjacent to embodied visual editing rather than robot control.
- [LaST0: Latent Spatio-Temporal Chain-of-Thought for Robotic Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05248): Replaces explicit linguistic reasoning traces with latent spatiotemporal chain-of-thought states that capture physical dynamics before VLA action generation.
- [Deep Whole-body Parkour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07701): Integrates exteroceptive sensing with whole-body motion tracking so one humanoid policy can execute dynamic multi-contact motions on unstructured terrain.
- [AME-2: Agile and Generalized Legged Locomotion via Attention-Based Neural Map Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08485): Trains legged-locomotion RL policies with an attention-based neural map encoder and uncertainty-aware mapping pipeline for agile, generalized quadruped and biped terrain traversal.
- [Fast-ThinkAct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09708): Improves VLA reasoning efficiency with verbalizable latent planning before control execution.
- [RobotDesignGPT: Automated Robot Design Synthesis using Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.11801): Uses VLM reasoning and visual feedback to synthesize kinematically valid robot designs from user prompts and reference images without hand-written design grammars.
- [ForeDiffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12925): Uses future-view construction to condition diffusion policies for robot manipulation.
- [Static Is Not Enough: A Comparative Study of VR and SpaceMouse in Static and Dynamic Teleoperation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.13042): Compares VR controller and SpaceMouse teleoperation on static and reactive dynamic tasks, showing interface choice changes demonstration success, workload, and usability.
- [MATTERIX: toward a digital twin for robotics-assisted chemistry laboratory automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.13232): Builds a GPU-accelerated chemistry-lab digital twin that combines robotic manipulation, powder and liquid dynamics, heat transfer, reaction kinetics, and semantic workflow simulation.
- [UniCon: A Unified System for Efficient Robot Learning Transfers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14617): Standardizes robot-learning states, control flow, and instrumentation as reusable execution graphs to transfer controllers across heterogeneous robot platforms with lower latency.
- [TIDAL: Temporally Interleaved Diffusion and Action Loop for High-Frequency VLA Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14945): Builds a temporally interleaved diffusion and action loop for high-frequency VLA control.
- [DextER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16046): Uses language-conditioned embodied reasoning to generate dexterous grasps, strengthening VLA-style manipulation where grasp choice depends on object function and task intent.
- [MetaWorld: Skill Transfer and Composition in a Hierarchical World Model for Grounding High-Level Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17507): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [A Pragmatic VLA Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18692): Presents a pragmatic VLA foundation-model direction for embodied control.
- [Fauna Sprout: A lightweight, approachable, developer-ready humanoid robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18963): Presents a lightweight humanoid developer platform with compliant control, soft exteriors, grippers, VR teleoperation, and an expressive head for safer long-term HRI deployment.
- [Tactile Memory With Soft Robot: Robust Object Insertion via Masked Encoding and Soft Wrist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19275): Introduces model-side work on tactile Memory With Soft Robot: Robust Object Insertion via Masked Encoding and Soft Wrist, fitting the repository's training, architecture, post-training, verifier, or synthetic-data coverage.
- [GPO: Growing Policy Optimization for Legged Robot Locomotion and Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20668): Narrows torque-control action spaces early in PPO with a time-varying transformation, then grows exploration for legged locomotion and whole-body control.
- [Thinker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21199): Introduces a vision-language foundation model for embodied intelligence.
- [Nimbus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21449): proposes a unified embodied synthetic-data generation framework for training and evaluating embodied models.
- [Information Filtering via Variational Regularization for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21926): Adds variational information filtering to robot manipulation policies, improving policy learning through model-side regularization.
- [mjlab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22074): Provides a lightweight GPU-accelerated robot-learning framework, lowering the cost of large-scale policy training and simulation loops.
- [Task-Centric Latent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22714): Uses VLM task descriptions to separate controllable task-relevant motion from distractors when training latent action models.
- [Learning Geometrically-Grounded 3D Visual Representations for View-Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22988): Pretrains single-view 3D representations with point-cloud reconstruction and Gaussian splatting, then distills that geometry into manipulation policies for view generalization.
- [ZEST: Zero-shot Embodied Skill Transfer for Athletic Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00401): Trains zero-shot humanoid skill-transfer policies from motion capture, monocular video, and animation, reducing per-skill reward engineering for agile control.
- [Toward Reliable Sim-to-Real Predictability for MoE-based Robust Quadrupedal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00678): Combines a gated MoE proprioceptive locomotion policy with RoboGauge sim-to-sim metrics to predict robust quadruped sim-to-real transfer before physical trials.
- [UniMorphGrasp: Diffusion Model with Morphology-Awareness for Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Grasp Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00915): Conditions a diffusion grasp generator on hand kinematic graphs and object geometry after mapping diverse robotic hands into a shared canonical hand-pose space.
- [Offline Discovery of Interpretable Skills from Multi-Task Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01018): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [A Systematic Study of Data Modalities and Strategies for Co-training Large Behavior Models for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01067): Studies data modalities and co-training strategies for large behavior models in robotic manipulation.
- [A Closed-Form Geometric Retargeting Solver for Upper Body Humanoid Robot Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01632): Reframes humanoid arm retargeting as shoulder-elbow-wrist orientation alignment, yielding a fast closed-form SEW-Mimic solver with an optimality guarantee.
- [GSR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01693): Learns structured reasoning for embodied manipulation, turning manipulation policy learning into an explicit reasoning and action-grounding problem.
- [World-Gymnast: Training Robots with Reinforcement Learning in a World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02454): Trains robots with reinforcement learning inside a world model, linking model-based simulation and embodied policy learning.
- [HumanX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02473): Turns human videos into agile humanoid interaction skills through a data-generation pipeline and reward-free real-world policy learning.
- [StepNav](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02590): introduces structured trajectory priors for efficient and multimodal visual navigation.
- [Language Movement Primitives: Grounding Language Models in Robot Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02839): Grounds language models in robot motion through movement primitives, giving language-conditioned control a model-side form.
- [Hand3R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03200): performs online 4D hand-scene reconstruction in the wild for embodied hand-object perception.
- [RDT2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03310): Scales UMI demonstration data for a VLA robotic foundation model, targeting zero-shot generalization across robot embodiments.
- [AffordanceGrasp-R1:Leveraging Reasoning-Based Affordance Segmentation with Reinforcement Learning for Robotic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03547): Combines reasoning-based affordance segmentation with reinforcement learning for robotic grasping.
- [EgoActor: Grounding Task Planning into Spatial-aware Egocentric Actions for Humanoid Robots via Visual-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04515): Grounds VLM task planning into spatially aware egocentric humanoid actions, connecting high-level plans with executable embodied behavior.
- [AGILE Hand-Object Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04672): uses VLM-guided agentic generation to reconstruct hand-object interactions for robotics and VR data creation.
- [Boosting SAM for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Segmentation via Conditional Point Sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05218): Uses conditional point sparsification to adapt SAM prompts for cross-domain few-shot segmentation, making it an adjacent perception method rather than a robot-control model.
- [RoboPaint: From Human Demonstration to Any Robot and Any View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05325): Turns multimodal human demonstrations into robot training data through tactile-aware retargeting and photorealistic Isaac Sim rendering, then trains VLA policies without teleoperation.
- [InterPrior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06035): Scales generative humanoid-object control through imitation pretraining and reinforcement-learning post-training, targeting compositional loco-manipulation skills.
- [Towards Adaptive Environment Generation for Training Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06366): Proposes a closed-loop environment generator that adjusts training-world difficulty to the agent's current performance, improving embodied-agent curriculum design.
- [Humanoid Manipulation Interface: Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation from Robot-Free Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06643): Converts robot-free demonstrations into humanoid whole-body manipulation training signals, broadening data sources for humanoid policies.
- [DreamDojo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06949): Trains a generalist robot world model from large-scale human videos so robot agents can simulate action outcomes before acting.
- [Learning Nonlinear Systems In-Context: From Synthetic Data to Real-World Motor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07173): Trains transformers on synthetic nonlinear systems so they can infer feedforward motor-control laws in context and transfer few-shot to real motor control.
- [Scalable Dexterous Robot Learning with AR-based Remote Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07341): Collects remote AR demonstrations, pretrains dexterous policies with behavior cloning, and uses contrastive improvement to scale human-robot teaching.
- [CoLF: Learning Consistent Leader-Follower Policies for Vision-Language-Guided Multi-Robot Cooperative Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07776): Uses asymmetric leader-follower MARL to stabilize decentralized language-grounded multi-robot transport under viewpoint and instruction ambiguity.
- [MOSAIC: Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap in Generalist Humanoid Motion Tracking and Teleoperation with Rapid Residual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08594): Adds rapid residual adaptation to a generalist humanoid motion tracker, improving sim-to-real transfer across teleoperation interfaces.
- [Dexterous Manipulation Policies from RGB Human Videos via 3D Hand-Object Trajectory Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09013): Reconstructs 3D hand-object trajectories from monocular RGB human videos and retargets them with contact optimization to train dexterous robot manipulation policies.
- [TwinRL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09023): Uses digital-twin-driven reinforcement learning for real-world robotic manipulation through simulated-to-real feedback.
- [STaR: Scalable Task-Conditioned Retrieval for Long-Horizon Multi-Modal Robot Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09255): Builds task-agnostic multimodal long-term robot memory and uses information-bottleneck retrieval for long-horizon navigation reasoning in simulation and on a Husky robot.
- [Co-jump: Cooperative Jumping with Quadrupedal Robots via Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10514): Uses multi-agent reinforcement learning to train cooperative jumping policies for quadrupedal robots.
- [RISE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11075): A self-improving robot-policy framework with a compositional world model; core idea: use world-model structure to support policy improvement across embodied tasks.
- [YOR: Your Own Mobile Manipulator for Generalizable Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11150): Open-sources a low-cost mobile manipulator with omnidirectional base, telescopic lift, and dual arms for whole-body navigation and bimanual manipulation research.
- [Human Preference Modeling Using Visual Motion Prediction Improves Robot Skill Learning from Egocentric Human Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11393): Improves robot skill learning from egocentric human video by using visual motion prediction as a preference-derived reward for policy optimization.
- [HAIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11758): Uses a dynamics-aware world model to control agile humanoid-object interaction with underactuated objects and occluded state.
- [JEPA-VLA: Video Predictive Embedding is Needed for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11832): Uses video predictive embeddings for VLA models, adding a JEPA-style world-modeling route.
- [LAMP: Implicit Language Map for Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11862): Encodes language features as a continuous neural field for robot navigation, replacing memory-heavy grid or node language maps while supporting fine-grained path generation.
- [Learning to Manipulate Anything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11885): Studies data scaling laws for bounding-box-guided robot policies, clarifying how visual localization supervision scales toward open-ended manipulation.
- [Robot-DIFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11934): Distills diffusion features for geometrically consistent visuomotor control, using generative visual representations as policy-side spatial signals.
- [VLAW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12063): Co-trains a VLA policy and world model through iterative improvement, linking action learning with predictive environment modeling.
- [GigaBrain-0.5M*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12099): Trains a VLA through world-model-based reinforcement learning, connecting action learning with learned environment dynamics.
- [LDA-1B: Scaling Latent Dynamics Action Model via Universal Embodied Data Ingestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12215): Scales latent dynamics action modeling through universal embodied data ingestion.
- [Scaling Verification Can Be More Effective than Scaling Policy Learning for Vision-Language-Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12281): Uses test-time verification and joint scaling of rephrased instructions plus generated actions to reduce VLA intention-action mismatch.
- [LongNav-R1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12351): Trains VLA navigation with horizon-adaptive multi-turn reinforcement learning over continuous embodied interaction.
- [CRAFT: Adapting VLA Models to Contact-rich Manipulation via Force-aware Curriculum Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12532): Adapts VLA models to contact-rich manipulation through force-aware curriculum fine-tuning.
- [Dual-Granularity Contrastive Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12636): Uses dual-granularity contrastive rewards and generated episodic guidance for embodied reinforcement learning.
- [ALOE: Action-Level Off-Policy Evaluation for Vision-Language-Action Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12691): Adds action-level off-policy evaluation for VLA post-training, so robot-policy updates can be judged on executed actions rather than only proxy rewards.
- [SafeFlowMPC: Predictive and Safe Trajectory Planning for Robot Manipulators with Learning-based Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12794): Combines flow-matching learned policies with online MPC-style optimization to preserve safety guarantees while retaining flexible trajectory generation.
- [Steerable Vision-Language-Action Policies for Embodied Reasoning and Hierarchical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13193): Adds steerability for embodied reasoning and hierarchical control in VLA policies.
- [Imitating What Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13197): Filters human-video demonstrations through simulation before modular policy learning, making video imitation more execution-grounded for robot control.
- [FlowHOI: Flow-based Semantics-Grounded Generation of Hand-Object Interactions for Dexterous Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13444): Generates semantically grounded hand-object interaction sequences with flow matching from egocentric observations, instructions, and 3D Gaussian scene reconstructions.
- [TactAlign: Human-to-Robot Policy Transfer via Tactile Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13579): Transfers human-collected tactile demonstrations to robots through an unpaired shared latent tactile representation, improving contact-rich manipulation policy learning.
- [Semantic-Contact Fields for Category-Level Generalizable Tactile Tool Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13833): Fuses visual semantics with dense contact probability and force estimates, then feeds SCFields into diffusion policies for category-level tactile tool manipulation.
- [Rigidity-Based Multi-Finger Coordination for Precise In-Hand Manipulation of Force-Sensitive Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14104): Plans coordinated fingertip forces with graph-rigidity and force-closure constraints, then maps them to joint trajectories without tactile feedback.
- [Affordance Transfer Across Object Instances via Semantically Anchored Functional Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14874): Transfers action affordances across object instances through semantically anchored functional maps.
- [Feasibility-aware Imitation Learning from Observation with Multimodal Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15351): Combines behavior cloning from observation with robot-dynamics feasibility estimates and visual-haptic feedback so demonstrations become more executable.
- [Constraining Streaming Flow Models for Adapting Learned Robot Trajectory Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15567): Adds constraint-aware metrics to streaming flow policies, reshaping learned velocity fields at execution time to satisfy workspace and configuration-space constraints.
- [MeshMimic: Geometry-Aware Humanoid Motion Learning through 3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15733): Reconstructs 3D scenes and human trajectories from video, then retargets geometry-aware motion-terrain interactions to humanoid policies.
- [Perceptive Humanoid Parkour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15827): Chains dynamic humanoid skills through motion matching and perception-driven selection, targeting long-horizon parkour-style control in complex scenes.
- [RoboGene: Boosting VLA Pre-training via Diversity-Driven Agentic Framework for Real-World Task Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16444): Boosts VLA pre-training with diversity-driven real-world task generation, expanding the task data available to embodied models.
- [EgoScale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16710): Scales dexterous manipulation by transferring diverse egocentric human data into high-degree-of-freedom robotic control.
- [FORMICA: Decision-Focused Learning for Communication-Free Multi-Robot Task Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18622): Learns communication-free multi-robot task allocation by predicting teammates' bid distributions and optimizing allocation regret rather than only bid-prediction error.
- [Learning Invariant Visual Representations for Planning with Joint-Embedding Predictive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18639): Trains joint-embedding predictive world models to learn planning-ready visual representations that stay invariant across nuisance changes in embodied environments.
- [RoboCurate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18742): Uses action-verified neural trajectories to curate diverse robot-learning data, focusing model improvement on demonstrations that survive execution checks.
- [HeRO: Hierarchical 3D Semantic Representation for Pose-aware Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18817): Builds hierarchical 3D semantic object representations so manipulation policies can reason about pose and object structure before acting.
- [Human-to-Robot Interaction: Learning from Video Demonstration for Robot Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19184): Decouples video understanding from policy learning so robots can identify task-relevant objects and acquire manipulation skills from unstructured demonstration videos.
- [Compositional Planning with Jumpy World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19634): Uses jumpy world models for compositional planning, targeting longer-horizon embodied decisions through learned state-transition abstraction.
- [Towards Dexterous Embodied Manipulation via Deep Multi-Sensory Fusion and Sparse Expert Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19764): Uses multi-sensory fusion and sparse expert scaling for dexterous embodied manipulation.
- [Grasp to Act: Dexterous Grasping for Tool Use in Dynamic Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20466): Learns dexterous grasping for physical tool use in dynamic settings, adding an embodied model line for action-conditioned manipulation.
- [Probing and Bridging Geometry-Interaction Cues for Affordance Reasoning in Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20501): Studies how vision foundation models encode geometry and interaction cues, then bridges those cues for affordance reasoning in embodied manipulation.
- [Recursive Belief VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20659): Introduces recursive belief modeling for VLA policies, making uncertainty over observations and actions part of the policy representation.
- [IG-RFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20715): Applies interaction-guided reinforcement learning to VLA models for long-horizon robotic manipulation, emphasizing feedback-driven post-training.
- [A Robotic Testing Platform for Pipelined Discovery of Resilient Soft Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20963): Provides an automated robotic testbed for screening resilient soft actuators, supplying hardware-in-the-loop data for embodied design and control loops.
- [HALO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21157): Unifies vision, language, and action modeling for embodied multimodal chain-of-thought reasoning.
- [ADM-DP: Adaptive Dynamic Modality Diffusion Policy through Vision-Tactile-Graph Fusion for Multi-Agent Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21622): Integrates vision, tactile feedback, and graph-based multi-agent pose context into an adaptive diffusion policy for coordinated robotic manipulation.
- [LessMimic: Long-Horizon Humanoid Interaction with Unified Distance Field Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21723): Uses distance-field interaction representations and RL post-training to enable reference-free, long-horizon humanoid-object interaction and skill composition.
- [CWM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22452): Learns contrastive world models for action feasibility in embodied-agent pipelines. Core idea: check whether proposed actions are physically executable before planning continues.
- [EgoAVFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22461): Learns robot policies with active vision from human egocentric videos through 3D flow.
- [SignVLA: A Gloss-Free Vision-Language-Action Framework for Real-Time Sign Language-Guided Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22514): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [Pixel2Catch: Multi-Agent Sim-to-Real Transfer for Agile Manipulation with a Single RGB Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22733): Uses pixel-level motion cues from a single RGB camera and a heterogeneous multi-agent RL setup that separates arm and hand control, enabling sim-to-real thrown-object catching without explicit 3D object tracking.
- [EmbodMocap: In-the-Wild 4D Human-Scene Reconstruction for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23205): Reconstructs in-the-wild 4D human-scene interactions, providing data infrastructure for embodied agents that learn from human behavior in context.
- [FAVLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23648): Adds force-adaptive fast-slow control to VLA models for contact-rich manipulation, connecting language-conditioned policies with tactile force dynamics.
- [OmniTrack: General Motion Tracking via Physics-Consistent Reference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23832): Trains humanoid motion tracking through physics-consistent reference rollouts, separating feasible motion generation from the deployable tracking policy.
- [OmniXtreme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23843): Scales high-dynamic humanoid control by addressing multi-motion optimization and real-world executability constraints.
- [Foundation World Models for Agents that Learn, Verify, and Adapt Reliably Beyond Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23997): Proposes foundation world models for agents that learn, verify, and adapt beyond static environments.
- [SafeGen-LLM: Enhancing Safety Generalization in Task Planning for Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.24235): Enhances safety generalization in robotic task planning, targeting embodied policies that must transfer safe plans across changing task and environment conditions.
- [EgoMoD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00167): Predicts maps of dynamics from egocentric robot observations, giving embodied navigation policies a model of motion beyond the visible local scene.
- [Embedding Morphology into Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00182): Encodes robot morphology inside transformer policies to improve cross-robot transfer, treating embodiment differences as model inputs rather than fixed assumptions.
- [ReMoT: Reinforcement Learning with Motion Contrast Triplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00461): Uses automatically generated motion-contrast triplets and GRPO to train VLMs on fine-grained spatiotemporal distinctions relevant to navigation, robotics, and autonomous driving.
- [TGM-VLA: Task-Guided Mixup for Sampling-Efficient and Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00615): Improves imitation-learning efficiency with optimized keyframe sampling, color-inversion projection for dark objects, and task-guided mixup for robust manipulation.
- [Pro-HOI: Perceptive Root-guided Humanoid-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01126): Uses root-trajectory conditioning and persistent object estimation as a general interface for robust long-horizon humanoid-object interaction.
- [D-REX: Differentiable Real-to-Sim-to-Real Engine for Learning Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01151): Builds a differentiable real-to-sim-to-real engine for dexterous grasping, using Gaussian-splat object twins and force-aware policy learning from limited demonstrations.
- [PhysGraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01436): builds physically grounded graph-Transformer policies for bimanual hand-tool-object manipulation.
- [Mean-Flow based One-Step Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01469): Removes iterative flow-matching sampling by using Mean-Flow to generate high-frequency VLA action chunks in one step.
- [State-Action Inpainting Diffuser for Continuous Control with Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01553): Formulates delayed continuous control as joint state-action sequence inpainting, letting a diffuser combine dynamics modeling with direct plan generation.
- [KERV: Kinematic-Rectified Speculative Decoding for Embodied VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01581): Uses kinematic-rectified speculative decoding for faster embodied VLA models.
- [Characterizing VLA Models: Identifying the Action Generation Bottleneck for Edge AI Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02271): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [CoFL: Continuous Flow Fields for Language-Conditioned Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02854): Recasts language-conditioned navigation as BEV continuous-flow-field prediction, turning each scene-instruction pair into dense local motion supervision.
- [Rhythm: Learning Interactive Whole-Body Control for Dual Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02856): Trains interactive whole-body control for dual humanoids, combining interaction-aware retargeting with real-world deployment of physically coupled behaviors.
- [CMoE: Contrastive Mixture of Experts for Motion Control and Terrain Adaptation of Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03067): Adds contrastive constraints to mixture-of-experts reinforcement learning so terrain-specific experts specialize for humanoid motion control.
- [Look Forward to Walk Backward: Efficient Terrain Memory for Backward Locomotion with Forward Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03138): Adds a terrain-memory mechanism for backward locomotion, allowing legged robots to reuse forward-looking perception when moving without rear visibility.
- [HoMMI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03243): Learns whole-body mobile manipulation from human demonstrations, extending embodied policy learning beyond arm-only manipulation.
- [ULTRA: Unified Multimodal Control for Autonomous Humanoid Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03279): Combines physics-driven retargeting with a unified multimodal controller for autonomous humanoid loco-manipulation from references, sparse goals, and egocentric perception.
- [How to Peel with a Knife: Aligning Fine-Grained Manipulation with Human Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03280): Aligns detailed manipulation behavior with human preferences, strengthening embodied action-model learning.
- [MEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03596): Combines video-based short-horizon memory and text-based long-horizon memory so VLA policies can execute multi-stage real-world tasks.
- [RAGNav: A Retrieval-Augmented Topological Reasoning Framework for Multi-Goal Visual-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03745): Combines retrieval with topological map reasoning so a navigation policy can plan across multiple language-specified goals.
- [SkillVLA: Tackling Combinatorial Diversity in Dual-Arm Manipulation via Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03836): Designs bimanual VLA policies for single-arm skill reuse and recombination, avoiding separate learning for every left-right dual-arm behavior pairing.
- [Self-adapting Robotic Agents through Online Continual Reinforcement Learning with World Model Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04029): Builds on DreamerV3 by using world-model prediction residuals to detect distribution shifts and trigger online continual RL fine-tuning during deployment.
- [Diffusion Policy through Conditional Proximal Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04790): Trains diffusion policies with conditional PPO, adapting score-based action generators through reinforcement learning rather than pure imitation.
- [K-Gen: A Multimodal Language-Conditioned Approach for Interpretable Keypoint-Guided Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04868): Uses MLLMs over raster BEV maps and scene text to generate interpretable trajectory keypoints, then refines them after trajectory-aware RL fine-tuning.
- [SeedPolicy: Horizon Scaling via Self-Evolving Diffusion Policy for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05117): Extends diffusion-policy horizons with a self-evolving training loop for longer robot manipulation rollouts.
- [PhysiFlow: Physics-Aware Humanoid Whole-Body VLA via Multi-Brain Latent Flow Matching and Robust Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05410): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [An Embodied Companion for Visual Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05511): Grounds visual storytelling in an embodied companion setting, linking multimodal scene understanding with situated interaction behavior.
- [EmboAlign: Aligning Video Generation with Compositional Constraints for Zero-Shot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05757): Uses VLM-generated compositional constraints at inference time to align video-generation rollouts before geometric retargeting to zero-shot robot actions.
- [OpenHEART: Opening Heterogeneous Articulated Objects with a Legged Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05830): Learns policies for a legged manipulator to open diverse articulated objects, joining mobility, contact-rich manipulation, and object-state grounding.
- [Lifelong Embodied Navigation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06073): Formalizes lifelong embodied navigation learning and introduces Uni-Walker, which separates shared and task-specific navigation knowledge with DE-LoRA so agents can learn sequential navigation tasks without forgetting earlier skills.
- [CN-CBF: Composite Neural Control Barrier Function for Safe Robot Navigation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06921): Uses composite neural control barrier functions to enforce safety constraints for robot navigation around dynamic obstacles.
- [RoTri-Diff: A Spatial Robot-Object Triadic Interaction-Guided Diffusion Model for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07165): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [HSC-VLA: Hierarchical Scene-Clearing for Robust Bimanual Manipulation in Dense Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07484): Splits bimanual manipulation into a high-level visual-semantic Brain that masks task-relevant geometry and a low-level diffusion-policy Cerebellum.
- [InterReal: A Unified Physics-Based Imitation Framework for Learning Human-Object Interaction Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07516): Unifies physics-based imitation for human-object interaction skills, supporting reusable interaction-policy learning beyond isolated motions.
- [FeasibleCap: Real-Time Embodiment Constraint Guidance for In-the-Wild Robot Demonstration Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07580): Adds real-time embodiment-constraint feedback during demonstration capture, filtering human demonstrations toward motions a target robot can execute.
- [Low-Cost Teleoperation Extension for Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07672): Describes a low-cost mobile-manipulator teleoperation extension that can broaden data collection for whole-body manipulation policies.
- [TeamHOI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07988): Learns a decentralized Transformer policy for cooperative humanoid-object interactions across variable team sizes.
- [Towards Human-Like Manipulation through RL-Augmented Teleoperation and Mixture-of-Dexterous-Experts VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08122): Pairs RL-trained in-hand atomic skills for shared-autonomy teleoperation with a mixture-of-dexterous-experts VLA for contact-rich bimanual manipulation.
- [Seed2Scale: A Self-Evolving Data Engine for Embodied AI via Small to Large Model Synergy and Multimodal Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08260): Uses a small VLA collector, large VLM verifier, and target-model learning loop to self-generate and score robot trajectories from a few seed demonstrations.
- [3PoinTr: 3D Point Tracks for Robot Manipulation Pretraining from Casual Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08485): Pretrains robot manipulation policies from casual human videos by predicting 3D point tracks as embodiment-agnostic intermediate representations.
- [APPLV: Adaptive Planner Parameter Learning from Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08862): Contributes reusable orchestration, planning, memory, verification, tool use, or runtime logic for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: external planning, memory, verification, collaboration, or language-to-action control for robots.
- [PlayWorld: Learning Robot World Models from Autonomous Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09030): Trains action-conditioned video world simulators from unsupervised robot self-play rather than success-biased demonstrations, capturing long-tailed manipulation interactions.
- [PM-Nav: Priori-Map Guided Embodied Navigation in Functional Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09113): Guides object-goal navigation in functional buildings with prior maps, injecting building-layout knowledge into embodied route planning.
- [DexHiL: A Human-in-the-Loop Framework for Vision-Language-Action Model Post-Training in Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09121): Uses a human-in-the-loop framework for VLA post-training in dexterous manipulation, making feedback collection part of the execution loop.
- [ZeroWBC: Learning Natural Visuomotor Humanoid Control Directly from Human Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09170): Learns natural humanoid visuomotor control from human egocentric video, linking in-the-wild observation data to whole-body policies.
- [AR-VLA: True Autoregressive Action Expert for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10126): Adds a standalone autoregressive action expert with long-lived action memory and refreshable vision-language prefixes for temporally consistent VLA control.
- [Cross-Hand Latent Representation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10158): Introduces XL-VLA with a unified embodiment-invariant latent action space that can plug into VLA architectures for multiple dexterous hands.
- [From Prior to Pro: Efficient Skill Mastery via Distribution Contractive RL Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10263): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [Update-Free On-Policy Steering via Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10282): Trains verifier functions on policy rollouts and uses them at execution time to steer black-box diffusion policies without updating base parameters.
- [Safe Probabilistic Planning for Human-Robot Interaction using Conformal Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10392): Applies conformal risk control to probabilistic human-robot planning so safety bounds remain explicit during interaction.
- [COHORT: Hybrid RL for Collaborative Large DNN Inference on Multi-Robot Systems Under Real-Time Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10436): Uses hybrid reinforcement learning to schedule collaborative DNN inference across robots under real-time constraints.
- [Agentic AI for Embodied-enhanced Beam Prediction in Low-Altitude Economy Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11392): Applies embodied-agent reasoning to beam prediction in low-altitude networks, treating aerial mobility and sensing context as part of the prediction loop.
- [CoViLLM: An Adaptive Human-Robot Collaborative Assembly Framework Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11461): Uses an LLM-driven adaptive framework for collaborative assembly, translating human-robot context into task decisions during physical assembly.
- [SVLL: Staged Vision-Language Learning for Physically Grounded Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11563): Stages vision-language learning for physically grounded embodied task planning.
- [See, Symbolize, Act: Grounding VLMs with Spatial Representations for Better Gameplay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11601): Grounds VLMs with spatial representations for gameplay actions.
- [Sim-to-reality adaptation for Deep Reinforcement Learning applied to an underwater docking application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12020): Studies deep-reinforcement-learning sim-to-real adaptation for underwater robotic docking.
- [TacVLA: Contact-Aware Tactile Fusion for Robust Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12665): Fine-tunes VLA policies with tactile tokens and a contact-aware gating mechanism that activates tactile fusion only during physical contact.
- [AnchorVLA4D: an Anchor-Based Spatial-Temporal Vision-Language-Action Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12730): Adds anchor images and a lightweight spatial encoder to preserve initial scene context and temporal memory during VLA manipulation.
- [SAVA-X: Ego-to-Exo Imitation Error Detection via Scene-Adaptive View Alignment and Bidirectional Cross View Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12764): Detects imitation errors by aligning egocentric and exocentric views with scene-adaptive fusion, improving demonstration quality checks for robot learning.
- [PVI: Plug-in Visual Injection for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12772): Attaches zero-initialized residual visual-injection modules to pretrained flow-matching action experts, preserving behavior while adding fine-grained spatial and temporal cues.
- [Beyond Imitation: Reinforcement Learning Fine-Tuning for Adaptive Diffusion Navigation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12868): Fine-tunes diffusion navigation policies with reinforcement learning beyond imitation learning.
- [ReMem-VLA: Empowering Vision-Language-Action Model with Memory via Dual-Level Recurrent Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12942): Adds dual-level recurrent-query memory to VLA models, improving memory-dependent manipulation without being only a runtime harness.
- [Navig-AI-tion: Navigation by Contextual AI and Spatial Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13200): Combines contextual AI with spatial-audio cues for navigation assistance, expanding embodied guidance beyond visual and language channels.
- [Bi-HIL: Bilateral Control-Based Multimodal Hierarchical Imitation Learning via Subtask-Level Progress Rate and Keyframe Memory for Long-Horizon Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13315): Uses bilateral-control demonstrations, subtask progress rates, and keyframe memory for hierarchical imitation in long-horizon contact-rich manipulation.
- [Exploration-assisted Bottleneck Transition Toward Robust and Data-efficient Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13756): Focuses exploration on bottleneck transitions so deformable-object manipulation policies become more robust with less data.
- [Path-Deviation Attention Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13782): Finds VLA attention heads that detect path deviation, supporting training-free embodied reliability monitoring.
- [Load-Aware Locomotion Control for Humanoid Robots in Industrial Transportation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14308): Conditions humanoid locomotion control on carried-load dynamics for industrial transport tasks where payload changes affect balance and gait.
- [OmniClone: Engineering a Robust, All-Rounder Whole-Body Humanoid Teleoperation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14327): Builds a source-agnostic whole-body humanoid control policy and diagnostic benchmark that support teleoperation, generated motion playback, and VLA-model control inputs.
- [OCRA: Object-Centric Learning with 3D and Tactile Priors for Human-to-Robot Action Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14401): Reconstructs object-centric 3D point clouds from multiview human videos, fuses tactile priors through ResFiLM, and feeds them to a diffusion policy.
- [One-Policy-Fits-All: Geometry-Aware Action Latents for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14522): Learns geometry-aware latent actions with 3D convolutions and transformers, then decodes embodiment-specific actions through a unified retargeting decoder.
- [The Scenic Route to Deception: Dark Patterns and Explainability Pitfalls in Conversational Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14586): Analyzes how conversational navigation systems can mislead users, making it a cautionary reliability reference for embodied route guidance.
- [AnoleVLA: Lightweight Vision-Language-Action Model with Deep State Space Models for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15046): Builds a lightweight VLA for mobile manipulation around deep state-space models rather than a large Transformer-only action head.
- [KiRAS: Keyframe Guided Self-Imitation for Robust and Adaptive Skill Learning in Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15179): Uses keyframe-guided self-imitation to make quadruped skill policies more robust and adaptive after initial demonstrations.
- [MoE-ACT: Scaling Multi-Task Bimanual Manipulation with Sparse Language-Conditioned Mixture-of-Experts Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15265): Inserts sparse MoE layers into ACT and uses language-conditioned FiLM modulation to reduce task interference in multi-task bimanual imitation learning.
- [MA-VLCM: A Vision Language Critic Model for Value Estimation of Policies in Multi-Agent Team Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15418): Fine-tunes a pretrained vision-language model as a centralized critic for multi-agent robot policy value estimation instead of learning the critic from scratch.
- [Look Before Acting: Enhancing Vision Foundation Representations for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15618): Proposes DeepVision-VLA, a vision-language mixture-of-transformers that injects multi-level visual features into deeper VLA layers for action grounding.
- [Enhancing Linguistic Generalization of VLA: Fine-Tuning OpenVLA via Synthetic Instruction Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16044): Uses LLM-generated paraphrased commands and LoRA fine-tuning on Bridge Dataset trajectories to improve OpenVLA linguistic generalization.
- [When Should a Robot Think? Resource-Aware Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning for Embodied Robotic Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16673): Learns resource-aware reasoning for embodied robotic decision making through RL.
- [Influence of Gripper Design on Human Demonstration Quality for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17189): Studies how gripper morphology affects human demonstration quality, informing hardware-aware data collection for robot policy learning.
- [Recurrent Reasoning with Vision-Language Models for Estimating Long-Horizon Embodied Task Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17312): Uses recurrent chain-of-thought over local video snippets to estimate long-horizon embodied task progress without processing the whole trajectory at once.
- [Towards Motion-aware Referring Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17413): Adds motion awareness to referring image segmentation, supporting embodied perception when object references depend on dynamic scene cues.
- [EVA: Aligning Video World Models with Executable Robot Actions via Inverse Dynamics Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17808): Post-trains video world models with inverse-dynamics rewards so visually plausible rollouts become more executable as robot action sequences.
- [Specification-Aware Distribution Shaping for Robotics Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17969): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [SR-Nav: Spatial Relationships Matter for Zero-shot Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18443): Uses spatial relationships as zero-shot priors for object-goal navigation, helping agents search functional scenes without task-specific training.
- [MemoAct: Atkinson-Shiffrin-Inspired Memory-Augmented Visuomotor Policy for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18494): Uses lossless short-term memory and compressed long-term memory tiers to improve visuomotor policy state tracking and long-horizon retention.
- [Scaling Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Robot VLAs with Generative 3D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18532): Fine-tunes robot VLAs with RL inside generated 3D worlds, scaling scene and object diversity without overfitting to narrow real-world training setups.
- [V-Dreamer: Automating Robotic Simulation and Trajectory Synthesis via Video Generation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18811): Uses video-generation priors to automate robotic simulation and synthesize trajectories for policy training data.
- [Morphology-Consistent Humanoid Interaction through Robot-Centric Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19709): Synthesizes robot-centric videos that preserve humanoid morphology, supplying training data for interaction policies.
- [StageCraft: Execution Aware Mitigation of Distractor and Obstruction Failures in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20659): Adds execution-aware mitigation for VLA failures caused by distractors and obstructions during robot action.
- [RoboAlign: Learning Test-Time Reasoning for Language-Action Alignment in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21341): Trains test-time reasoning for VLA models so language instructions and generated actions stay aligned during execution.
- [VP-VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22003): Introduces visual prompting as an interface for VLA models, improving spatial grounding and robustness in action generation.
- [UniDex: A Robot Foundation Suite for Universal Dexterous Hand Control from Egocentric Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22264): Builds a 50K-trajectory cross-hand dataset from egocentric human videos via retargeting and trains a unified VLA policy for dexterous hands.
- [ThinkJEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22281): Couples latent JEPA-style world modeling with vision-language reasoning to predict trajectories and actions in embodied settings.
- [CanViT: Toward Active-Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22570): Develops an active-vision foundation-model direction where perception is trained around viewpoint choice rather than static image recognition alone.
- [VLGOR: Visual-Language Knowledge Guided Offline Reinforcement Learning for Generalizable Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22892): Guides offline reinforcement learning with visual-language knowledge to improve policy generalization across embodied tasks.
- [AeroScene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23224): generates progressive scenes for aerial robotics, adding a scene-synthesis model line for embodied aerial agents.
- [Efficient Hybrid SE(3)-Equivariant Visuomotor Flow Policy via Spherical Harmonics for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23227): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [UniFunc3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23478): Unifies active spatial-temporal grounding for 3D functionality segmentation, linking 3D perception with actionable affordance reasoning.
- [StateLinFormer: Stateful Training Enhancing Long-term Memory in Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23571): Adds stateful training to a LinFormer-style navigation model, targeting longer-term memory for mapless or partially observed navigation.
- [3D-Mix for VLA: A Plug-and-Play Module for Integrating VGGT-based 3D Information into Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24393): Compares VGGT integration schemes and uses semantic-conditioned gated fusion to balance 2D semantics and 3D geometry in VLA control.
- [FODMP: Fast One-Step Diffusion of Movement Primitives Generation for Time-Dependent Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24806): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [ThermoAct: Thermal-Aware Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotic Perception and Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25044): Adds thermal awareness to VLA robotic perception and decision making.
- [Visualizing Impedance Control in Augmented Reality for Teleoperation: Design and User Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25418): Visualizes impedance-control state in augmented reality, improving operator feedback for contact-rich robot teleoperation.
- [LILAC: Language-Conditioned Object-Centric Optical Flow for Open-Loop Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25481): Generates language-conditioned object-centric 2D optical flow and converts it into 6-DoF manipulator trajectories using semantic alignment and cross-modal adapters.
- [Towards Embodied AI with MuscleMimic: Unlocking full-body musculoskeletal motor learning at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25544): Scales full-body musculoskeletal motor learning for embodied AI, extending VLA-style model coverage from perception-conditioned actions toward anatomically grounded whole-body control.
- [Persistent Robot World Models: Stabilizing Multi-Step Rollouts via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25685): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [ETA-VLA: Efficient Token Adaptation via Temporal Fusion and Intra-LLM Sparsification for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25766): Contributes a model, training, inference, or data-generation approach for embodied agents and vision-language-action systems.
- [Policy-Guided World Model Planning for Language-Conditioned Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25981): Combines policy guidance with world-model planning for language-conditioned visual navigation.
- [DiffusionAnything: End-to-End In-context Diffusion Learning for Unified Navigation and Pre-Grasp Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26322): Unifies navigation and pre-grasp motion in an RGB diffusion policy with multi-scale FiLM conditioning, trajectory-aligned depth prediction, and self-supervised attention.
- [Heracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27756): Combines precise motion tracking with generative recovery behavior for more general and perturbation-tolerant humanoid control.
- [Feel Robot Feels: Tactile Feedback Array Glove for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28542): Introduces a tactile feedback array glove for dexterous manipulation, closing the sensing loop between robot contact and human teleoperation.
- [HCLSM: Hierarchical Causal Latent State Machines for Object-Centric World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29090): Combines slot-attention object decomposition, multi-level temporal dynamics, and graph-based causal interaction learning for robot world modeling.
- [RAAP: Retrieval-Augmented Affordance Prediction with Cross-Image Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29419): Predicts affordances with retrieval-augmented cross-image action alignment.
- [DIAL: Decoupling Intent and Action via Latent World Modeling for End-to-End VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29844): Inserts a differentiable latent intent bottleneck, letting a VLM-based System-2 synthesize latent visual foresight before low-level action decoding.
- [HapCompass: A Rotational Haptic Device for Contact-Rich Robotic Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30042): Provides a rotational haptic interface for contact-rich teleoperation, giving operators force-direction feedback during manipulation.
- [DreamControl-v2: Simpler and Scalable Autonomous Humanoid Skills via Trainable Guided Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00202): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [Learning Humanoid Navigation from Human Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00416): Learns humanoid navigation policies from human data, transferring human locomotion and route behavior into embodied control.
- [Functional Force-Aware Retargeting from Virtual Human Demos to Soft Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01224): Retargets virtual human demonstrations into soft-robot policies with functional force awareness for contact-sensitive manipulation.
- [A3R: Agentic Affordance Reasoning via Cross-Dimensional Evidence in 3D Gaussian Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01882): Trains an MLLM policy for sequential affordance evidence acquisition in 3D Gaussian scenes. Core idea: gather complementary geometric and semantic views before committing to fine-grained action-affordance regions.
- [Tune to Learn: How Controller Gains Shape Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02523): Studies how low-level controller gains affect downstream robot policy learning, exposing a tuning axis between control design and learned behavior.
- [OMNI-PoseX: A Fast Vision Model for 6D Object Pose Estimation in Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02759): Provides a fast 6D pose-estimation vision model for embodied tasks where object pose must feed manipulation or navigation policies.
- [Learning Structured Robot Policies from Vision-Language Models via Synthetic Neuro-Symbolic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02812): Trains VLMs to synthesize executable behavior-tree robot policies from visual observations, instructions, and synthetic neuro-symbolic supervision.
- [Multi-View Video Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03181): Builds a 3D spatio-temporal-aware video action model, linking multi-view video generation with action-conditioned embodied policy learning.
- [CoME-VL: Scaling Complementary Multi-Encoder Vision-Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03231): Scales complementary multi-encoder vision-language learning, offering a perception backbone that can feed VLA grounding and action selection.
- [Learning Additively Compositional Latent Actions for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03340): Enforces identity, inverse, and cycle-consistency constraints so latent actions learned from video transitions have additive compositional motion structure.
- [Drift-Based Policy Optimization: Native One-Step Policy Learning for Online Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03540): Trains native one-step generative robot policies with fixed-point drifting objectives, shifting iterative refinement from inference into policy training.
- [CRAFT: Video Diffusion for Bimanual Robot Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03552): Uses video diffusion to generate bimanual robot data, extending synthetic-data routes for embodied policy learning.
- [A Multi-View 3D Telepresence System for XR Robot Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03730): Builds an XR teleoperation system with multi-view 3D scene feedback, supporting richer human demonstrations for robot control.
- [Build on Priors: Vision--Language--Guided Neuro-Symbolic Imitation Learning for Data-Efficient Real-World Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03759): Uses VLM skill classification and ASP-based PDDL synthesis to build symbolic planning domains and data-efficient controllers from a few unannotated demonstrations.
- [Veo-Act: How Far Can Frontier Video Models Advance Generalizable Robot Manipulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04502): Tests whether Veo-3 future-frame rollouts plus an inverse dynamics model trained on random-play data can produce executable dexterous robot actions.
- [E-VLA: Event-Augmented Vision-Language-Action Model for Dark and Blurred Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04834): Augments VLA models with event signals for dark and blurred scenes.
- [Synergizing Efficiency and Reliability for Continuous Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05430): Targets continuous mobile manipulation with a policy design that balances execution efficiency and reliability across navigation and manipulation phases.
- [CrowdVLA: Embodied Vision-Language-Action Agents for Context-Aware Crowd Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05525): Models pedestrians as VLA agents that read visual scene semantics and language goals, then choose crowd-navigation actions through consequence-aware reasoning.
- [Grounding Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action Models Through Explicit Language-Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05614): Grounds hierarchical VLA models through explicit language-action alignment.
- [FunRec](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05621): Reconstructs functional 3D scenes from egocentric interaction videos, linking scene reconstruction with action and affordance information for embodied agents.
- [SnapFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05656): Compresses multi-step flow-matching VLA denoising into one-step action generation through progressive self-distillation.
- [Train-Small Deploy-Large](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06598): Uses diffusion-based multi-robot planning to transfer from small training settings to larger deployment teams.
- [Sustainable Transfer Learning for Adaptive Robot Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06943): Studies zero-shot and fine-tuned RL policy transfer across robot platforms for peg-in-hole skills, emphasizing sample-efficient adaptation over retraining from scratch.
- [PhyEdit: Towards Real-World Object Manipulation via Physically-Grounded Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07230): Uses physically grounded image editing to synthesize object-manipulation states, turning visual generation into supervision for real-world manipulation.
- [RoSHI: A Versatile Robot-oriented Suit for Human Data In-the-Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07331): Collects robot-oriented in-the-wild human data with a wearable suit, expanding reusable demonstration sources for humanoid learning.
- [Event-Centric World Modeling with Memory-Augmented Retrieval for Embodied Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07392): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [HY-Embodied-0.5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07430): Builds embodied foundation models with a mixture-of-transformers design, latent tokens, self-evolving post-training, and on-policy distillation.
- [LAMP: Lift Image-Editing as General 3D Priors for Open-world Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08475): Lifts image-editing spatial cues into continuous 3D inter-object transformations that guide fine-grained open-world manipulation.
- [TAIHRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08921): Localizes task-aware 3D human keypoints for close-range human-robot interaction, linking human spatial perception with embodied interaction.
- [TouchAnything: Diffusion-Guided 3D Reconstruction from Sparse Robot Touches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08945): Reconstructs 3D object shape from sparse robot touches with diffusion guidance, strengthening tactile perception for manipulation.
- [ProGAL-VLA: Grounded Alignment through Prospective Reasoning in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09824): Aligns VLA models through grounded prospective reasoning.
- [OmniUMI: Towards Physically Grounded Robot Learning via Human-Aligned Multimodal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10647): Extends UMI-style demonstrations with synchronized RGB, depth, trajectory, tactile, grasp-force, and wrench signals under a shared human-aligned embodiment.
- [LIDEA: Human-to-Robot Imitation Learning via Implicit Feature Distillation and Explicit Geometry Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10677): Aligns implicit features and explicit geometry for human-to-robot imitation learning.
- [WARPED: Wrist-Aligned Rendering for Robot Policy Learning from Egocentric Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10809): Synthesizes wrist-view robot observations from monocular egocentric human videos by reconstructing scenes, tracking hand-object interactions, and retargeting trajectories.
- [Ro-SLM: Onboard Small Language Models for Robot Task Planning and Operation Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10929): Uses onboard small language models for robot task planning and operation-code generation, emphasizing deployable embodied reasoning under robot-side compute constraints.
- [Fast-SegSim: Real-Time Open-Vocabulary Segmentation for Robotics in Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10951): Provides a real-time open-vocabulary segmentation and panoptic-reconstruction route for robotics simulation, targeting perception loops fast enough for control.
- [Seeing Through Touch: Tactile-Driven Visual Localization of Material Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11579): Uses tactile observations to localize material regions visually, linking contact sensing with spatial perception for manipulation.
- [Human-Inspired Context-Selective Multimodal Memory for Social Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12081): Designs context-selective multimodal memory for social robots so interaction history can guide situated responses.
- [Designing for Error Recovery in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12473): Studies error-recovery design in human-robot interaction, surfacing recovery behaviors and feedback loops rather than a standalone policy architecture.
- [Tree Learning: A Multi-Skill Continual Learning Framework for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12909): Organizes multi-skill continual learning for humanoid robots, targeting reusable acquisition and retention of whole-body capabilities.
- [Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14834): Learns hierarchical skill switching for humanoid robots, using a skill graph and transition policy to move safely between agile behaviors.
- [A Hierarchical Spatiotemporal Action Tokenizer for In-Context Imitation Learning in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15215): Quantizes robot actions in two hierarchical levels and reconstructs actions with timestamps to support in-context imitation learning.
- [Cross-Geometry Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15455): Transfers learned robotic policies across object geometry through part decomposition; classify as embodied-model work, not reusable agent skills.
- [Continual Hand-Eye Calibration for Open-world Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15814): Continually updates hand-eye calibration during open-world manipulation, keeping perception-action geometry aligned as conditions change.
- [DexWorldModel: Causal Latent World Modeling towards Automated Learning of Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16484): Builds causal latent world models for automated learning of embodied tasks.
- [A Rapid Deployment Pipeline for Autonomous Humanoid Grasping Based on Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17258): Combines foundation-model annotation, SAM 3D reconstruction, FoundationPose tracking, and IK planning to onboard new humanoid grasping objects in about 30 minutes.
- [ST-$\pi$: Structured SpatioTemporal VLA for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17880): Introduces a structured spatiotemporal VLA policy for robotic manipulation, making temporal action structure explicit in the model.
- [Safety-Critical Contextual Control via Online Riemannian Optimization with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19639): Combines online Riemannian optimization with world models for safety-critical contextual control in embodied systems.
- [SpanVLA: Efficient Action Bridging and Learning from Negative-Recovery Samples for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19710): Improves VLA action learning with action bridging and negative-recovery samples, using failure recovery as training signal.
- [VLA Foundry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19728): Provides a unified framework for training VLA models, covering model, data, training, and evaluation components.
- [ETac: A Lightweight and Efficient Tactile Simulation Framework for Learning Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20295): Provides a lightweight tactile simulation framework for dexterous manipulation, enabling scalable synthetic contact data for policy learning.
- [MOMO: A framework for seamless physical, verbal, and graphical robot skill learning and adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20468): Supports robot skill learning and adaptation across physical, verbal, and graphical channels.
