- [DeVI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20841): Uses text-conditioned synthetic videos as imitation signals for physics-based dexterous human-object control.
- [Hi-WM: Human-in-the-World-Model for Scalable Robot Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21741): Uses human-in-the-world-model training for scalable robot post-training.
- [VistaBot: View-Robust Robot Manipulation via Spatiotemporal-Aware View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21914): Uses 4D geometry estimation, view-synthesis latents, and latent action learning to make ACT and diffusion policies robust to camera viewpoint shifts.
- [BridgeACT: Bridging Human Demonstrations to Robot Actions via Unified Tool-Target Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23249): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [Modular Sensory Stream for Integrating Physical Feedback in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23272): Adds decoupled physical-sensory streams with cross-modal self-attention and future-signal prediction so pretrained VLAs can use heterogeneous feedback.
- [Lifting Embodied World Models for Planning and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26182): Composes a lightweight high-level-to-low-level action policy with a frozen world model so planning can search over interpretable 2D waypoint actions instead of high-dimensional joints.
- [ATLAS: An Annotation Tool for Long-horizon Robotic Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26637): Supplies annotation tooling for long-horizon robotic action segmentation, improving supervision for policies that need subtask boundaries.
- [Learning Tactile-Aware Quadrupedal Loco-Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27224): Trains quadrupedal loco-manipulation policies that condition on tactile feedback during contact-rich movement and interaction.
- [DOT-Sim: Differentiable Optical Tactile Simulation with Precise Real-to-Sim Physical Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27367): Calibrates differentiable optical tactile simulation from real sensors, enabling sim-to-real tactile policy training.
- [MotuBrain: An Advanced World Action Model for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27792): Unifies video and action modeling with a UniDiffuser-style three-stream Mixture-of-Transformers architecture for policy learning, world modeling, inverse dynamics, and robot control.
- [FlexiTac: A Low-Cost, Open-Source, Scalable Tactile Sensing Solution for Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28156): Provides a low-cost open tactile sensor platform for collecting contact feedback in robotic perception and manipulation loops.
- [LaST-R1: Reinforcing Robotic Manipulation via Adaptive Physical Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28192): Presents LaST-R1 and Latent-to-Action Policy Optimization, an RL post-training framework that optimizes latent reasoning-before-acting policies together with physical robot actions.
- [MSACT: Multistage Spatial Alignment for Stable Low-Latency Fine Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00475): Uses multistage spatial alignment to stabilize low-latency fine manipulation where small perception-action errors quickly compound.
- [Hydra-DP3: Frequency-Aware Right-Sizing of 3D Diffusion Policies for Visuomotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01581): Introduces frequency-aware right-sizing for 3D diffusion policies used in visuomotor control.
- [Embody4D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01799): Builds a video-to-video world model for embodied 4D data generation, converting monocular robot videos into novel-view manipulation videos with synthetic compositional training, confidence-aware expert routing, and interaction-aware attention.
- [Latent Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02739): Predicts feature deltas for efficient dual-system VLA inference, reducing expensive backbone calls while preserving action quality.
- [BifrostUMI: Bridging Robot-Free Demonstrations and Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03452): Bridges robot-free demonstrations and humanoid whole-body manipulation, expanding how humanoid policies can be trained from human demonstrations.
- [When Life Gives You BC, Make Q-functions: Extracting Q-values from Behavior Cloning for On-Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05172): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [Creative Robot Tool Use by Counterfactual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05411): Uses causal discovery, VLM-suggested features, and counterfactual tool generation to support creative robot tool use beyond nominal object functions.
- [DexSynRefine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05925): synthesizes and refines human-object interaction motion for physically feasible dexterous robot actions.
- [Hitting Time Isomorphism for Multi-Stage Planning with Foundation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06470): Learns an offline-RL latent displacement geometry from hitting-time observations and derives a goal-agnostic foundation policy for multi-stage planning.
- [AT-VLA: Adaptive Tactile Injection for Enhanced Feedback Reaction in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07308): Dynamically decides when and where tactile signals should enter a pretrained VLA, reducing modality interference while improving contact-rich feedback reaction.
- [NoiseGate: Learning Per-Latent Timestep Schedules as Information Gating in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07794): Learns per-latent denoising timestep schedules as information gates in world-action models that jointly generate future video and robot actions.
- [MoMo: Conditioned Contrastive Representation Learning for Preference-Modulated Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08512): Learns conditioned contrastive representations so planning can adapt to user preferences rather than optimizing a fixed objective.
- [Beyond Thinking: Imagining in 360$^\circ$ for Humanoid Visual Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09146): Uses 360-degree imagination to support humanoid visual search in embodied settings.
- [Emergent Communication for Co-constructed Emotion Between Embodied Agents via Collective Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09522): Adds an embodied-agent model for emergent emotional communication.
- [ORICF -- Open Robotics Inference and Control Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09656): Defines an open framework for robotics inference and control, emphasizing deployable runtime structure around embodied models.
- [Forecast-aware Gaussian Splatting for Predictive 3D Representation in Language-Guided Pick-and-Place Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11144): Adds forecast-aware Gaussian splatting for predictive 3D scene representation in language-guided pick-and-place tasks.
- [3D-Belief: Embodied Belief Inference via Generative 3D World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11367): Uses generative 3D world modeling for embodied belief inference, linking spatial scene hypotheses with action-relevant world-state reasoning.
- [Dynamic Full-body Motion Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11369): Blends pretrained modular controllers for full-body motion with object interaction, fitting embodied action-generation models.
- [Behavioral Mode Discovery for Fine-tuning Multimodal Generative Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11387): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [Grounding by Remembering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11616): Adds cross-scene and in-scene memory for 3D functional affordance grounding, strengthening embodied reasoning about object uses across environments.
- [EvoNav: Evolutionary Reward Function Design for Robot Navigation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11859): Uses large language models for evolutionary reward-function design in robot navigation learning.
- [Think Twice, Act Once](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12620): Uses a trained verifier to select among sampled embodied actions at test time without changing the base policy.
- [MUJICA: Multi-skill Unified Joint Integration of Control Architecture for Wheeled-Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13058): Unifies multiple locomotion and control skills in a joint architecture for wheeled-legged robots.
- [What to Ignore, What to React: Visually Robust RL Fine-Tuning of VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13105): Fine-tunes VLAs with PPO plus paired-action invariance and sensitivity objectives so policies ignore irrelevant visual shifts but react to task-changing ones.
- [Beyond VMAF: Towards Application-Specific Metrics for Teleoperation Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13525): Proposes teleoperation-specific video quality metrics, linking perception latency and visual fidelity to remote robot control performance.
- [CreFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14274): Uses corrective reflow for sparse-reward embodied video diffusion reinforcement learning.
- [Let Robots Feel Your Touch: Visuo-Tactile Cortical Alignment for Embodied Mirror Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14571): Aligns visual and tactile representations with cortical signals to support embodied mirror-resonance style robot touch understanding.
- [Hand-in-the-Loop: Improving Dexterous VLA via Seamless Interventional Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15157): Blends human corrective intent with autonomous dexterous VLA execution to avoid abrupt hand-configuration jumps during interactive imitation learning.
- [EgoExo-WM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15477): Uses exocentric video to improve egocentric world models, connecting embodied perception with predictive world modeling.
- [SkiP: When to Skip and When to Refine for Efficient Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15536): Learns when a manipulation policy can skip computation and when it must refine actions, improving efficiency without discarding precision.
- [UAM: A Dual-Stream Perspective on Forgetting in VLA Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15735): Diagnoses forgetting during VLA fine-tuning as an embodiment tax and proposes a dual-stream Unified Action Model with a separate dorsal expert for control-relevant visual features.
- [OHP-RL: Online Human Preference as Guidance in Reinforcement Learning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15971): Uses online human preference feedback as reinforcement-learning guidance for robot manipulation policies.
- [MAgSeg: Segmentation of Agricultural Landscapes in High-Resolution Satellite Imagery using Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16179): Appears off-axis for embodied VLA; it applies multimodal LLMs to high-resolution satellite landscape segmentation rather than robot action learning.
- [Offline Semantic Guidance for VLA Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16241): uses offline semantic guidance to distill efficient vision-language-action policies.
- [ResDreamer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17537): Trains a hierarchical visual world model for open-ended 3D reinforcement-learning environments. Core idea: higher layers reconstruct residuals from lower layers, yielding task-relevant abstract dynamics without domain-specific knowledge while keeping cross-layer communication linear.
- [AtlasVA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17933): Introduces a self-evolving visual skill memory made of heatmaps, exemplars, and text skills, then uses trajectory-statistics evolution and reinforcement-learning shaping rewards on Sokoban, navigation, and robot-manipulation tasks.
- [Rethinking Muon Beyond Pretraining: Spectral Failures and High-Pass Remedies for VLA and RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19282): Proposes Pion, a drop-in Muon replacement whose high-pass Newton-Schulz update preserves dominant singular directions while suppressing noisy tail components for VLA training and RLVR.
- [PointACT: Vision-Language-Action Models with Multi-Scale Point-Action Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21414): Introduces PointACT, a 3D-aware VLA policy that brings hierarchical point-cloud representations into action decoding through multi-scale point-action interaction for precise manipulation.
- [Qwen-VLA: Unifying Vision-Language-Action Modeling across Tasks, Environments, and Robot Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30280): Extends Qwen's vision-language stack into Qwen-VLA with a DiT-based continuous action decoder and joint pretraining across heterogeneous tasks, environments, and robot embodiments.
- [MemoryVLA++: Temporal Modeling via Memory and Imagination in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09827): Adds MemoryVLA++, a temporal VLA framework that combines working memory, episodic memory, and imagined future-state evolution for long-horizon robotic manipulation.
- [APT: Action Expert Pretraining Improves Instruction Generalization of Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12366): Proposes APT, a two-stage VLA training method that pretrains a language-agnostic vision-action prior and then injects language through gated fusion to improve out-of-distribution instruction generalization.
- [$μ$VLA: On Recurrent Memory for Partially Observable Manipulation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12497): Studies minimal recurrent memory in VLA models by carrying learnable memory tokens across timesteps in an OpenVLA-OFT backbone, isolating when recurrence is enough for partially observable manipulation.
- [Collaborative Map-Based and Route-Based Policy Learning for Continuous Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2026.3662659): Combines map-based spatial reasoning with route-based procedural alignment so continuous VLN agents can explore unseen 3D environments while tracking language instructions.
- [Coupled-Layer Diffusion for Kinodynamic Trajectory Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2026.3682615): Uses coupled-layer diffusion to generate kinodynamically feasible robot trajectories.
- [FRMD: fast robot motion diffusion via trajectory-level consistency distillation](https://doi.org/10.3389/frobt.2026.1751688): Accelerates robot motion diffusion through trajectory-level consistency distillation.
- [NPPC: Neural Parametric Planning Cost for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2026.3663819): Learns a neural parametric planning cost for end-to-end autonomous driving.
- [Neuro-Symbolic Reachability Reasoning](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11462710): Adds reachability reasoning to physically grounded embodied question answering, linking symbolic feasibility with embodied perception.
- [Part-Centric Diffusion Policy with Vision Language Model for Generalizable Articulated Object Manipulation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11463603): Combines a part-centric diffusion policy with a vision-language model for generalizable articulated-object manipulation.
- [SceneReasoner](https://doi.org/10.1109/TCSVT.2026.3651752): Improves embodied scene understanding from limited perception with explicit functional associations.
- [Robot Termination Pose Generation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2026.131929): combines active object detection with task-oriented logical reasoning for manipulation termination-pose generation.
- [Industrial-grade trustworthy embodied system for power battery disassembly based on the disassembly NeuroSymbolic World Model](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jmsy.2026.03.008): Builds an industrial embodied system for power-battery disassembly around a NeuroSymbolic World Model, connecting world modeling with physical disassembly planning.
- [ZeroBot: Learning From Scratch in Minutes With Generative Real2Sim](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2026.3662595): Uses generative real-to-sim construction so a robot policy can be learned from scratch within minutes rather than requiring a prebuilt simulator.
- [Robust robotic assembly via hierarchical diffusion policy-guided reinforcement learning](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.aei.2026.104399): Trains a hierarchical diffusion policy and then refines assembly behavior with reinforcement learning, targeting robust industrial assembly under contact and task constraints.
- [Affordance-Guided Robotic Grasping via Multimodal Large Language Model Reasoning](https://doi.org/10.1109/tase.2026.3651854): Uses multimodal large-language-model reasoning to infer grasp affordances, turning visual-language object understanding into grasp selection for robotic manipulation.
- [MoReL: A Generalizable Framework for Dexterous Hand Retargeting via Modular Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2026.3653325): Retargets dexterous-hand motions with modular residual reinforcement learning, adding residual controllers that adapt demonstrations across hand embodiments.
- [Physics-informed embodied intelligence in the foundation model era: Advancing robot manipulation for smart manufacturing](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.aei.2026.104370): Reviews physics-informed foundation-model routes for robot manipulation in smart manufacturing, emphasizing physical constraints, action feedback, and deployment reliability.
- [ObjectDiff: An object-centric diffusion policy with modality-specific conditioning for robot manipulation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.knosys.2026.116124): Builds an object-centric diffusion policy whose conditioning is separated by modality, so manipulation actions can attend to task-relevant object state rather than only global observations.
- [ACF-MATD3 Formation Control for Multi-Agent Wheeled Mobile Robot Systems](https://doi.org/10.1109/icaace69793.2026.11508876): Applies an ACF-enhanced MATD3 reinforcement-learning controller to formation control for wheeled multi-robot systems, making it a control-policy item rather than VLA-specific work.
- [Action timing and classification for human-robot collaborative processes](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2026.132532): Models when collaborative human-robot actions occur and how they should be classified, supplying temporal action-recognition signals for safer shared work cells.
- [SPENav: Dynamic Object Filtering With Spatial Perception Enhancement for Vision-Language Navigation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2026.3651320): Improves vision-language navigation by filtering dynamic objects and enhancing spatial perception before route reasoning, reducing distractors in embodied navigation scenes.
- [Dynamic reconfiguration in multi-robot agent systems using embedded language models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.rcim.2026.103308): Uses embedded language models to reconfigure multi-robot roles and task allocation at run time, an orchestration-adjacent robot-agent method rather than a learned VLA policy.
- [Towards collaborative intelligence: Reimagining human-agent co-design in traditional craft design through multi-agent Empathic Handover](https://doi.org/10.1080/15710882.2026.2647077): Off-axis for robot VLA; frames multi-agent empathic handover for craft co-design, so it is retained as adjacent human-agent collaboration rather than embodied control.
- [Uncertainty-Guided and Reliable Collaborative Ground Robot Perception for Open Heterogeneous Systems](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patrec.2026.04.011): Fuses heterogeneous ground-robot perception with uncertainty guidance, focusing on reliable multi-robot sensing rather than language-conditioned action.
- [Embodied multi-modal data fusion via geometry anchoring for continuous perception in ground robots](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patrec.2026.02.025): Anchors multimodal robot sensor fusion in geometry to maintain continuous ground-robot perception across changing scenes.
- [A multi-modal image fusion network with learnable color restoration and semantic guidance: Towards real-time robot perception and scene parsing](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patrec.2026.02.026): Learns color restoration and semantic guidance for multimodal image fusion, providing real-time scene-parsing inputs for robot perception.
- [Deep Neural Network with A* Based on Floyd Search for Quadrupedal Robot Navigation](https://doi.org/10.1109/iceaai68945.2026.11442555): Combines a deep neural navigation model with A* and Floyd search, giving quadruped robots a hybrid learned-and-graph-search path planner.
- [Emotion Analysis in Human–Robot Communication With ELMO Robot Using Deep Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2026.3678983): Trains deep emotion analysis for communication with the ELMO robot, adjacent to social embodied perception but not a VLA control policy.
- [DiffusionHandover: Reliable Human-to-Robot Handover Generation With Anthropomorphic Hand](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2026.3674002): Uses diffusion generation for anthropomorphic handover motions, modeling reliable human-to-robot transfer trajectories under hand-shape constraints.
- [Real-Time Dual-Arm Cooperative Manipulation Under Multiple Constraints: A Two-Stage Sampling MPC Approach](https://doi.org/10.1109/tro.2026.3686224): Plans dual-arm cooperative manipulation with two-stage sampling model-predictive control, handling real-time constraints before executing coordinated robot actions.
- [Golden-Time Policy-Grounded Natural-Language Control for Public Safety Cyber-Physical Systems](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2026.3687364): Grounds natural-language commands in public-safety policies for cyber-physical control, adjacent to embodied command following but outside robot VLA manipulation.
- [Hand-Gesture Based Robot Tracking and Teaching Framework: Demonstration in Surface Polishing Task](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2026.3666366): Uses hand gestures as a tracking and teaching interface for robot learning from demonstration, with surface polishing as the real-task validation.
- [Enhancing stability and reliability in LLM-driven robotic manipulation through human skill demonstration and visual tracking](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.birob.2026.100305): Stabilizes LLM-driven manipulation by adding human skill demonstrations and visual tracking feedback, tying language-level plans to observed robot execution.
- [Towards Object-Level Multimodal Task Planning for Long-Term Robotic Manipulation with Vision Language Model and Behavior Tree](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11461939): Combines a vision-language model with behavior trees for object-level long-horizon manipulation planning, making the symbolic plan executable over time.
- [Neural–symbolic reasoning for end-of-life product identification in robot-assisted disassembly](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jmsy.2026.02.009): Applies neural-symbolic reasoning to identify end-of-life products during robot-assisted disassembly, linking perception with disassembly decisions.
- [Framework for Robot Door Opening Based on Visual, Force, and Tactile Integration](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2026.3655617): Integrates visual, force, and tactile signals for door opening, giving the robot multimodal feedback for contact-rich manipulation.
- [DCR-BC: Target Conditional Embodied Imitation Learning Based on Dynamic Consistency Regularization](https://doi.org/10.1109/etai68332.2026.11485324): Adds dynamic consistency regularization to target-conditioned behavior cloning, improving embodied imitation learning when visual targets and actions must stay aligned.
- [A Hybrid Attention and Prioritized Replay Framework for Humanoid Robot Basketball Shooting](https://doi.org/10.1109/cisce69494.2026.11504693): Trains a humanoid basketball-shooting controller with hybrid attention and prioritized replay, a narrow embodied RL/control case.
- [Embodied Intelligence-Enhanced Anti-Jamming Resource Allocation for Low-Altitude Communication Networks](https://doi.org/10.1109/twc.2026.3677272): Off-axis for robot VLA; uses embodied-intelligence framing for anti-jamming resource allocation in low-altitude communication networks.
- [A Comparative Study of DRL Algorithms for Map-Free Robot Navigation With Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2026.3684520): Compares deep-reinforcement-learning policies for map-free navigation and zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, providing navigation-policy evidence rather than a new VLA architecture.
- [Large language model assisted human-AI collaborative development of analytical inverse kinematics solvers for robot manipulators](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.mechmachtheory.2026.106392): Uses an LLM in a human-AI workflow to derive analytical inverse-kinematics solvers for manipulators, adjacent to robot design and planning rather than policy learning.
- [Towards a theory of human creativity sustained by embodied collective intelligence](https://doi.org/10.3389/fpsyg.2026.1752280): Off-axis for this section; reviews embodied collective intelligence in human creativity, with no robot policy, simulator, or VLA training contribution.
- [Learning Robot Visual Navigation in Crowds via Intention-Aware Scene Representations](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2026.3677748): Learns intention-aware scene representations for visual navigation in crowds, helping robots predict human motion and choose socially feasible paths.
- [Simultaneous Optimization of Hand-Eye and Robot-World Parameters Exploiting Accuracy Discrepancies in Robotic and Vision Sensor](https://doi.org/10.1109/tase.2026.3673150): Optimizes hand-eye and robot-world calibration together by exploiting accuracy discrepancies between robot and vision sensors, improving manipulation perception geometry.
- [Real-Time Vision-Based Human—Robot Interaction Framework for Low-Cost Embedded Robotic Arms](https://doi.org/10.1109/isdfs69419.2026.11458919): Implements real-time vision-based interaction for low-cost embedded robot arms, an applied perception-control framework for accessible manipulation.
- [Coordinated World Model Learning for Deep Space Robot Teams](https://doi.org/10.1109/aero66936.2026.11519895): Coordinates world-model learning across deep-space robot teams, focusing on shared predictive environment models for multi-robot autonomy.
- [LLM-Diffu: Robot Dexterous Grasp Generation Network With Diffusion Model and LLM](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2026.3685930): Couples an LLM with a diffusion model to generate dexterous grasp candidates, using language-level object reasoning to condition low-level grasp synthesis.
- [SSARA: Integrating Safety and Security for Adaptive Risk Assessment of Connected and Automated Vehicles in the Operational Phase](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2026.131966): Integrates safety and security signals for adaptive operational risk assessment in connected and automated vehicles.
- [VLM-PoseManip: Dexterous robotic manipulation via Vision-Language model based instructive pose estimation for Human-Robot collaboration](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.aei.2026.104508): Evidence-needed: Crossref confirms the Advanced Engineering Informatics title but has no abstract, so the instructive pose-estimation mechanism needs publisher verification.
- [RT-K: A key point-aware transformer framework for efficient vision-language robot control](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.birob.2026.100341): Adds keypoint-aware transformer modeling for efficient vision-language robot control.
- [Understanding Uncertain Spatial Terms for Manipulating Objects on Irregular Plane Surfaces](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2026.3691261): Models uncertain spatial terms for object manipulation on irregular planes.
- [Semantic-Aware Behavior Optimization With Safety Reinforcement Feedback for Language-Conditioned Manipulation](https://doi.org/10.1109/lra.2026.3685452): Evidence-needed: Crossref confirms the RA-L title but exposes no abstract, leaving the safety-reinforcement feedback objective and optimization details unverified.
- [InCLET](https://ifaamas.csc.liv.ac.uk/Proceedings/aamas2025/pdfs/p2134.pdf): Uses LLM in-context trajectory imagination to form task representations for language-conditioned reinforcement-learning agents in embodied control environments.
- [Collision-Free Robot Path Planning by Integrating DRL with Noise Layers and MPC](https://doi.org/10.3390/s25206263): Combines deep reinforcement learning, noise layers, and model predictive control for autonomous mobile robot path planning in dynamic industrial and logistics environments.
- [DPDQN-TER: An Improved Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach for Mobile Robot Path Planning in Dynamic Scenarios](https://doi.org/10.3390/s25216741): Introduces an improved deep reinforcement-learning method for mobile-robot path planning in dynamic scenarios.
- [Natural-Language-Interfaced Robotic Synthesis for AI-Copilot-Assisted Exploration of Inorganic Materials.](https://doi.org/10.1021/jacs.5c05916): Connects natural-language user interaction to an AI-copilot robotic synthesis platform for inorganic materials, an embodied laboratory automation case rather than a general VLA policy.
- [EgoVision a YOLO-ViT hybrid for robust egocentric object recognition](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-025-18341-y): Combines YOLO-style detection with ViT representation learning for robust egocentric object recognition, adjacent to embodied perception without action learning.
- [Knowledge Enhanced Efficient Robotic Decision Making with Intuitive-Deliberate LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1007/s44230-025-00122-5): Introduces KEID for ObjectNav, using a lightweight intuition module to decide when to invoke an LLM and specialized navigation knowledge bases for deliberate reasoning.
- [Robot Autonomous Avoidance System Based on Reinforcement Learning in 6G Network Scenarios](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11277-024-11216-x): Uses reinforcement learning to train autonomous robot avoidance in 6G network scenarios.
- [The Limitations of Large Language Models for Understanding Human Language and Cognition](https://doi.org/10.1162/opmi_a_00160): Off-axis for this section; contrasts text-only LLM learning with human multimodal embodied language acquisition rather than proposing a robot model.
- [A chemical autonomous robotic platform for end-to-end synthesis of nanoparticles](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-025-62994-2): Presents an autonomous robotic chemistry platform that closes the loop from synthesis to characterization for nanoparticles, a laboratory embodiment of action-feedback automation.
- [SimPLE, a visuotactile method learned in simulation to precisely pick, localize, regrasp, and place objects](https://doi.org/10.1126/scirobotics.adi8808): Learns pick-localize-regrasp-place behavior in simulation from CAD models, combining task-aware grasping, visuotactile perception, and regrasp planning for precise kitting.
- [Trajectory Diffusion for ObjectGoal Navigation](https://doi.org/10.52202/079017-3504): Uses diffusion modeling over navigation trajectories for ObjectGoal navigation, treating route generation as a learned distribution over feasible paths.
- [Sora for foundation robots with parallel intelligence: three world models, three robotic systems](https://doi.org/10.1631/fitee.2400144): Discusses parallel-intelligence foundation robots through three world-model designs and three robotic systems, making it a conceptual world-model entry.
- [Visual-tactile pretraining and online multitask learning for humanlike manipulation dexterity](https://doi.org/10.1126/scirobotics.ady2869): Pretrains visual-tactile representations from human demonstrations, then trains a unified multitask dexterous-hand policy with reinforcement learning and online imitation learning.
- [LASeR: Towards Diversified and Generalizable Robot Design with Large Language Models](https://openreview.net/forum?id=7mlvOHL6qJ): Uses large language models to diversify robot design proposals and improve generalization across design requirements, fitting robot-design generation rather than policy control.
- [Multi-Humanoid Robot Arm Motion Imitation and Collaboration Based on Improved Retargeting](https://doi.org/10.3390/biomimetics10030190): Improves humanoid arm retargeting by balancing human-like joint configurations with accurate end-effector tracking, enabling multi-humanoid imitation and collaboration.
- [LLM-Powered Embodied Intelligence for Socially-Aware Robot Navigation in Human-Robot Interaction](https://doi.org/10.1145/3748777.3748807): Uses an LLM to reason about social navigation constraints in human-robot interaction, converting human-aware context into navigation decisions.
- [Neural Network Architecture of Embodied Intelligence](https://doi.org/10.3103/s0005105525700311): Surveys or proposes neural-network architectural framing for embodied intelligence; evidence is metadata-only, so it remains a low-detail architecture reference.
- [Can AIGC Aid Intelligent Robot Design? A Tentative Research of Apple-Harvesting Robot](https://doi.org/10.3390/pr13082422): Uses multimodal AIGC and human checks to assist apple-harvesting robot design, including conceptual design and generated apple images for perception-system training.
- [Uncertainty-Aware Stochastic Hybrid World Models with Neural Map Memory for Autonomous Navigation in Partially Observable Grid Environments](https://doi.org/10.66279/t95nh857): Proposes SHWM-NM, combining stochastic latent dynamics, neural map memory, and multi-level uncertainty estimates for navigation under partial observability.
- [RoboTheater: A Multi-Robot Storytelling Platform from LLM Scripts to Stage Performance](https://doi.org/10.1145/3731459.3779333): Builds a multi-robot performance pipeline from LLM-generated scripts to stage actions, closer to embodied orchestration than model training.
- [Unbiased Embodied Visual Representation Learning with Causal Inference and Cross-Modality Alignment](https://doi.org/10.1145/3760261): Proposes UEVR for ObjectNav, using causal inference and cross-modality alignment to reduce spurious scene-representation and perspective biases.
- [Leveraging large language models for efficient scheduling in Human–Robot collaborative flexible manufacturing systems](https://doi.org/10.1038/s44334-025-00061-w): Applies LLM reasoning to scheduling in flexible human-robot manufacturing systems, adjacent to robot task allocation rather than low-level VLA control.
- [Cross-robot behavior adaptation through intention alignment.](https://doi.org/10.1126/scirobotics.adv2250): Introduces Intention-Aligned Imitation Learning, adapting demonstrations across heterogeneous robots by aligning high-level natural-language intentions instead of copying motor trajectories.
- [Adaptive Action Chunking for Robotic Imitation Learning](https://doi.org/10.3390/biomimetics11050316): Predicts action chunk length from real-time visual context with a dual-branch network, allowing imitation policies to switch between efficient long chunks and precise short adjustments.
- [Robot Behavior Generation for Social Human-Robot Interaction](https://doi.org/10.1007/s12369-025-01333-3): Focuses on generating robot behaviors for social HRI, an embodied interaction item whose public metadata does not expose a concrete policy-training recipe.
- [Seamless Indoor and Outdoor Navigation Using IMU-GNSS Sensor Data Fusion](https://doi.org/10.3390/s26072215): Combines a physics-informed neural network with an extended Kalman filter for GNSS-IMU localization, mainly a wearable navigation-sensor fusion method.
- [KAHLT: Kolmogorov-Arnold Hybrid Latent Transformer - A Hybrid Transformer For Classification Tasks](https://doi.org/10.5220/0014488700004052): Off-axis for robot VLA; introduces a Kolmogorov-Arnold hybrid latent transformer for generic classification tasks, with no embodied action mechanism evident from metadata.
- [KA-AttLSTMnet: a Kolmogorov-Arnold attentional architecture for egocentric navigation prediction from hippocampal CA1 spikes](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10489-025-07070-4): Models egocentric navigation prediction from hippocampal CA1 spikes with a Kolmogorov-Arnold attentional LSTM architecture, adjacent to embodied neuroscience rather than robot policy learning.
- [Multi-factor optimization of epoxy based Kevlar-coir hybrid composites with alumina using deep graph learning](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00289-026-06357-x): Off-axis for this section; uses deep graph learning for materials-composite optimization and does not provide embodied, robot-action, or VLA machinery.
- [Categorical Codebook Matching for Embodied Character Controllers](https://doi.org/10.1145/3658209): Generates full-body avatar motion from sparse sensor signals by matching input and output categorical codebooks, a virtual embodied-controller method.
- [Real-time deep learning-based model predictive control of a 3-DOF biped robot leg](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-024-66104-y): Learns a dynamics model from joint-angle and actuator-torque data and embeds it in MPC for real-time trajectory tracking of a biped robot leg.
- [Adversarial Robust Safeguard for Evading Deep Facial Manipulation](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v38i1.27762): Off-axis for embodied VLA; trains a one-pass adversarial-noise generator to protect faces from manipulation, belonging to media safety rather than robot action.
- [Mr.LfD: A Mixed Reality Interface for Robot Learning from Demonstration](https://doi.org/10.1145/3726986.3727004): Provides a mixed-reality interface for learning from demonstration, letting users teach robot skills through embodied spatial interaction.
- [Cognizing and Imitating Robotic Skills via a Dual Cognition-Action Architecture](https://www.ifaamas.org/Proceedings/aamas2024/pdfs/p2204.pdf): Uses a dual cognition-action architecture for robotic skill imitation, separating skill understanding from action execution in the AAMAS system description.
- [Bio-Signal-Guided Robot Adaptive Stiffness Learning via Human-Teleoperated Demonstrations](https://doi.org/10.3390/biomimetics10060399): Maps human sEMG and teleoperation demonstrations to robot end-effector stiffness with Gaussian mixture modeling and regression for adaptive contact skills.
- [Human-Interactive Robot Learning: Definition, Challenges, and Recommendations](https://doi.org/10.1145/3779297): Defines Human-Interactive Robot Learning as robots and humans interacting during learning, contrasting it with offline demonstration-only paradigms and identifying model-design challenges.
- [Embodied Intelligence in Wearable Robotics](https://doi.org/10.1088/1757-899x/1343/1/012014): Frames wearable robotics around morphology-aware generative design, reinforcement-learning controllers, simulator-informed sim-to-real transfer, and human-centered soft-rigid interfaces.
- [Motion Planning Diffusion: Learning and Adapting Robot Motion Planning with Diffusion Models (Abstract Reprint)](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v40i47.41373): Learns trajectory-distribution priors with diffusion models so motion planners can sample or optimize better initial paths in high-dimensional scenes.
- [Real‐Time Behavior Recognition Using a Legged Robot for Animal–Robot Interaction](https://doi.org/10.1002/rob.70123): Runs YOLO-Cows, YOLO-Chicken, and distilled DARTEMIS onboard a robotic dog for real-time animal detection and behavior recognition in field trials.
- [One-shot learning-driven autonomous robotic assembly via human-robot symbiotic interaction](https://doi.org/10.1038/s44334-025-00030-3): Uses one-shot learning inside a human-robot symbiotic interaction loop so assembly skills can be taught with minimal demonstrations.
- [A Robust Pose Estimation Method for Robot Grasping in Bin‐Picking Scenarios Using Point Cloud](https://doi.org/10.1002/rob.22571): Proposes NRP-Net for bin picking, combining directional encoding with differential-feature instance segmentation to improve point-cloud pose estimation under occlusion.
- [Humanoid robotics in material and chemistry experiments](https://doi.org/10.1007/s40843-025-3384-9): Discusses humanoid robots as embodied laboratory operators for material and chemistry experiments; public metadata exposes limited method detail.
- [Lidar-inertial odometry tightly coupled SLAM research based on scene degradation detection](https://doi.org/10.1177/16878132261442321): Tightly couples LiDAR and IMU optimization, switches pose estimation via perturbation-based scene degradation detection, and validates real-time SLAM on vehicle tests.
- [Motor-Mediated Creativity: Bridging Embodied Skill Training and Digital Expression](https://doi.org/10.1145/3772318.3791935): Connects embodied skill training to digital expression, making it an HCI-adjacent embodied-learning item rather than a robot policy model.
- [Who Leads the Story? Comparing Autonomous vs. Adult-Supported Child-Robot Interactions](https://doi.org/10.1145/3757279.3785564): Compares autonomous Pepper-led and adult-supported child-robot storytelling, using LLM-enabled dialogue and visual prompts in an educational HRI setting.
- [Adaptive Variable Admittance Control for Intent-Aware Human–Robot Collaboration](https://doi.org/10.3390/machines14020221): Stress-tests HRC control with an intent-aware force generator and self-supervised variable stiffness, damping, and inertia adaptation in dual-arm transport.
- [El corpus ROBOT-TALK para el reconocimiento del origen robótico de textos en español](https://doi.org/10.21071/arf.v37i.18687): Provides a Spanish human-versus-LLM text corpus for detecting robotic text origin; this is off-axis for physical embodied VLA models.
- [Business Process Management in Optimizing Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) for Organizational Performance](https://doi.org/10.1007/s12369-026-01373-3): Treats human-robot collaboration as an organizational process-optimization problem, adjacent to embodied deployment rather than a VLA policy.
- [Innovative Soft Material‐Assisted Robot Grasping Devices: From Design Concept to Fabrication and Application Scenarios](https://doi.org/10.1002/rob.70162): Reviews soft-material gripper principles, structures, actuation, sensing, fabrication, and application scenarios, making it hardware-adjacent rather than a policy model.
- [Robust Human-to-Robot Handover System Under Adverse Lighting](https://doi.org/10.3390/biomimetics11040231): Uses a dual-path RGB-D and ToF perception stack with fusion and reliability weighting to keep handover localization robust under adverse lighting.
- [Voice, Text, or Embodied AI Avatar? Effects of Generative AI Interface Modalities in VR Museums](https://doi.org/10.3390/informatics13030042): Compares voice, text, and embodied avatar interfaces in a VR museum study, an HCI modality result adjacent to embodied AI rather than robot policy learning.
- [Emerging wurtzite ferroelectrics and their prospect in embodied intelligence neuromorphic devices](https://doi.org/10.1063/5.0300822): Surveys wurtzite ferroelectric materials for embodied-intelligence neuromorphic devices, relevant as hardware substrate rather than VLA policy machinery.
- [Mobile robot mapping and navigation system with Raspberry Pi and multiple sensors using ROS2](https://doi.org/10.1063/5.0313408): Builds a ROS2 mobile-robot mapping and navigation stack around Raspberry Pi and multiple sensors, closer to robotics integration than VLA modeling.
- [Active reward learning and iterative trajectory improvement from comparative language feedback](https://doi.org/10.1177/02783649251394742): Learns a shared trajectory-language latent space for comparative language feedback, improving robot trajectories and reward learning with active queries.
- [UAV Autonomous Navigation System Based on Air–Ground Collaboration in GPS-Denied Environments](https://doi.org/10.3390/drones9060442): Combines multi-UAV mobile-anchor localization, aerial-ground 3D environmental modeling, and improved D* Lite planning for near-ground UAV navigation without GPS.
- [Redundancy-Based Motion Planning with Task Constraints for Robot Manipulators](https://doi.org/10.3390/s25061900): Samples Cartesian task nodes and maps them onto 7-DoF joint-space constraint manifolds, exploiting redundancy for constrained manipulator motion planning.
- [Intelligent Robot Control and Uncertainty Analysis Integrating Reinforcement Learning and Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.54097/r6xtmv46): Discusses RL and LLM fusion for intelligent robot control and uncertainty management, but the available abstract is high-level and management-oriented.
- [Online decentralized planning for a cable-driven parallel robot with aerial and ground actuators through multi-agent reinforcement learning](https://doi.org/10.1177/17298806251390589): Trains a MARL planner for a cable-towed aerial platform to plan online around obstacles when aerial and ground actuators lose communication.
- [Learning robot behavior from human-human interactions](https://doi.org/10.1126/scirobotics.aee5779): Trains robot behavior models from observed human-human interactions so HRI actions appear more natural than hand-designed behavior scripts.
- [Dual Process Dreamer: Fast and Slow Decision-Making with World Models](https://doi.org/10.5220/0014243200004052): Evidence-needed: Crossref lists the ICAART 2026 title but no abstract, so the fast/slow world-model decision mechanism remains unverified.
- [EXAONE-VLA: A Unified Vision–Language Framework for Mobile Manipulation via Semantic Topology and Hierarchical LLM Reasoning](https://doi.org/10.3390/app16052600): Adds a unified vision-language-action model for mobile manipulation.
- [DiffusionVLA: Scaling Robot Foundation Models via Unified Diffusion and Autoregression](https://www.semanticscholar.org/paper/f0d17501c051e4c7220affd296a307763741bada): Couples autoregressive VLM reasoning with diffusion action policies and injects generated reasoning phrases into policy learning for scalable robot foundation models.
- [REPLAY: Robot Embodiment via Intent-aware Policy Imitation by Replicating Human Demonstrations From Video](https://doi.org/10.1007/s12555-025-0505-8): Evidence-needed: Crossref confirms the journal title but provides no abstract, so the intent-aware video-to-robot imitation mechanism needs publisher verification.
- [Generating Robotic Control Strategies With LLMs: Via Human–Robot Voice Interaction](https://doi.org/10.1002/cpe.70372): Contributes a model, training, inference, or data-generation approach for embodied agents and vision-language-action systems.
- [One model, two skills: active vision and action learning model for robotic manipulation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11432-023-4282-8): Evidence-needed: Crossref confirms the Science China Information Sciences title but exposes no abstract, so the combined active-vision and action-learning mechanism needs paper verification.
- [Agent-Based Control of Robot Behavior Using Reasoning Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1134/s0005117925601101): Contributes a model, training, inference, or data-generation approach for embodied agents and vision-language-action systems.
- [Force Sensing Control for Physical Human–Robot Interaction: A Transformer-Based Action Chunking Approach](https://doi.org/10.3390/machines14020249): Contributes a model-side method, training recipe, data engine, or alignment technique for embodied and VLA agents. Core signal: robot policy learning, VLA action generation, embodied world modeling, or embodied data generation.
- [Uncertainty-Calibrated Safety Gating for Vision–Language– Action Manipulation Under Domain Shift: Reliability Gains and Intervention–Efficiency Trade-Offs](https://doi.org/10.3390/s26103140): Wraps VLA manipulation policies with calibrated online failure-risk estimates that gate between execution, pause-and-reobserve, and fallback planning under domain shift.
- [Empowering World Models with Reflection for Embodied Video Prediction](): Adds reflection to world models for embodied video prediction.
